# ShellX A10 — Quality Pass P0 (desktop + mobile) Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Make the terminal dependable in daily work, as cmux is: find text in a long scrollback, click URLs and file paths (and OSC 8 links), drop files or paste a screenshot as a shell-escaped path, paste a large text without losing a byte, a keyboard that always goes to the right pane, no transient sizes reaching a TUI while a window or divider is dragged, a layout that survives two crashes in a row and a window that never opens off screen — and on the phone: a "View as text" sheet to copy output, bursts of output that do not stall typing, a composer that never turns `--` into `—`, a tap on a URL, and a test that typing reaches the terminal exactly once.

**Architecture:** Mostly small pure modules with a thin UI layer on top, each verified in isolation first. Desktop: `ResizeSettler` (per-pane trailing debounce of the size sent to the PTY), `PasteSender` (paced, cancellable, confirmable paste through `PanePasteSink`), `terminal_search.dart` + `FindSession`/`FindSessions`/`FindBar` (whole-buffer search over xterm's `Buffer`, highlights through `TerminalController.highlight`), `shellx_core` `terminal_links.dart` + desktop `HyperlinkRegistry` (OSC 8 via anchors), `terminal_buffer_text.dart`, `link_finder.dart`, `LinkLayer` (hover + ⌘/Ctrl-click through a `Listener`, because xterm 4.0.0 never calls `TerminalView.onTapUp`), `shell_escape.dart`, `PasteImageStore`, `ClipboardMedia` (`pasteboard`), `PaneInput` + `PaneDropTarget` (`desktop_drop`), `pickSnapshot` + layout generations in `RestoreStore`, a clamping `restorableBounds`. Mobile: `TerminalOutputFeed` (budgeted, foreground-first output), `terminalText` + `ViewAsTextSheet`, `urlAt` + `LinkSheet`, composer input flags. The phone protocol, the relay and the agent are **not** touched.

**Tech Stack:** Dart ^3.11 / Flutter 3.41.4 via fvm (`~/fvm/versions/3.41.4/bin/dart`, `PATH=~/fvm/versions/3.41.4/bin:$PATH flutter`), `flutter_test`, `package:test`, `fake_async`, flutter_riverpod 3 (desktop), signals + get_it (mobile), xterm 4.0.0 (pinned). **New dependencies, desktop only:** `desktop_drop: 0.8.4`, `pasteboard: 0.5.0` (Task 9). Nothing new on mobile.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-07-quality-p0-a10-design.md` (binding; D1–D41 are final, §4 is the copy, §5 the privacy rules, §9 the manual checklist). Research: `docs/superpowers/research/2026-10-07-cmux-gap-analysis.md` (§4 lists the cmux bug classes each test pins; §5 the original P0 list). Read alongside: A3 spec D13/D18, A5a plan Tasks 2–6, A8 spec D22 (`paneReplacements`) and plan Task 7.

## Prerequisite and how this plan was made

Start `feat/desktop-a10` from `main` (A9 merged; A5b partly merged — **no task here touches `main.dart`, `agent_side.dart`, `daemon_main.dart`, `src/mux/*`, `src/agent/*`**). Items 1–2 of the gap list (IME, bold font) are on `fix/desktop-font-ime` and are out of scope (spec D38 lists the interactions).

**Every code block below was executed** in a scratch copy of the worktree while this plan was written: all new test files green, `flutter analyze lib test` clean for both apps, the full `shellx_core` suite green (304), the full mobile suite green (727) and the full desktop suite green (2070, no skips added). Several existing desktop tests went red along the way and each fix is in the task that causes it: the reconnect keeps-the-view-mounted pair (Task 5 Step 7.2), the two layout-size tests of `terminal_tabs_test` and the five layout tests that read `resizes` (Task 2 Step 4.6), the paste tests that build their own containers (Task 9 Step 6). One more, `restore_store_test` "prepare does not wait for the backup exclusion", failed once under full-suite load and passes alone — a pre-existing 500 ms timing test (Task 11 notes it). Where a step says "the existing test X must stay green" that was observed. Three findings from that run change the obvious design and are called out where they bite:

1. `PtyHandle.write` (patched flutter_pty) queues at most 1 MiB and **drops the rest silently** — hence paced pastes (Task 3).
2. xterm 4.0.0 **never calls `TerminalView.onTapUp`** (`TerminalGestureDetector` only forwards `onSingleTapUp`, which `TerminalView` does not pass) — hence a `Listener` for link clicks on both apps (Tasks 7, 15).
3. A `GlobalKey` shared by the `TerminalView`s of different tabs carries the previous tab's state along — hence one key per pane (desktop) / per tab (`GlobalObjectKey`, mobile).

Record each answer in `docs/superpowers/ledgers/a1-desktop-terminal.md` under a new heading `## A10 (quality P0) — execution` (counts of tests before and after; every ⚠ item as "pending manual" until done):

```bash
test -f apps/desktop/lib/src/terminal/terminal_feed.dart && echo "desktop TerminalFeed present (mobile gets a copy, Task 16)"

grep -q "keybindings" apps/desktop/lib/src/ui/terminal/terminal_shortcuts.dart && echo "A9 merged"
git log --oneline -1 -- apps/desktop/lib/src/mux/daemon_host.dart   # A5b progress, informational
```

## Global Constraints

- The spec wins. User-facing strings in the vi + en ARB files of the app that shows them (desktop `app_en.arb`/`app_vi.arb` + `flutter gen-l10n`; mobile `intl_en.arb`/`intl_vi.arb` + `dart run intl_utils:generate`); "SSH", "tmux", "pane", "tab" stay English in Vietnamese copy; `⌘`/`Ctrl` come from the platform, never from a translation.
- **Phone protocol, relay, agent unchanged:** no edits to `packages/shellx_core/lib/src/protocol`, `packages/shellx_agent`, `relay/`. The only edit to `packages/shellx_core` is the new pure file `terminal_links.dart` and its export.
- **Privacy (spec §5):** never log terminal text, a search query, a URL, an OSC 8 target, a path, a file name, clipboard content, an image size or the text of "View as text" — not even under `kDebugMode`. Logs carry an event kind, an error `runtimeType`, a count. `toString()` of every new value class that can hold such data returns `'…'` (tests pin it).
- **Injection safety:** a URL reaches `open`/`rundll32` only after `webUriOf` (http/https, a host, no controls, ≤ 2048); a path reaches `open -R`/`explorer` only absolute and control-free; a file name reaches a shell only through `escapePath` (a control character refuses the whole drop). `UrlOpener.open` stays https-only; the new `openWebLink` and `reveal` are separate methods.
- **No new settings keys** (`SettingKeys`, `DaemonSettingKeys`, `UiSettingKeys` unchanged). No `layout.json` format change (generations are copies).
- Limits: find query ≤ 200 chars, ≤ 1 000 matches highlighted; link ≤ 2 048 chars, logical line ≤ 8 rows; paste direct ≤ 64 KiB, pieces of 32 KiB every 8 ms, confirm > 1 MiB, refuse > 16 MiB; pasted image ≤ 20 MiB, ≤ 50 files, ≤ 24 h; OSC 8 ≤ 2 000 ranges per pane; layout generations: `layout.json.1`, `.2`; mobile "View as text" ≤ 2 000 lines / 200 000 characters; mobile feed 128 KiB / 16 ms (front), 64 KiB / 250 ms (back), cap 2 MiB keep 1 MiB.
- Timings: PTY size settles after 80 ms (`resizeSettleProvider`; the UI test harness sets it to zero); find refresh 150 ms after output; hover path check cached 2 s.
- **Format only the files you touched** (`dart format <files>`): `dart format lib` on a whole app reformats unrelated files (it did, twice, while this plan was verified). Every task ends green (its tests + the full suite of every package it touched), `dart format --set-exit-if-changed` on its files, `flutter analyze lib test` clean, and a commit containing only the listed files — never `git add -A`.
- Every interface that gains members updates **every** implementer in the same task (`UrlOpener`: `SystemUrlOpener` + `RecordingUrlOpener`).
- Test conventions: widget tests that tap with xterm's gesture detector must `pump(const Duration(milliseconds: 400))` before they end (its double-tap timer); a mouse click in a widget test is `tapAt(p, kind: PointerDeviceKind.mouse)` (the default is a touch); real file-system calls inside `testWidgets` hang under fake async — inject `LinkResolver(typeOf: …)`; the shared UI harness is `pumpConfiguredWorkspace` (`test/support/config_workspace.dart`) and it has **no dialog host** (use `ShellxDesktopApp`, as `focus_dialogs_test.dart` does, for dialogs).

## Review Focus

1. **Hostile text reaches `open`, `explorer` or a shell** — an OSC 8 `javascript:`, a URL with a control character, a file named `$(rm -rf ~)`, `-rf`, with `\n`. Pinned by `terminal_links_test`, `link_resolver_test`, `shell_escape_test`, `url_opener_web_link_test`, `hyperlink_registry_test` (Tasks 1, 6, 7, 8).
2. **A paste still loses bytes or leaves the program in paste mode** — cancelled mid-way, the pane replaced by a reconnect, two pastes at once, text containing `ESC[201~`, the pane switched while the clipboard is read. Pinned by `paste_sender_test`, `paste_flow_test` (the race test fails if the target is read after the `await` — verified by temporarily swapping the order) (Task 3).
3. **The size settler leaves the PTY on a stale size** — a key typed right after a resize, a tab that becomes visible, a pane that ends with a timer pending, a disposed notifier. Pinned by `resize_settler_test`, the whole existing `terminal_tabs_*` suite (green with the harness override) (Task 2).
4. **The find bar keeps the keyboard, changes the grid, or leaks highlights/anchors** — pinned by `pane_find_test` ("grid unchanged", "keyboard back", "no anchors stay"), `find_session_test` ("close leaves no highlight and no anchor"), `focus_invariants_test` (Tasks 4, 5, 10). ⌘V in the bar pastes into the bar, not the terminal (`textEditingShortcuts`).
5. **A restore picks the wrong layout** — an empty layout after a *clean* quit must be respected; after a crash it must give way to the previous run; a damaged generation must be skipped. Pinned by `restore_store_rotation_test` (a pure `pickSnapshot` plus real-directory tests) (Task 11).
6. **Mobile output batching reorders, drops or delays echo** — the echo of a key, an exit banner arriving before the last output, a `session.reset` with output waiting, a surrogate pair cut between two writes. Pinned by `terminal_output_feed_test`, `terminal_tab_controller_test` (Task 16). Note the stream-zone trap in the test of the background tick.
7. **Two copies drift** — mobile `TerminalOutputFeed` vs desktop `TerminalFeed`, mobile `urlAt`/`terminalText` vs desktop `logicalLineAt`: each has its own tests; the pure link detector is shared in `shellx_core` (spec D37).
8. **Privacy** — a log-capture test per feature is part of each task's checklist (`debugPrint` captured, test strings absent).
9. **Interaction with `fix/desktop-font-ime`** (spec D38) — after that branch merges, re-run `pane_find_test`, `focus_invariants_test`, and the manual find-field Vietnamese typing check (Task 5 ⚠).

File: `packages/shellx_core/test/util/terminal_links_test.dart`

```dart
import 'package:shellx_core/src/util/terminal_links.dart';
import 'package:test/test.dart';

List<String> urls(String text) => [
  for (final l in findLinks(text)) l.target,
];

List<(String, int?, int?)> paths(String text) => [
  for (final l in findLinks(text, paths: true))
    if (l.kind == TerminalLinkKind.path) (l.target, l.line, l.column),
];

void main() {
  group('URLs (A10 D6)', () {
    test('plain, with query, sentence punctuation is not part of it', () {
      expect(urls('see https://example.com/a?b=1.'), [
        'https://example.com/a?b=1',
      ]);
      expect(urls('(https://example.com/x), and "http://a.b/c";'), [
        'https://example.com/x',
        'http://a.b/c',
      ]);
      expect(urls('**https://example.com**'), ['https://example.com']);
    });

    test('a closing bracket that closes one inside stays', () {
      expect(urls('https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dart_(language)'), [
        'https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dart_(language)',
      ]);
      expect(urls('[x](https://example.com/a)'), ['https://example.com/a']);
    });

    test('only http and https; no host, no link', () {
      expect(urls('ftp://x.y file:///etc/passwd javascript:alert(1)'), isEmpty);
      expect(urls('https:// http://'), isEmpty);
    });

    test('IPv6, ports, unicode paths, case-insensitive scheme', () {
      expect(urls('HTTP://[::1]:8080/x'), ['HTTP://[::1]:8080/x']);
      expect(urls('https://vi.wikipedia.org/wiki/Việt_Nam'), [
        'https://vi.wikipedia.org/wiki/Việt_Nam',
      ]);
    });

    test('longer than 2048 is not a link', () {
      expect(urls('https://a.b/${'x' * 2100}'), isEmpty);
    });

    test('linkAt answers for the characters of the link only', () {
      const text = 'go to https://example.com now';
      expect(linkAt(text, 5), isNull);
      expect(linkAt(text, 6)!.target, 'https://example.com');
      expect(linkAt(text, 24)!.target, 'https://example.com');
      expect(linkAt(text, 25), isNull);
    });
  });

  group('paths (A10 D7)', () {
    test('absolute, home, dot-relative, with a line and column', () {
      expect(paths('open /usr/local/bin/dart now'), [
        ('/usr/local/bin/dart', null, null),
      ]);
      expect(paths('~/work/a.txt:12:3 ./x.dart ../y/z.md'), [
        ('~/work/a.txt', 12, 3),
        ('./x.dart', null, null),
        ('../y/z.md', null, null),
      ]);
      expect(paths('lib/main.dart(42,7): error'), [('lib/main.dart', 42, 7)]);
    });

    test('a trailing full stop is not part of the path', () {
      expect(paths('wrote /tmp/out.txt.'), [('/tmp/out.txt', null, null)]);
    });

    test('words with a slash and no extension are not paths', () {
      expect(paths('and/or yes/no 1/2'), isEmpty);
    });

    test('a URL path is not also a file path', () {
      expect(paths('https://example.com/a/b.txt'), isEmpty);
    });

    test('Windows drive paths', () {
      expect(paths(r'at C:\Users\Nguyễn\a.txt:9'), [
        (r'C:\Users\Nguyễn\a.txt', 9, null),
      ]);
    });

    test('toString never shows the target', () {
      expect(findLinks('https://example.com').single.toString(), isNot(contains('example')));
    });
  });

  group('OSC 8 targets (A10 D10)', () {
    test('http and https only, bounded, no controls', () {
      expect(isSafeHyperlinkUri('https://example.com/a'), isTrue);
      expect(isSafeHyperlinkUri('javascript:alert(1)'), isFalse);
      expect(isSafeHyperlinkUri('file:///etc/passwd'), isFalse);
      expect(isSafeHyperlinkUri('https://example.com/\n'), isFalse);
      expect(isSafeHyperlinkUri('https://a.b/${'x' * 2100}'), isFalse);
      expect(isSafeHyperlinkUri('//example.com'), isFalse);
    });

    test('visible text that is the address, or on its host, needs no question',
        () {
      expect(hyperlinkTextMatches('https://example.com/a', 'https://example.com/a'), isTrue);
      expect(hyperlinkTextMatches('https://example.com/b', 'https://example.com/a'), isTrue);
      expect(hyperlinkTextMatches('click here', 'https://example.com/a'), isFalse);
      expect(hyperlinkTextMatches('https://paypal.com', 'https://evil.example'), isFalse);
    });
  });
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**


File: `packages/shellx_core/lib/src/util/terminal_links.dart`

```dart
/// What a line of terminal text links to (A10 D6, D7, D10). Pure Dart, no
/// xterm: the desktop and the phone each map a buffer line to a string and
/// the cell of every character, then ask here. Nothing in this file logs.
library;

/// The longest URL or path considered (A10 D6).
const int kMaxLinkLength = 2048;

enum TerminalLinkKind { url, path }

/// A link in a line of text: `[start, end)` are indices into the text that
/// was scanned, [target] is the URL, or the path without its line suffix.
final class TerminalLink {
  const TerminalLink({
    required this.kind,
    required this.start,
    required this.end,
    required this.target,
    this.line,
    this.column,
  });

  final TerminalLinkKind kind;
  final int start;
  final int end;
  final String target;
  final int? line;
  final int? column;

  bool contains(int index) => index >= start && index < end;

  /// Never shows what the link points at (privacy: A10 §5).
  @override
  String toString() => 'TerminalLink(…)';
}

const String _p = r'[A-Za-z0-9_.\-@%+=~\u00C0-\uFFFF]';
const String _before = r'(?<![A-Za-z0-9_.\-@%+=~/:\\\u00C0-\uFFFF])';
const String _suffix = r'(?::(\d{1,6})(?::(\d{1,6}))?|\((\d{1,6})(?:,\s*(\d{1,6}))?\))?';

final RegExp _urlPattern = RegExp(
  r'https?://[^\s<>"`\u0000-\u001f\u007f]+',
  caseSensitive: false,
);
final RegExp _absPath = RegExp(
  '$_before(?:~|\\.{1,2})?/(?:$_p+/)*$_p*$_suffix',
);
final RegExp _relPath = RegExp(
  '$_before(?:$_p+/)+$_p*\\.[A-Za-z0-9]{1,8}$_suffix',
);
final RegExp _winPath = RegExp(
  r'(?<![A-Za-z0-9])[A-Za-z]:\\(?:[^\s:*?"<>|\\]+\\)*[^\s:*?"<>|\\]*' + _suffix,
);

const String _trailingPunctuation = '.,;:!?\'"*';

/// [raw] without the punctuation a sentence adds after a URL: `.,;:!?'"*`,
/// and a closing `)`, `]` or `}` with no opening one inside the URL (so a
/// Wikipedia address ending in `)` keeps it).
String trimLinkEnd(String raw) {
  var s = raw;
  while (s.isNotEmpty) {
    final last = s[s.length - 1];
    if (_trailingPunctuation.contains(last)) {
      s = s.substring(0, s.length - 1);
      continue;
    }
    final open = switch (last) {
      ')' => '(',
      ']' => '[',
      '}' => '{',
      _ => null,
    };
    if (open != null && _count(s, last) > _count(s, open)) {
      s = s.substring(0, s.length - 1);
      continue;
    }
    break;
  }
  return s;
}

int _count(String s, String c) {
  var n = 0;
  for (var i = 0; i < s.length; i++) {
    if (s[i] == c) n++;
  }
  return n;
}

/// [raw] as a web address that may be opened: absolute `http`/`https` with a
/// host, no control characters, at most [kMaxLinkLength]. Null otherwise
/// (A10 D6: nothing else ever opens).
Uri? webUriOf(String raw) {
  if (raw.isEmpty || raw.length > kMaxLinkLength) return null;
  if (RegExp(r'[\u0000-\u001f\u007f\u0080-\u009f\s]').hasMatch(raw)) {
    return null;
  }
  final uri = Uri.tryParse(raw);
  if (uri == null || !uri.isAbsolute) return null;
  final scheme = uri.scheme.toLowerCase();
  if (scheme != 'http' && scheme != 'https') return null;
  if (uri.host.isEmpty) return null;
  return uri;
}

/// A10 D10: an OSC 8 target is acceptable only as [webUriOf] says.
bool isSafeHyperlinkUri(String uri) => webUriOf(uri) != null;

/// A10 D10: the text shown for an OSC 8 link already tells where it goes
/// (it is that address, or an address on the same host): no confirmation.
bool hyperlinkTextMatches(String visible, String uri) {
  final shown = visible.trim();
  if (shown == uri) return true;
  final target = webUriOf(uri);
  if (target == null) return false;
  final asUri = webUriOf(shown);
  if (asUri != null) return asUri.host.toLowerCase() == target.host.toLowerCase();
  return false;
}

/// Every link in [text], in order, none overlapping. URLs first; with
/// [paths] (a local pane, A10 D7) paths that do not overlap a URL.
List<TerminalLink> findLinks(String text, {bool paths = false}) {
  final links = <TerminalLink>[];
  for (final m in _urlPattern.allMatches(text)) {
    final trimmed = trimLinkEnd(m.group(0)!);
    if (webUriOf(trimmed) == null) continue;
    links.add(
      TerminalLink(
        kind: TerminalLinkKind.url,
        start: m.start,
        end: m.start + trimmed.length,
        target: trimmed,
      ),
    );
  }
  if (!paths) return links;
  final found = <TerminalLink>[];
  for (final regex in [_absPath, _relPath, _winPath]) {
    for (final m in regex.allMatches(text)) {
      final link = _pathLink(m);
      if (link == null) continue;
      final overlaps =
          links.any((l) => link.start < l.end && l.start < link.end) ||
          found.any((l) => link.start < l.end && l.start < link.end);
      if (!overlaps) found.add(link);
    }
  }
  links.addAll(found);
  links.sort((a, b) => a.start.compareTo(b.start));
  return links;
}

TerminalLink? _pathLink(RegExpMatch m) {
  final whole = m.group(0)!;
  final hasSuffix =
      m.group(1) != null || m.group(3) != null; // :12 or (12)
  var core = hasSuffix
      ? whole.substring(0, whole.length - _suffixLength(m, whole))
      : whole;
  if (!hasSuffix) core = _trimPathEnd(core);
  if (core.isEmpty || core.length > kMaxLinkLength) return null;
  // A lone "/" or "~" or "./" is not a path anyone means.
  if (core == '/' || core == '~' || core == './' || core == '../') return null;
  final line = int.tryParse(m.group(1) ?? m.group(3) ?? '');
  final col = int.tryParse(m.group(2) ?? m.group(4) ?? '');
  return TerminalLink(
    kind: TerminalLinkKind.path,
    start: m.start,
    end: m.start + core.length + (hasSuffix ? _suffixLength(m, whole) : 0),
    target: core,
    line: line,
    column: col,
  );
}

int _suffixLength(RegExpMatch m, String whole) {
  final tail = RegExp(r'(?::\d{1,6}(?::\d{1,6})?|\(\d{1,6}(?:,\s*\d{1,6})?\))$')
      .firstMatch(whole);
  return tail?.group(0)?.length ?? 0;
}

String _trimPathEnd(String s) {
  var t = s;
  while (t.length > 1 && '.,;:!?'.contains(t[t.length - 1])) {
    t = t.substring(0, t.length - 1);
  }
  return t;
}

/// The link under character [index] of [text], or null.
TerminalLink? linkAt(String text, int index, {bool paths = false}) {
  for (final link in findLinks(text, paths: paths)) {
    if (link.contains(index)) return link;
  }
  return null;
}
```

### Task 2: Desktop — the PTY hears a pane's size only when it settles (D20, D21) — **S**

Fixes the bug of §2 row 8: `terminal.onResize` called `view.resize` on every layout frame of a window or divider drag. The xterm grid still follows at once; the session waits 80 ms of quiet, or hears the size sooner when a key is typed, when the tab comes to the front, or on `flushResizes()`. The phone/desktop rule (A1 D6, "the side that last acted decides") is **kept**; its regression tests already exist (`local_sessions_test.dart` around line 178 and mobile `terminal_tab_controller_test.dart`).

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/desktop/lib/src/terminal/resize_settler.dart`
- Modify: `apps/desktop/lib/src/terminal/terminal_tabs.dart`
- Modify (test harness only): `apps/desktop/test/support/config_workspace.dart`, `apps/desktop/test/ui/sidebar_test_harness.dart`, `apps/desktop/test/ui/terminal_workspace_test.dart`, `apps/desktop/test/terminal/terminal_appearance_test.dart`, `apps/desktop/test/terminal/terminal_tabs_test.dart`
- Test: `apps/desktop/test/terminal/resize_settler_test.dart`, `apps/desktop/test/terminal/terminal_tabs_resize_test.dart`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `LocalSessionView.resize(cols, rows)`, `DesktopTerminal.onResize`.
- Produces:
  - `final class ResizeSettler { ResizeSettler({Duration settle = 80 ms}); void request(int id, int cols, int rows, void Function(int, int) send); bool flush(int id); void flushAll(); void cancel(int id); bool hasPending(int id); void dispose(); }` — `settle == Duration.zero` sends at once.
  - `final resizeSettleProvider = Provider<Duration>((ref) => 80 ms)` and `TerminalTabs.flushResizes()` in `terminal_tabs.dart`.

```dart
import 'package:fake_async/fake_async.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:shellx_desktop/src/terminal/resize_settler.dart';

void main() {
  test('120 sizes in two seconds make one send, after the last settles', () {
    fakeAsync((async) {
      final settler = ResizeSettler();
      final sent = <(int, int)>[];
      for (var i = 0; i < 120; i++) {
        settler.request(1, 80 + i % 40, 24, (c, r) => sent.add((c, r)));
        if (i < 119) async.elapse(const Duration(milliseconds: 16));
      }
      expect(sent, isEmpty, reason: 'no size reached the PTY mid-drag');
      async.elapse(const Duration(milliseconds: 79));
      expect(sent, isEmpty);
      async.elapse(const Duration(milliseconds: 1));
      expect(sent, [(80 + 119 % 40, 24)]);
      expect(settler.hasPending(1), isFalse);
    });
  });

  test('flush sends the waiting size at once and only once', () {
    fakeAsync((async) {
      final settler = ResizeSettler();
      final sent = <(int, int)>[];
      settler.request(1, 100, 30, (c, r) => sent.add((c, r)));
      expect(settler.flush(1), isTrue);
      expect(sent, [(100, 30)]);
      expect(settler.flush(1), isFalse);
      async.elapse(const Duration(seconds: 1));
      expect(sent, hasLength(1));
    });
  });

  test('panes are independent; cancel drops a pane\'s size', () {
    fakeAsync((async) {
      final settler = ResizeSettler();
      final sent = <(int, int, int)>[];
      settler.request(1, 80, 24, (c, r) => sent.add((1, c, r)));
      settler.request(2, 90, 30, (c, r) => sent.add((2, c, r)));
      settler.cancel(1);
      async.elapse(const Duration(milliseconds: 100));
      expect(sent, [(2, 90, 30)]);
    });
  });

  test('flushAll and dispose', () {
    fakeAsync((async) {
      final settler = ResizeSettler();
      final sent = <int>[];
      settler.request(1, 80, 24, (c, r) => sent.add(1));
      settler.request(2, 80, 24, (c, r) => sent.add(2));
      settler.flushAll();
      expect(sent, [1, 2]);
      settler.request(3, 80, 24, (c, r) => sent.add(3));
      settler.dispose();
      async.elapse(const Duration(seconds: 1));
      expect(sent, [1, 2]);
      settler.request(4, 80, 24, (c, r) => sent.add(4));
      async.elapse(const Duration(seconds: 1));
      expect(sent, [1, 2], reason: 'a disposed settler sends nothing');
    });
  });

  test('a zero settle time sends straight away', () {
    final sent = <int>[];
    ResizeSettler(
      settle: Duration.zero,
    ).request(1, 80, 24, (c, r) => sent.add(c));
    expect(sent, [80]);
  });
}
```

```dart
import 'package:fake_async/fake_async.dart';
import 'package:flutter/painting.dart' show Size;
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:shellx_agent/shellx_agent.dart';
import 'package:shellx_desktop/l10n/app_localizations.dart';
import 'package:shellx_desktop/src/terminal/local_sessions_provider.dart';
import 'package:shellx_desktop/src/terminal/pane_layout.dart';
import 'package:shellx_desktop/src/terminal/terminal_tabs.dart';
import 'package:flutter/widgets.dart' show Locale;

import '../support/fake_local_sessions.dart';

/// A10 D20: what the PTY is told while a window or a divider is dragged.
/// The container keeps the production settle time (80 ms).
late FakeLocalSessions local;
late ProviderContainer container;

TerminalTabs tabs() => container.read(terminalTabsProvider.notifier);
TerminalTabsState state() => container.read(terminalTabsProvider);

void boot() {
  container = ProviderContainer.test(
    overrides: [
      localSessionsProvider.overrideWithValue(local),
      appL10nProvider.overrideWithValue(
        lookupAppLocalizations(const Locale('en')),
      ),
    ],
  );
  state();
}

void main() {
  setUp(() => local = FakeLocalSessions());

  test('120 grid changes in two seconds make one resize of the session', () {
    fakeAsync((async) {
      local.add(local.summary(1, origin: SessionOrigin.desktop));
      boot();
      final view = local.views[1]!;
      final terminal = state().tabs.single.terminal;
      final before = view.resizes.length;
      for (var i = 0; i < 120; i++) {
        terminal.resize(80 + i % 30, 24);
        if (i < 119) async.elapse(const Duration(milliseconds: 16));
      }
      expect(view.resizes.length, before, reason: 'nothing mid-drag');
      async.elapse(const Duration(milliseconds: 79));
      expect(view.resizes.length, before);
      async.elapse(const Duration(milliseconds: 1));
      expect(view.resizes.length, before + 1);
      expect(view.resizes.last, (80 + 119 % 30, 24));
    });
  });

  test('the xterm grid follows at once; only the session waits', () {
    fakeAsync((async) {
      local.add(local.summary(1, origin: SessionOrigin.desktop));
      boot();
      final terminal = state().tabs.single.terminal;
      terminal.resize(132, 43);
      expect((terminal.viewWidth, terminal.viewHeight), (132, 43));
    });
  });

  test(
    'a key typed right after a resize reaches a PTY that has the new size',
    () {
      fakeAsync((async) {
        local.add(local.summary(1, origin: SessionOrigin.desktop));
        boot();
        final view = local.views[1]!;
        final terminal = state().tabs.single.terminal;
        final before = view.resizes.length;
        terminal.resize(100, 30);
        expect(view.resizes.length, before);
        terminal.textInput('x');
        expect(view.resizes.length, before + 1, reason: 'sent before the key');
        expect(view.resizes.last, (100, 30));
        expect(view.writes.last, 'x');
      });
    },
  );

  test('flushResizes sends what waits, once', () {
    fakeAsync((async) {
      local.add(local.summary(1, origin: SessionOrigin.desktop));
      boot();
      final view = local.views[1]!;
      final before = view.resizes.length;
      state().tabs.single.terminal.resize(90, 20);
      tabs().flushResizes();
      tabs().flushResizes();
      async.elapse(const Duration(seconds: 1));
      expect(view.resizes.length, before + 1);
    });
  });

  test('a pane that ends with a size waiting never gets it', () {
    fakeAsync((async) {
      local.add(local.summary(1, origin: SessionOrigin.desktop));
      boot();
      final view = local.views[1]!;
      final before = view.resizes.length;
      state().tabs.single.terminal.resize(90, 20);
      view.end(3);
      async.elapse(const Duration(seconds: 1));
      expect(view.resizes.length, before);
    });
  });

  test(
    'a hidden tab is not told; coming to the front it hears its size now',
    () {
      fakeAsync((async) {
        local.add(local.summary(1, origin: SessionOrigin.desktop));
        local.add(local.summary(2, origin: SessionOrigin.desktop));
        boot();
        tabs().reportGrid((cols: 80, rows: 24));
        final hidden = state().tabs.last; // tab 2: not selected
        expect(state().selectedId, 1);
        final view = local.views[2]!;
        final before = view.resizes.length;
        hidden.terminal.resize(111, 33);
        async.elapse(const Duration(seconds: 1));
        expect(view.resizes.length, before, reason: 'never while hidden');
        tabs().select(2);
        expect(view.resizes.last, (111, 33));
      });
    },
  );

  test(
    'closing the tab drops its waiting size and disposing the notifier is safe',
    () {
      fakeAsync((async) {
        local.add(local.summary(1, origin: SessionOrigin.desktop));
        boot();
        state().tabs.single.terminal.resize(90, 20);
        tabs().close(1);
        container.dispose();
        async.elapse(
          const Duration(seconds: 1),
        ); // no timer fires into a dead notifier
      });
    },
  );

  test('a split: both visible panes settle independently', () async {
    local.add(local.summary(1, origin: SessionOrigin.desktop));
    boot();
    tabs().reportPaneMetrics(
      const PaneMetrics(
        area: Size(1000, 600),
        minPane: Size(120, 80),
        divider: 1,
        devicePixelRatio: 1,
      ),
    );
    await tabs().split(SplitAxis.row);
    final tab = state().selected!;
    final ids = tab.paneOrder;
    expect(ids, hasLength(2));
    fakeAsync((async) {
      final a = local.views[ids[0]]!;
      final b = local.views[ids[1]]!;
      final beforeA = a.resizes.length;
      final beforeB = b.resizes.length;
      tab.panes[ids[0]]!.terminal.resize(70, 20);
      async.elapse(const Duration(milliseconds: 40));
      tab.panes[ids[1]]!.terminal.resize(60, 20);
      async.elapse(const Duration(milliseconds: 41));
      expect(a.resizes.length, beforeA + 1, reason: 'its own 80 ms are over');
      expect(b.resizes.length, beforeB, reason: 'still waiting');
      async.elapse(const Duration(milliseconds: 40));
      expect(b.resizes.length, beforeB + 1);
    });
  });
}
```

File: `apps/desktop/lib/src/terminal/resize_settler.dart`

```dart
import 'dart:async';

/// A10 D20: what the PTY is told about a pane's size. xterm resizes its own
/// grid at once (the screen is right); the session hears the size only
/// after it stayed the same for [settle] — never every frame of a window
/// or divider drag — or sooner when something needs it right (typing,
/// [flush]). Per pane (session id).
final class ResizeSettler {
  ResizeSettler({this.settle = const Duration(milliseconds: 80)});

  /// Zero sends at once (tests that do not care).
  final Duration settle;
  final _pending = <int, _Pending>{};
  bool _disposed = false;

  bool hasPending(int id) => _pending.containsKey(id);

  /// Remembers the latest size of pane [id]; [send] is called with it once
  /// it settles.
  void request(int id, int cols, int rows, void Function(int, int) send) {
    if (_disposed) return;
    if (settle == Duration.zero) {
      send(cols, rows);
      return;
    }
    _pending[id]?.timer.cancel();
    late final _Pending entry;
    entry = _Pending(
      cols,
      rows,
      send,
      Timer(settle, () {
        if (identical(_pending[id], entry)) _pending.remove(id);
        send(cols, rows);
      }),
    );
    _pending[id] = entry;
  }

  /// Sends pane [id]'s waiting size now (before a key reaches the PTY, when
  /// the pane becomes visible). True when there was one.
  bool flush(int id) {
    final entry = _pending.remove(id);
    if (entry == null) return false;
    entry.timer.cancel();
    entry.send(entry.cols, entry.rows);
    return true;
  }

  void flushAll() {
    for (final id in _pending.keys.toList()) {
      flush(id);
    }
  }

  /// The pane closed or ended: nothing is sent for it any more.
  void cancel(int id) => _pending.remove(id)?.timer.cancel();

  void dispose() {
    _disposed = true;
    for (final e in _pending.values) {
      e.timer.cancel();
    }
    _pending.clear();
  }
}

final class _Pending {
  _Pending(this.cols, this.rows, this.send, this.timer);
  final int cols;
  final int rows;
  final void Function(int, int) send;
  final Timer timer;
}
```

### Task 3: Desktop — paste robustness (D13, D14) — **M**

The PTY writer drops what exceeds its 1 MiB queue and `_paste` sent the whole text in one write: a large paste was silently truncated. Now: ≤ 64 KiB as before (`Terminal.paste` brackets it); larger in 32 KiB pieces 8 ms apart inside one bracketed paste; > 1 MiB asks first; > 16 MiB refuses; the target pane is chosen **before** the clipboard is read. Bracketed paste itself and control-character stripping already existed and are untouched.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/desktop/lib/src/terminal/{paste_sender,pane_paste,byte_size}.dart`
- Modify: `apps/desktop/lib/src/terminal/terminal_tabs.dart` (`PasteTooLargeNotice`), `apps/desktop/lib/src/ui/terminal/terminal_overlays.dart` (toast text), `apps/desktop/lib/src/ui/terminal/terminal_workspace_page.dart` (`_paste`), `apps/desktop/lib/l10n/app_{en,vi}.arb`
- Test: `apps/desktop/test/terminal/paste_sender_test.dart`, `apps/desktop/test/ui/paste_flow_test.dart`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `normalizePastedText` (terminal_input.dart), `DesktopTerminal.paste/sendUserText/bracketedPasteMode`, `WindowRouter.confirm(ConfirmRequest)`.
- Produces:
  - `abstract interface class PasteSink { bool get alive; bool get bracketed; void paste(String); void send(String); }`, `enum PasteOutcome { sent, empty, tooLarge, declined, cancelled }`, `PasteSender({direct, piece, gap, confirmAbove, maxBytes}).send(int key, String text, PasteSink sink, {Future<bool> Function(int bytes)? confirm})`, `.cancel(int key)`
  - `final pasteSenderProvider`, `PanePasteSink(TerminalTabsState Function() tabs, int paneId, DesktopTerminal terminal)` (alive = pane still in a tab, not exited, same terminal)
  - `String formatByteSize(int bytes, {String? locale})`
  - `final class PasteTooLargeNotice extends TabNotice`

```dart
import 'package:fake_async/fake_async.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:shellx_desktop/src/terminal/paste_sender.dart';

final class FakeSink implements PasteSink {
  FakeSink({this.bracketed = false});
  @override
  bool alive = true;
  @override
  final bool bracketed;
  final pasted = <String>[];
  final sent = <String>[];
  @override
  void paste(String text) => pasted.add(text);
  @override
  void send(String data) => sent.add(data);
  String get all => sent.join();
}

void main() {
  test('a small paste is one Terminal.paste (it brackets itself)', () async {
    final sink = FakeSink(bracketed: true);
    final out = await PasteSender().send(1, 'ls -la\n', sink);
    expect(out, PasteOutcome.sent);
    expect(sink.pasted, ['ls -la\r']);
    expect(sink.sent, isEmpty);
  });

  test('control characters never survive, large or small', () async {
    final sink = FakeSink(bracketed: true);
    await PasteSender().send(1, 'a\x1b[201~rm -rf ~', sink);
    expect(sink.pasted.single, 'a[201~rm -rf ~');
  });

  test('empty clipboard text sends nothing', () async {
    final sink = FakeSink();
    expect(await PasteSender().send(1, '', sink), PasteOutcome.empty);
    expect(await PasteSender().send(1, '\x1b\x07', sink), PasteOutcome.empty);
    expect(sink.pasted, isEmpty);
  });

  test('800 KB goes in 32 KiB pieces 8 ms apart, bracketed once', () {
    fakeAsync((async) {
      final sink = FakeSink(bracketed: true);
      final text = 'x' * 800000;
      PasteOutcome? outcome;
      PasteSender().send(1, text, sink).then((o) => outcome = o);
      async.flushMicrotasks();
      expect(sink.sent.first, '\x1b[200~');
      expect(sink.sent[1].length, 32 * 1024);
      expect(outcome, isNull);
      async.elapse(const Duration(seconds: 1));
      expect(outcome, PasteOutcome.sent);
      expect(sink.sent.last, '\x1b[201~');
      expect(sink.sent.where((s) => s == '\x1b[200~'), hasLength(1));
      final body = sink.sent.sublist(1, sink.sent.length - 1).join();
      expect(body, text);
      expect(sink.sent.length, 2 + (800000 / (32 * 1024)).ceil());
    });
  });

  test('without bracketed paste there are no markers', () {
    fakeAsync((async) {
      final sink = FakeSink();
      PasteSender().send(1, 'y' * 100000, sink);
      async.elapse(const Duration(seconds: 1));
      expect(sink.all, 'y' * 100000);
    });
  });

  test('a surrogate pair is never cut between two pieces', () {
    fakeAsync((async) {
      final sink = FakeSink();
      // 😀 is two UTF-16 units; put it across the 32 KiB boundary.
      final text = '${'a' * (32 * 1024 - 1)}😀${'b' * 70000}';
      PasteSender().send(1, text, sink);
      async.elapse(const Duration(seconds: 1));
      for (final piece in sink.sent) {
        final u = piece.codeUnitAt(piece.length - 1);
        expect(u >= 0xD800 && u <= 0xDBFF, isFalse);
      }
      expect(sink.all, text);
    });
  });

  test('a pane that goes away stops the paste and sends nothing more', () {
    fakeAsync((async) {
      final sink = FakeSink(bracketed: true);
      PasteOutcome? outcome;
      PasteSender().send(1, 'z' * 400000, sink).then((o) => outcome = o);
      async.elapse(const Duration(milliseconds: 20));
      final before = sink.sent.length;
      sink.alive = false;
      async.elapse(const Duration(seconds: 2));
      expect(outcome, PasteOutcome.cancelled);
      expect(sink.sent.length, before);
      expect(sink.sent.contains('\x1b[201~'), isFalse);
    });
  });

  test(
    'cancel on a live pane closes the bracket so the program is not stuck',
    () {
      fakeAsync((async) {
        final sender = PasteSender();
        final sink = FakeSink(bracketed: true);
        PasteOutcome? outcome;
        sender.send(1, 'z' * 400000, sink).then((o) => outcome = o);
        async.elapse(const Duration(milliseconds: 20));
        sender.cancel(1);
        async.elapse(const Duration(seconds: 2));
        expect(outcome, PasteOutcome.cancelled);
        expect(sink.sent.last, '\x1b[201~');
      });
    },
  );

  test('two pastes into one pane run one after the other', () {
    fakeAsync((async) {
      final sender = PasteSender();
      final sink = FakeSink();
      sender.send(1, 'a' * 100000, sink);
      sender.send(1, 'b' * 100000, sink);
      async.elapse(const Duration(seconds: 2));
      expect(sink.all, '${'a' * 100000}${'b' * 100000}');
    });
  });

  test('above 1 MiB the user is asked; no means nothing is sent', () async {
    final sink = FakeSink();
    int? asked;
    final out = await PasteSender().send(
      1,
      'q' * (1024 * 1024 + 1),
      sink,
      confirm: (bytes) async {
        asked = bytes;
        return false;
      },
    );
    expect(out, PasteOutcome.declined);
    expect(asked, 1024 * 1024 + 1);
    expect(sink.sent, isEmpty);
    expect(sink.pasted, isEmpty);
  });

  test('over 16 MiB is refused without asking', () async {
    final sink = FakeSink();
    var asked = false;
    final out = await PasteSender().send(
      1,
      'q' * (16 * 1024 * 1024 + 1),
      sink,
      confirm: (_) async => asked = true,
    );
    expect(out, PasteOutcome.tooLarge);
    expect(asked, isFalse);
  });

  test('the pane may be gone by the time the user confirms', () async {
    final sink = FakeSink();
    final out = await PasteSender().send(
      1,
      'q' * (1024 * 1024 + 1),
      sink,
      confirm: (_) async {
        sink.alive = false;
        return true;
      },
    );
    expect(out, PasteOutcome.cancelled);
    expect(sink.sent, isEmpty);
  });
}
```

```dart
import 'dart:async';

import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:shellx_desktop/src/terminal/pane_layout.dart';
import 'package:shellx_desktop/src/terminal/terminal_tabs.dart';
import 'package:shellx_desktop/src/ui/window_router.dart';

import '../support/config_workspace.dart';

/// With [gate] the clipboard answers only once the gate completes (the
/// time the platform takes to read it).
void mockClipboard(WidgetTester t, String text, {Completer<void>? gate}) {
  t.binding.defaultBinaryMessenger.setMockMethodCallHandler(
    SystemChannels.platform,
    (call) async {
      if (call.method != 'Clipboard.getData') return null;
      await gate?.future;
      return <String, Object?>{'text': text};
    },
  );
  addTearDown(
    () => t.binding.defaultBinaryMessenger.setMockMethodCallHandler(
      SystemChannels.platform,
      null,
    ),
  );
}

Future<void> pasteChord(WidgetTester t) async {
  await t.sendKeyDownEvent(LogicalKeyboardKey.metaLeft);
  await t.sendKeyEvent(LogicalKeyboardKey.keyV);
  await t.sendKeyUpEvent(LogicalKeyboardKey.metaLeft);
  await t.pump();
}

void main() {
  testWidgets(
    'a small paste is written at once, whole',
    (t) async {
      final w = await pumpConfiguredWorkspace(t);
      mockClipboard(t, 'echo hi\n');
      final id = w.container.read(terminalTabsProvider).selected!.focusedPaneId;
      await pasteChord(t);
      await t.pump();
      expect(w.local.views[id]!.writes.join(), 'echo hi\r');
      await disposeWorkspace(t, w.container);
    },
    variant: TargetPlatformVariant.only(TargetPlatform.macOS),
  );

  testWidgets(
    '800 KB arrives whole, in pieces, bracketed once',
    (t) async {
      final w = await pumpConfiguredWorkspace(t);
      final tab = w.container.read(terminalTabsProvider).selected!;
      tab.terminal.write('\x1b[?2004h'); // the program asks for bracketed paste
      final text = 'x' * 800000;
      mockClipboard(t, text);
      await pasteChord(t);
      await t.pump(const Duration(seconds: 2));
      await t.pump(const Duration(seconds: 2));
      final writes = w.local.views[tab.focusedPaneId]!.writes;
      expect(writes.first, '\x1b[200~');
      expect(writes.last, '\x1b[201~');
      expect(writes.sublist(1, writes.length - 1).join(), text);
      await disposeWorkspace(t, w.container);
    },
    variant: TargetPlatformVariant.only(TargetPlatform.macOS),
  );

  testWidgets(
    'over 1 MiB asks first; No sends nothing',
    (t) async {
      final w = await pumpConfiguredWorkspace(t);
      final tab = w.container.read(terminalTabsProvider).selected!;
      mockClipboard(t, 'y' * (1024 * 1024 + 10));
      await pasteChord(t);
      await t.pump();
      final ask = w.container.read(windowRouterProvider).confirm!;
      expect(ask.title, 'Paste 1.0 MB?');
      ask.complete(false);
      await t.pump(const Duration(seconds: 1));
      expect(w.local.views[tab.focusedPaneId]!.writes, isEmpty);
      await disposeWorkspace(t, w.container);
    },
    variant: TargetPlatformVariant.only(TargetPlatform.macOS),
  );

  testWidgets(
    'over 16 MiB is refused with a toast',
    (t) async {
      final w = await pumpConfiguredWorkspace(t);
      final tab = w.container.read(terminalTabsProvider).selected!;
      mockClipboard(t, 'z' * (16 * 1024 * 1024 + 1));
      await pasteChord(t);
      await t.pump();
      expect(
        w.container.read(terminalTabsProvider).notice,
        isA<PasteTooLargeNotice>(),
      );
      expect(w.local.views[tab.focusedPaneId]!.writes, isEmpty);
      await t.pump(const Duration(seconds: 7));
      await disposeWorkspace(t, w.container);
    },
    variant: TargetPlatformVariant.only(TargetPlatform.macOS),
  );

  testWidgets(
    'the text goes to the pane focused when the key was pressed',
    (t) async {
      final w = await pumpConfiguredWorkspace(t);
      final tabs = w.container.read(terminalTabsProvider.notifier);
      await tabs.split(SplitAxis.row); // pane 101 focused, pane 100 not
      await t.pump();
      final first = w.container.read(terminalTabsProvider).selected!;
      final target = first.focusedPaneId;
      final other = first.paneOrder.firstWhere((id) => id != target);
      final gate = Completer<void>();
      mockClipboard(t, 'to the first', gate: gate);
      await pasteChord(t);
      tabs.focusPane(other); // focus moves while the clipboard is read
      await t.pump();
      gate.complete();
      await t.pump();
      await t.pump();
      expect(w.local.views[target]!.writes.join(), 'to the first');
      expect(w.local.views[other]!.writes, isEmpty);
      await disposeWorkspace(t, w.container);
    },
    variant: TargetPlatformVariant.only(TargetPlatform.macOS),
  );

  testWidgets(
    'a pane that closes mid-paste stops it and sends nothing more',
    (t) async {
      final w = await pumpConfiguredWorkspace(t);
      final tab = w.container.read(terminalTabsProvider).selected!;
      mockClipboard(t, 'k' * 400000);
      await pasteChord(t);
      await t.pump(const Duration(milliseconds: 20));
      final view = w.local.views[tab.focusedPaneId]!;
      w.container.read(terminalTabsProvider.notifier).close(tab.id);
      await t.pump();
      final seen = view.writes.length;
      await t.pump(const Duration(seconds: 3));
      expect(view.writes.length, seen);
      await disposeWorkspace(t, w.container);
    },
    variant: TargetPlatformVariant.only(TargetPlatform.macOS),
  );
}
```

File: `apps/desktop/lib/src/terminal/paste_sender.dart`

```dart
import 'dart:async';
import 'dart:convert';

import 'terminal_input.dart' show normalizePastedText;

/// What [PasteSender] writes to: one pane's terminal (A10 D13). The sink is
/// built from the pane that was focused when the user pasted, **before** the
/// clipboard was read.
abstract interface class PasteSink {
  /// The pane still exists, has not exited, and is still the same session.
  bool get alive;

  /// The program turned bracketed paste on.
  bool get bracketed;

  /// A small paste: `Terminal.paste` (brackets it itself).
  void paste(String text);

  /// Raw typed bytes: the markers and the pieces of a large paste.
  void send(String data);
}

enum PasteOutcome { sent, empty, tooLarge, declined, cancelled }

/// A10 D13: pastes in order, per pane; a large one goes in pieces so the
/// PTY's 1 MiB input queue never overflows (it drops what does not fit).
final class PasteSender {
  PasteSender({
    this.direct = 64 * 1024,
    this.piece = 32 * 1024,
    this.gap = const Duration(milliseconds: 8),
    this.confirmAbove = 1024 * 1024,
    this.maxBytes = 16 * 1024 * 1024,
  });

  /// Up to this many bytes go in one write.
  final int direct;
  final int piece;
  final Duration gap;
  final int confirmAbove;
  final int maxBytes;

  static const String _start = '\x1b[200~';
  static const String _end = '\x1b[201~';

  final _tails = <int, Future<void>>{};
  final _cancelled = <int>{};

  /// Stops pane [key]'s running and queued pastes after the piece being
  /// written; a bracketed one is closed first so the program leaves paste
  /// mode.
  void cancel(int key) => _cancelled.add(key);

  /// Pastes [text] into [sink] for pane [key]. [confirm] is asked (with the
  /// size in bytes) above [confirmAbove]. Never throws for a gone pane.
  Future<PasteOutcome> send(
    int key,
    String text,
    PasteSink sink, {
    Future<bool> Function(int bytes)? confirm,
  }) {
    final done = Completer<PasteOutcome>();
    final previous = _tails[key] ?? Future<void>.value();
    final run = previous.then((_) async {
      try {
        done.complete(await _run(key, text, sink, confirm));
      } on Object catch (error, stack) {
        done.completeError(error, stack);
      }
    });
    _tails[key] = run.whenComplete(() {
      if (_tails[key] == run) {
        _tails.remove(key);
        _cancelled.remove(key);
      }
    });
    return done.future;
  }

  Future<PasteOutcome> _run(
    int key,
    String raw,
    PasteSink sink,
    Future<bool> Function(int bytes)? confirm,
  ) async {
    if (raw.isEmpty) return PasteOutcome.empty;
    final text = normalizePastedText(raw);
    if (text.isEmpty) return PasteOutcome.empty;
    final bytes = text.length <= direct ~/ 4
        ? text.length
        : utf8.encode(text).length;
    if (bytes > maxBytes) return PasteOutcome.tooLarge;
    if (!sink.alive || _cancelled.contains(key)) return PasteOutcome.cancelled;
    if (bytes > confirmAbove && confirm != null) {
      if (!await confirm(bytes)) return PasteOutcome.declined;
      if (!sink.alive || _cancelled.contains(key)) {
        return PasteOutcome.cancelled;
      }
    }
    if (bytes <= direct) {
      sink.paste(text);
      return PasteOutcome.sent;
    }
    final bracketed = sink.bracketed;
    if (bracketed) sink.send(_start);
    var at = 0;
    while (at < text.length) {
      if (!sink.alive) return PasteOutcome.cancelled;
      if (_cancelled.contains(key)) {
        if (bracketed) sink.send(_end);
        return PasteOutcome.cancelled;
      }
      var end = at + piece;
      if (end >= text.length) {
        end = text.length;
      } else {
        final unit = text.codeUnitAt(end - 1);
        // Never split a surrogate pair between two writes.
        if (unit >= 0xD800 && unit <= 0xDBFF) end--;
      }
      sink.send(text.substring(at, end));
      at = end;
      if (at < text.length) await Future<void>.delayed(gap);
    }
    if (bracketed) sink.send(_end);
    return PasteOutcome.sent;
  }
}
```

File: `apps/desktop/lib/src/terminal/pane_paste.dart`

```dart
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';

import 'desktop_terminal.dart';
import 'paste_sender.dart';
import 'terminal_tabs.dart';

/// A10 D13: one sender for the window, so pastes into a pane queue up
/// whether they come from the keyboard, the menu or a drop.
final pasteSenderProvider = Provider<PasteSender>((ref) => PasteSender());

/// Pane [paneId]'s terminal as [PasteSender] writes to it. It is alive
/// while the pane is still in a tab, has not exited and still has the
/// terminal it had when the user pasted (a reconnect replaces the
/// session).
final class PanePasteSink implements PasteSink {
  PanePasteSink(this._tabs, this._paneId, this._terminal);

  /// Reads the current tabs (`ref.read(terminalTabsProvider)`).
  final TerminalTabsState Function() _tabs;
  final int _paneId;
  final DesktopTerminal _terminal;

  @override
  bool get alive {
    final pane = _tabs().tabOfSession(_paneId)?.panes[_paneId];
    return pane != null && !pane.exited && identical(pane.terminal, _terminal);
  }

  @override
  bool get bracketed => _terminal.bracketedPasteMode;

  @override
  void paste(String text) => _terminal.paste(text);

  @override
  void send(String data) => _terminal.sendUserText(data);
}
```

```dart
import 'package:intl/intl.dart';

/// "512 KB", "1.2 MB": for the confirmation of a very large paste (A10
/// D13). Powers of 1024, the decimal mark of [locale].
String formatByteSize(int bytes, {String? locale}) {
  if (bytes < 1024 * 1024) {
    return '${(bytes / 1024).ceil().clamp(1, 1023)} KB';
  }
  final mb = NumberFormat('#,##0.0', locale).format(bytes / (1024 * 1024));
  return '$mb MB';
}
```

Replace `_paste` in `terminal_workspace_page.dart` (imports: `byte_size.dart`, `pane_paste.dart`, `paste_sender.dart` from `../../terminal/`):

```dart
  /// ⌘V / Ctrl+Shift+V / the menu into the **focused pane of the selected
  /// tab at the moment of the key** — chosen before the clipboard is read,
  /// so a tab switch while it is read cannot send the text elsewhere (A10
  /// D13). Sanitised and bracketed by [DesktopTerminal.paste]; a large text
  /// is asked about, then sent in pieces ([PasteSender]).
  Future<void> _paste() async {
    final tab = ref.read(terminalTabsProvider).selected;
    if (tab == null) return;
    final paneId = tab.focusedPaneId;
    final terminal = tab.focused.terminal;
    final data = await Clipboard.getData(Clipboard.kTextPlain);
    final text = data?.text;
    if (!mounted || text == null || text.isEmpty) return;
    final sink = PanePasteSink(
      () => ref.read(terminalTabsProvider),
      paneId,
      terminal,
    );
    // An exited pane closes on Enter (UI §2.6): a paste must not do that.
    if (!sink.alive) return;
    final l10n = AppLocalizations.of(context);
    final outcome = await ref
        .read(pasteSenderProvider)
        .send(
          paneId,
          text,
          sink,
          confirm: (bytes) => ref
              .read(windowRouterProvider.notifier)
              .confirm(
                ConfirmRequest(
                  title: l10n.pasteLargeTitle(
                    formatByteSize(bytes, locale: l10n.localeName),
                  ),
                  body: l10n.pasteLargeBody,
                  confirmLabel: l10n.pasteLargeAction,
                  destructive: false,
                ),
              ),
        );
    if (outcome == PasteOutcome.tooLarge && mounted) {
      ref
          .read(terminalTabsProvider.notifier)
          .showNotice(const PasteTooLargeNotice());
    }
  }
```

```dart
// import 'package:shellx_desktop/src/terminal/paste_sender.dart';   (top of the file)

  // A10 D13: a large paste goes in paced pieces, so the 1 MiB write queue never overflows.
  testWidgets('a 792 KB paste through PasteSender arrives whole', (
    tester,
  ) async {
    final dir = await Directory.systemTemp.createTemp('shellx-paste-');
    addTearDown(() => dir.delete(recursive: true));
    final (pty, transcript) = open(shell);
    _type(pty, 'cat > ${dir.path}/pasted.txt');
    await Future<void>.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 300));
    final sink = _PtySink(pty);
    // Lines, not one long line: the terminal's line discipline cuts an unbroken 800 KB.
    final text = List.generate(8000, (i) => '${'x' * 98}
').join();
    final outcome = await PasteSender().send(1, text, sink);
    expect(outcome, PasteOutcome.sent);
    pty.write(Uint8List.fromList([4])); // end of file: the text ended with a newline
    await Future<void>.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 500));
    _type(pty, 'echo size-\$(wc -c < ${dir.path}/pasted.txt | tr -d " ")');
    await transcript.waitForLine((line) => line == 'size-792000');
    pty.kill();
    await pty.exitCode.timeout(const Duration(seconds: 15));
  }, skip: Platform.isWindows);

/// A pane's PTY as [PasteSender] writes to it (normal mode: no brackets).
final class _PtySink implements PasteSink {
  _PtySink(this._pty);
  final Pty _pty;
  @override
  bool get alive => true;
  @override
  bool get bracketed => false;
  @override
  void paste(String text) => _pty.write(Uint8List.fromList(utf8.encode(text)));
  @override
  void send(String data) => _pty.write(Uint8List.fromList(utf8.encode(data)));
}
```

### Task 4: Desktop — the find engine and one pane's find session (D3, D5, D34) — **M**

No UI yet: nothing becomes visible. `searchBuffer` walks xterm's `Buffer` (scrollback + screen), joins rows the terminal wrapped, maps every UTF-16 unit back to its cell, and returns non-overlapping matches (end column exclusive — verified by reading each range back with `Buffer.getText`). `FindSession` owns the query, the matches, the current one and the highlights (through `TerminalController.highlight`; the anchors are disposed with them).

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/desktop/lib/src/terminal/terminal_search.dart`, `apps/desktop/lib/src/terminal/find_session.dart`
- Test: `apps/desktop/test/terminal/terminal_search_test.dart`, `apps/desktop/test/terminal/find_session_test.dart`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: xterm `Buffer`, `BufferRangeLine`, `CellAnchor`, `TerminalController.highlight`.
- Produces:
  - `SearchMatch(startRow, startCol, endRow, endCol)` (`begin`/`end` as `CellOffset`; `toString()` shows positions only), `SearchResult(matches, {capped})`, `kMaxSearchMatches = 1000`
  - `SearchResult searchBuffer(Buffer, String needle, {bool matchCase = false, int maxMatches = 1000})`, `String foldCase(String)` (length-preserving), `int firstMatchFrom(List<SearchMatch>, int row)`
  - `FindSession({terminal, controller, matchColor, currentColor, refreshAfter = 150 ms})` with `query`, `matchCase`, `matches`, `capped`, `current` (-1 none), `hasQuery`, `focusNode`, `reveal` (callback), `setQuery`, `toggleMatchCase`, `next`, `previous`, `close()`/`dispose()`; the first current match is the **last** one; output refreshes the matches after 150 ms keeping the user's place; `kMaxFindQuery = 200`.

```dart
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:shellx_desktop/src/terminal/terminal_search.dart';
import 'package:xterm/core.dart';

Terminal terminal(String text, {int cols = 20, int rows = 5}) {
  final t = Terminal(maxLines: 1000)..resize(cols, rows);
  t.write(text);
  return t;
}

String textOf(Terminal t, SearchMatch m) =>
    t.buffer.getText(BufferRangeLine(m.begin, m.end));

void main() {
  test('finds every occurrence, case-insensitive by default', () {
    final t = terminal('Error one\r\nerror two\r\nno trouble\r\nERROR three');
    final r = searchBuffer(t.buffer, 'error');
    expect(r.matches, hasLength(3));
    expect(r.capped, isFalse);
    for (final m in r.matches) {
      expect(textOf(t, m).toLowerCase(), 'error');
    }
    expect(
      searchBuffer(t.buffer, 'error', matchCase: true).matches,
      hasLength(1),
    );
  });

  test('the end column is exclusive: the range reads back as the needle', () {
    final t = terminal('xx needle yy');
    final m = searchBuffer(t.buffer, 'needle').matches.single;
    expect((m.startRow, m.startCol, m.endRow, m.endCol), (0, 3, 0, 9));
    expect(textOf(t, m), 'needle');
  });

  test('a match can cross a wrapped row', () {
    final t = terminal('0123456789abcdefghijWRAPPED here', cols: 24);
    // 24 columns: "0123456789abcdefghijWRAP" | "PED here"
    final m = searchBuffer(t.buffer, 'wrapped').matches.single;
    expect(m.startRow, isNot(m.endRow));
    expect(textOf(t, m).replaceAll('\n', ''), 'WRAPPED');
  });

  test('scrollback is searched, not only the screen', () {
    final t = Terminal(maxLines: 1000)..resize(10, 3);
    for (var i = 0; i < 50; i++) {
      t.write('line $i\r\n');
    }
    final r = searchBuffer(t.buffer, 'line 7');
    expect(r.matches.single.startRow, lessThan(t.buffer.scrollBack));
  });

  test('wide characters and emoji map to the right cells', () {
    final t = terminal('日本語 test 😀 end');
    final jp = searchBuffer(t.buffer, 'test').matches.single;
    expect(textOf(t, jp), 'test');
    final em = searchBuffer(t.buffer, 'end').matches.single;
    expect(textOf(t, em), 'end');
    final cjk = searchBuffer(t.buffer, '本語').matches.single;
    expect(textOf(t, cjk), '本語');
  });

  test('Vietnamese folds case; the length never changes', () {
    final t = terminal('Đường đi');
    expect(searchBuffer(t.buffer, 'đường').matches, hasLength(1));
    expect(searchBuffer(t.buffer, 'ĐI').matches, hasLength(1));
    expect(foldCase('ÀÉĐİßABC').length, 8, reason: 'indices stay valid');
    expect(foldCase('ÀĐ'), 'àđ');
  });

  test('empty needle, empty buffer, no match', () {
    final t = terminal('abc');
    expect(searchBuffer(t.buffer, '').matches, isEmpty);
    expect(searchBuffer(t.buffer, 'zzz').matches, isEmpty);
    expect(searchBuffer(Terminal().buffer, 'a').matches, isEmpty);
  });

  test('matches do not overlap', () {
    final t = terminal('aaaa');
    expect(searchBuffer(t.buffer, 'aa').matches, hasLength(2));
  });

  test('stops at the cap and says so', () {
    final t = Terminal(maxLines: 5000)..resize(20, 5);
    for (var i = 0; i < 1500; i++) {
      t.write('x\r\n');
    }
    final r = searchBuffer(t.buffer, 'x');
    expect(r.matches, hasLength(kMaxSearchMatches));
    expect(r.capped, isTrue);
  });

  test('10 000 lines of 200 columns search in well under a second', () {
    final t = Terminal(maxLines: 10000)..resize(200, 40);
    final line = '${'abcdefghij' * 19}error\r\n';
    for (var i = 0; i < 10000; i++) {
      t.write(line);
    }
    final watch = Stopwatch()..start();
    final r = searchBuffer(t.buffer, 'error');
    watch.stop();
    expect(r.capped, isTrue);
    expect(watch.elapsedMilliseconds, lessThan(250)); // budget 50 ms, 5x slack
  });

  test('firstMatchFrom wraps to the first match', () {
    const ms = [SearchMatch(2, 0, 2, 1), SearchMatch(9, 0, 9, 1)];
    expect(firstMatchFrom(ms, 0), 0);
    expect(firstMatchFrom(ms, 3), 1);
    expect(firstMatchFrom(ms, 10), 0);
    expect(firstMatchFrom(const [], 0), -1);
  });

  test('toString never shows terminal text', () {
    expect(const SearchMatch(1, 2, 3, 4).toString(), isNot(contains('needle')));
  });
}
```

```dart
import 'package:fake_async/fake_async.dart';
import 'package:flutter/painting.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:shellx_desktop/src/terminal/find_session.dart';
import 'package:xterm/xterm.dart';

const _dim = Color(0x405FD4E2);
const _hot = Color(0x8C5FD4E2);

(Terminal, TerminalController, FindSession) rig(String text) {
  final t = Terminal(maxLines: 500)..resize(30, 5);
  t.write(text);
  final c = TerminalController();
  return (
    t,
    c,
    FindSession(
      terminal: t,
      controller: c,
      matchColor: _dim,
      currentColor: _hot,
    ),
  );
}

void main() {
  test(
    'typing finds matches, picks the last, paints one hot and the rest dim',
    () {
      final (_, c, s) = rig('one error\r\ntwo error\r\nthree error');
      s.setQuery('error');
      expect(s.matches, hasLength(3));
      expect(s.current, 2);
      expect(c.highlights, hasLength(3));
      expect(c.highlights.where((h) => h.color == _hot), hasLength(1));
      expect(c.highlights.where((h) => h.color == _dim), hasLength(2));
      s.close();
    },
  );

  test('next and previous wrap around and move the hot highlight', () {
    final (_, c, s) = rig('a\r\na\r\na');
    s.setQuery('a');
    expect(s.current, 2);
    s.next();
    expect(s.current, 0);
    s.previous();
    expect(s.current, 2);
    s.previous();
    expect(s.current, 1);
    expect(c.highlights.where((h) => h.color == _hot), hasLength(1));
    s.close();
  });

  test('every step reveals the match it lands on', () {
    final (_, _, s) = rig('a\r\nb\r\na');
    final seen = <int>[];
    s.reveal = (m) => seen.add(m.startRow);
    s.setQuery('a');
    s.next();
    expect(seen, [2, 0]);
    s.close();
  });

  test('no match: no highlight, current -1, steps do nothing', () {
    final (_, c, s) = rig('hello');
    s.setQuery('zzz');
    expect(s.current, -1);
    expect(c.highlights, isEmpty);
    s.next();
    s.previous();
    expect(s.current, -1);
    s.close();
  });

  test('match case narrows the matches', () {
    final (_, _, s) = rig('Error error');
    s.setQuery('error');
    expect(s.matches, hasLength(2));
    s.toggleMatchCase();
    expect(s.matches, hasLength(1));
    s.close();
  });

  test('line breaks never enter the query; it is cut at 200', () {
    final (_, _, s) = rig('x');
    s.setQuery('a\r\nb');
    expect(s.query, 'ab');
    s.setQuery('q' * 500);
    expect(s.query.length, kMaxFindQuery);
    s.close();
  });

  test('new output refreshes the matches after 150 ms, keeping the place', () {
    fakeAsync((async) {
      final (t, _, s) = rig('err 1\r\nerr 2');
      s.setQuery('err');
      s.previous(); // on match 0
      expect(s.current, 0);
      t.write('\r\nerr 3');
      expect(s.matches, hasLength(2), reason: 'not at once');
      async.elapse(const Duration(milliseconds: 151));
      expect(s.matches, hasLength(3));
      expect(s.current, 0, reason: 'the user stays where they were');
      s.close();
    });
  });

  test('close leaves no highlight and no anchor, and stops listening', () {
    fakeAsync((async) {
      final (t, c, s) = rig('a a a');
      s.setQuery('a');
      expect(c.highlights, hasLength(3));
      final lineAnchors = t.buffer.lines[0].anchors.length;
      expect(lineAnchors, greaterThan(0));
      s.close();
      expect(c.highlights, isEmpty);
      expect(t.buffer.lines[0].anchors, isEmpty);
      t.write('\r\na');
      async.elapse(const Duration(seconds: 1)); // would search if listening
      expect(c.highlights, isEmpty);
    });
  });

  test('changing the query replaces the highlights, never piling them up', () {
    final (_, c, s) = rig('ab ab ab');
    s.setQuery('ab');
    s.setQuery('b');
    expect(c.highlights, hasLength(3));
    s.setQuery('');
    expect(c.highlights, isEmpty);
    s.close();
  });

  test('toString never shows the query', () {
    final (_, _, s) = rig('x');
    s.setQuery('secret');
    expect(s.toString(), isNot(contains('secret')));
    s.close();
  });
}
```

File: `apps/desktop/lib/src/terminal/terminal_search.dart`

```dart
import 'package:xterm/core.dart';

/// One match, as buffer cells: rows are indices into `Buffer.lines`
/// (scrollback included), the end column is exclusive.
final class SearchMatch {
  const SearchMatch(this.startRow, this.startCol, this.endRow, this.endCol);
  final int startRow;
  final int startCol;
  final int endRow;
  final int endCol;

  CellOffset get begin => CellOffset(startCol, startRow);
  CellOffset get end => CellOffset(endCol, endRow);

  @override
  bool operator ==(Object other) =>
      other is SearchMatch &&
      other.startRow == startRow &&
      other.startCol == startCol &&
      other.endRow == endRow &&
      other.endCol == endCol;

  @override
  int get hashCode => Object.hash(startRow, startCol, endRow, endCol);

  /// Positions only; never the text (A10 §5).
  @override
  String toString() => 'SearchMatch($startRow:$startCol-$endRow:$endCol)';
}

final class SearchResult {
  const SearchResult(this.matches, {this.capped = false});
  final List<SearchMatch> matches;

  /// More than the cap matched; [matches] holds the first ones.
  final bool capped;
}

/// The most matches kept and highlighted (A10 D3, D5).
const int kMaxSearchMatches = 1000;

/// Lower-cases without changing the length of [s] (a character whose
/// lower case is longer or shorter stays as it is), so an index into the
/// result is an index into [s].
String foldCase(String s) {
  final out = StringBuffer();
  for (var i = 0; i < s.length; i++) {
    final c = s.codeUnitAt(i);
    if (c >= 0x41 && c <= 0x5A) {
      out.writeCharCode(c + 32);
    } else if (c < 0x80 || (c >= 0xD800 && c <= 0xDFFF)) {
      out.writeCharCode(c);
    } else {
      final lower = String.fromCharCode(c).toLowerCase();
      out.write(lower.length == 1 ? lower : s[i]);
    }
  }
  return out.toString();
}

/// Finds [needle] in the whole [buffer] (scrollback and screen), one
/// logical line at a time: rows the terminal wrapped (`isWrapped`) are
/// joined, so a match can cross a wrap. Non-overlapping, in order. An empty
/// needle matches nothing.
SearchResult searchBuffer(
  Buffer buffer,
  String needle, {
  bool matchCase = false,
  int maxMatches = kMaxSearchMatches,
}) {
  if (needle.isEmpty) return const SearchResult([]);
  final wanted = matchCase ? needle : foldCase(needle);
  final matches = <SearchMatch>[];
  final height = buffer.height;
  var row = 0;
  while (row < height) {
    // One logical line: [row] and the rows after it that wrap into it.
    var last = row;
    while (last + 1 < height && buffer.lines[last + 1].isWrapped) {
      last++;
    }
    final text = StringBuffer();
    final rows = <int>[];
    final cols = <int>[];
    final widths = <int>[];
    for (var r = row; r <= last; r++) {
      final line = buffer.lines[r];
      final cells = r < last ? line.length : line.getTrimmedLength();
      for (var c = 0; c < cells; c++) {
        final width = line.getWidth(c);
        if (width == 0) continue; // the second half of a wide character
        final code = line.getCodePoint(c);
        final unit = code == 0 ? ' ' : String.fromCharCode(code);
        for (var u = 0; u < unit.length; u++) {
          rows.add(r);
          cols.add(c);
          widths.add(width);
        }
        text.write(unit);
      }
    }
    final haystack = matchCase ? text.toString() : foldCase(text.toString());
    var from = 0;
    while (true) {
      final at = haystack.indexOf(wanted, from);
      if (at < 0) break;
      final lastUnit = at + wanted.length - 1;
      if (matches.length >= maxMatches) {
        return SearchResult(matches, capped: true);
      }
      matches.add(
        SearchMatch(
          rows[at],
          cols[at],
          rows[lastUnit],
          cols[lastUnit] + widths[lastUnit],
        ),
      );
      from = at + wanted.length;
    }
    row = last + 1;
  }
  return SearchResult(matches);
}

/// The index of the first match at or after [from] (a buffer row), wrapping
/// to the first one; -1 when there are none.
int firstMatchFrom(List<SearchMatch> matches, int from) {
  if (matches.isEmpty) return -1;
  for (var i = 0; i < matches.length; i++) {
    if (matches[i].startRow >= from) return i;
  }
  return 0;
}
```

File: `apps/desktop/lib/src/terminal/find_session.dart`

```dart
import 'dart:async';
import 'dart:ui' show Color;

import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:flutter/widgets.dart' show FocusNode;
import 'package:xterm/xterm.dart';

import 'terminal_search.dart';

/// The longest search text (A10 D3).
const int kMaxFindQuery = 200;

/// One pane's find (A10 D3–D5): the query, its matches in the pane's whole
/// buffer, the current one, and the highlights drawn for them.
final class FindSession extends ChangeNotifier {
  FindSession({
    required this.terminal,
    required this.controller,
    required this.matchColor,
    required this.currentColor,
    this.refreshAfter = const Duration(milliseconds: 150),
  }) {
    terminal.addListener(_onTerminal);
  }

  final Terminal terminal;
  final TerminalController controller;
  final Color matchColor;
  final Color currentColor;
  final Duration refreshAfter;

  /// The bar's input: focused when the bar opens or ⌘F is pressed again.
  final FocusNode focusNode = FocusNode(debugLabel: 'find');

  /// Scrolls match [m] into view; set by the pane that shows the bar.
  void Function(SearchMatch m)? reveal;

  String _query = '';
  bool _matchCase = false;
  SearchResult _result = const SearchResult([]);
  int _current = -1;
  final _highlights = <TerminalHighlight>[];
  Timer? _timer;
  bool _closed = false;

  String get query => _query;
  bool get matchCase => _matchCase;
  List<SearchMatch> get matches => _result.matches;
  bool get capped => _result.capped;

  /// Index of the current match; -1 when there is none.
  int get current => _current;
  bool get hasQuery => _query.isNotEmpty;

  /// Never shows the query (A10 §5).
  @override
  String toString() => 'FindSession(…)';

  void setQuery(String text) {
    var q = text.replaceAll(RegExp(r'[\r\n]'), '');
    if (q.length > kMaxFindQuery) q = q.substring(0, kMaxFindQuery);
    if (q == _query) return;
    _query = q;
    _search(pickLast: true, scroll: true);
  }

  void toggleMatchCase() {
    _matchCase = !_matchCase;
    _search(pickLast: true, scroll: true);
  }

  void next() => _step(1);
  void previous() => _step(-1);

  void _step(int delta) {
    final n = matches.length;
    if (n == 0) return;
    _setCurrent((_current + delta + n) % n, scroll: true);
  }

  void _onTerminal() {
    if (_closed || !hasQuery || _timer != null) return;
    _timer = Timer(refreshAfter, () {
      _timer = null;
      if (!_closed) _search(pickLast: false, scroll: false);
    });
  }

  void _search({required bool pickLast, required bool scroll}) {
    final before = _current >= 0 && _current < matches.length
        ? matches[_current]
        : null;
    _result = hasQuery
        ? searchBuffer(terminal.buffer, _query, matchCase: _matchCase)
        : const SearchResult([]);
    _clearHighlights();
    if (matches.isEmpty) {
      _current = -1;
      notifyListeners();
      return;
    }
    if (pickLast || before == null) {
      _current = matches.length - 1;
    } else {
      // Keep the match the user is on, if it is still there; else the one
      // that follows it.
      final same = matches.indexOf(before);
      _current = same >= 0 ? same : firstMatchFrom(matches, before.startRow);
    }
    _paint();
    if (scroll) reveal?.call(matches[_current]);
    notifyListeners();
  }

  void _setCurrent(int i, {required bool scroll}) {
    final old = _current;
    _current = i;
    if (old >= 0 && old < _highlights.length) _recolor(old, matchColor);
    if (i < _highlights.length) _recolor(i, currentColor);
    if (scroll) reveal?.call(matches[i]);
    notifyListeners();
  }

  TerminalHighlight _highlightFor(SearchMatch m, Color color) {
    final buffer = terminal.buffer;
    return controller.highlight(
      p1: buffer.createAnchor(m.startCol, m.startRow),
      p2: buffer.createAnchor(m.endCol, m.endRow),
      color: color,
    );
  }

  void _paint() {
    for (var i = 0; i < matches.length; i++) {
      _highlights.add(
        _highlightFor(matches[i], i == _current ? currentColor : matchColor),
      );
    }
  }

  void _recolor(int i, Color color) {
    _dispose(_highlights[i]);
    _highlights[i] = _highlightFor(matches[i], color);
  }

  void _dispose(TerminalHighlight h) {
    h.p1.dispose();
    h.p2.dispose();
    try {
      h.dispose();
    } on Object {
      // A pane that closes disposes its controller before this session
      // hears of it: the highlight is gone with it.
    }
  }

  void _clearHighlights() {
    for (final h in _highlights) {
      _dispose(h);
    }
    _highlights.clear();
  }

  /// The bar closed or its pane went: no highlight and no anchor is left.
  void close() => dispose();

  @override
  void dispose() {
    if (_closed) return;
    _closed = true;
    _timer?.cancel();
    terminal.removeListener(_onTerminal);
    _clearHighlights();
    focusNode.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }
}
```


Two points worth keeping when reviewing (both found while verifying): `FindSession.dispose` wraps `TerminalHighlight.dispose` in a `try` — a pane that closes disposes its `TerminalController` *before* this session hears of it, and notifying a disposed controller asserts in debug; and `close()` is `dispose()` (a `ChangeNotifier` override must call `super.dispose()`).

### Task 5: Desktop — Find in terminal: bar, keys, menu (D3, D4, D33) — **M · ⚠ MANUAL**

Makes Task 4 visible: ⌘F (Windows Ctrl+Shift+F) opens a bar over the top-right of the focused pane (it overlays — the grid never changes), ⌘G / ⌘⇧G step, Esc closes and returns the keyboard. Ghostty-named actions `start_search`, `navigate_search:next|previous`, `end_search` appear in the command palette, the shortcuts sheet and the config file.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/desktop/lib/src/terminal/find_sessions.dart`, `apps/desktop/lib/src/ui/terminal/find_bar.dart`
- Modify: `apps/desktop/lib/src/ui/terminal/terminal_intents.dart`, `apps/desktop/lib/src/config/keybind_actions.dart`, `apps/desktop/lib/src/config/keybindings.dart`, `apps/desktop/lib/src/ui/config/action_labels.dart`, `apps/desktop/lib/src/ui/terminal/shortcut_labels.dart`, `apps/desktop/lib/src/ui/terminal/terminal_workspace_page.dart`, `apps/desktop/lib/src/ui/terminal/pane_tree_view.dart`, `apps/desktop/lib/l10n/app_{en,vi}.arb` (+ generated)
- Test: `apps/desktop/test/ui/find_bar_test.dart`, `apps/desktop/test/ui/pane_find_test.dart`, `apps/desktop/test/ui/shortcut_golden_test.dart` (modify)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 4; `textEditingShortcuts()` (keeps ⌘V/⌘C of the window out of the bar's field), `terminalFocusRequestProvider`, `ShellxDesktopColors/Space/Radius/Text/Shadows`.
- Produces:
  - `final findSessionsProvider = NotifierProvider<FindSessions, Map<int, FindSession>>`; `FindSession? open(int paneId)` (a one-line selection ≤ 200 chars seeds the query), `void close(int paneId)`; sessions of panes that leave `terminalTabsProvider` are closed.
  - `FindBar({session, focusNode, onClose, seed})` — 360 × 36, keys `find.bar`, `find.field`, `find.count`, `find.matchCase`, `find.previous`, `find.next`, `find.close`.
  - Intents `StartSearchIntent`, `NavigateSearchIntent({next})`, `EndSearchIntent`; defaults ⌘F, ⌘G, ⌘⇧G / Ctrl+Shift+F, Ctrl+Shift+G, Ctrl+Shift+Alt+G (never a bare F3).
