```dart
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:shellx_desktop/l10n/app_localizations.dart';
import 'package:shellx_desktop/src/terminal/find_session.dart';
import 'package:shellx_desktop/src/ui/terminal/find_bar.dart';
import 'package:shellx_desktop/src/ui/theme/shellx_desktop_theme.dart';
import 'package:xterm/xterm.dart';

const _dim = Color(0x405FD4E2);
const _hot = Color(0x8C5FD4E2);

class Rig {
  Rig(String text, {Locale locale = const Locale('en')}) : _locale = locale {
    terminal = Terminal(maxLines: 500)..resize(30, 5);
    terminal.write(text);
    controller = TerminalController();
    session = FindSession(
      terminal: terminal,
      controller: controller,
      matchColor: _dim,
      currentColor: _hot,
    );
  }
  final Locale _locale;
  late final Terminal terminal;
  late final TerminalController controller;
  late final FindSession session;
  final focus = FocusNode();
  var closed = 0;

  Future<void> pump(WidgetTester t, {String? seed}) async {
    t.view.physicalSize = const Size(900, 400);
    t.view.devicePixelRatio = 1;
    addTearDown(t.view.reset);
    addTearDown(focus.dispose);
    await t.pumpWidget(
      MaterialApp(
        theme: buildDesktopTheme(),
        localizationsDelegates: AppLocalizations.localizationsDelegates,
        supportedLocales: AppLocalizations.supportedLocales,
        locale: _locale,
        home: Scaffold(
          body: Align(
            alignment: Alignment.topRight,
            child: FindBar(
              session: session,
              focusNode: focus,
              seed: seed,
              onClose: () => closed++,
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
    await t.pump();
  }
}

void main() {
  testWidgets('opens focused; typing counts matches "3 of 3"', (t) async {
    final r = Rig('one error\r\ntwo error\r\nthree error');
    await r.pump(t);
    expect(r.focus.hasFocus, isTrue);
    await t.enterText(find.byKey(const Key('find.field')), 'error');
    await t.pump();
    expect(t.widget<Text>(find.byKey(const Key('find.count'))).data, '3 of 3');
    r.session.close();
  });

  testWidgets('Vietnamese words, and no matches shown in red', (t) async {
    final r = Rig('x', locale: const Locale('vi'));
    await r.pump(t);
    await t.enterText(find.byKey(const Key('find.field')), 'zzz');
    await t.pump();
    final count = t.widget<Text>(find.byKey(const Key('find.count')));
    expect(count.data, 'Không có kết quả');
    expect(count.style!.color, ShellxDesktopColors.err);
    r.session.close();
  });

  testWidgets('Enter is next, Shift+Enter previous, Esc closes', (t) async {
    final r = Rig('a\r\na\r\na');
    await r.pump(t);
    await t.enterText(find.byKey(const Key('find.field')), 'a');
    await t.pump();
    expect(r.session.current, 2);
    await t.sendKeyEvent(LogicalKeyboardKey.enter);
    await t.pump();
    expect(r.session.current, 0);
    await t.sendKeyDownEvent(LogicalKeyboardKey.shiftLeft);
    await t.sendKeyEvent(LogicalKeyboardKey.enter);
    await t.sendKeyUpEvent(LogicalKeyboardKey.shiftLeft);
    await t.pump();
    expect(r.session.current, 2);
    await t.sendKeyEvent(LogicalKeyboardKey.arrowUp);
    await t.pump();
    expect(r.session.current, 1);
    await t.sendKeyEvent(LogicalKeyboardKey.escape);
    expect(r.closed, 1);
    r.session.close();
  });

  testWidgets('a selected line seeds the field', (t) async {
    final r = Rig('find me here');
    await r.pump(t, seed: 'me here');
    expect(
      t.widget<TextField>(find.byKey(const Key('find.field'))).controller!.text,
      'me here',
    );
    expect(r.session.matches, hasLength(1));
    r.session.close();
  });

  testWidgets('previous/next are disabled without matches; Aa toggles', (
    t,
  ) async {
    final r = Rig('Err err');
    await r.pump(t);
    IconButton button(String key) => t.widget<IconButton>(
      find.descendant(
        of: find.byKey(Key(key)),
        matching: find.byType(IconButton),
      ),
    );
    expect(button('find.next').onPressed, isNull);
    await t.enterText(find.byKey(const Key('find.field')), 'err');
    await t.pump();
    expect(button('find.next').onPressed, isNotNull);
    expect(r.session.matches, hasLength(2));
    await t.tap(find.byKey(const Key('find.matchCase')));
    await t.pump();
    expect(r.session.matches, hasLength(1));
    await t.tap(find.byKey(const Key('find.close')));
    expect(r.closed, 1);
    r.session.close();
  });

  testWidgets('360 × 36', (t) async {
    final r = Rig('x');
    await r.pump(t);
    expect(t.getSize(find.byKey(const Key('find.bar'))), const Size(360, 36));
    r.session.close();
  });
}

```dart
import 'package:flutter/gestures.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:shellx_desktop/src/terminal/find_sessions.dart';
import 'package:shellx_desktop/src/terminal/terminal_tabs.dart';

import '../support/config_workspace.dart';

Future<void> pressMeta(WidgetTester t, LogicalKeyboardKey key) async {
  await t.sendKeyDownEvent(LogicalKeyboardKey.metaLeft);
  await t.sendKeyEvent(key);
  await t.sendKeyUpEvent(LogicalKeyboardKey.metaLeft);
  await t.pump();
}

void main() {
  testWidgets(
    '⌘F opens the bar in the focused pane, focused, grid unchanged',
    (t) async {
      final rig = await pumpConfiguredWorkspace(t);
      final c = rig.container;
      final tab = c.read(terminalTabsProvider).selected!;
      tab.terminal.write('alpha\r\nbeta error\r\ngamma error');
      final before = (tab.terminal.viewWidth, tab.terminal.viewHeight);
      final resizes = rig.local.views[tab.focusedPaneId]!.resizes.length;
      await pressMeta(t, LogicalKeyboardKey.keyF);
      await t.pump();
      expect(find.byKey(const Key('find.bar')), findsOneWidget);
      expect(
        c.read(findSessionsProvider)[tab.focusedPaneId]!.focusNode.hasFocus,
        isTrue,
      );
      expect((tab.terminal.viewWidth, tab.terminal.viewHeight), before);
      expect(rig.local.views[tab.focusedPaneId]!.resizes.length, resizes);
      await disposeWorkspace(t, c);
    },
    variant: TargetPlatformVariant.only(TargetPlatform.macOS),
  );

  testWidgets(
    'typing finds; ⌘G steps; Esc closes and the terminal has the keyboard',
    (t) async {
      final rig = await pumpConfiguredWorkspace(t);
      final c = rig.container;
      final tab = c.read(terminalTabsProvider).selected!;
      tab.terminal.write('one error\r\ntwo error\r\nthree error');
      await pressMeta(t, LogicalKeyboardKey.keyF);
      await t.enterText(find.byKey(const Key('find.field')), 'error');
      await t.pump();
      final session = c.read(findSessionsProvider)[tab.focusedPaneId]!;
      expect(session.matches, hasLength(3));
      expect(session.current, 2);
      await pressMeta(t, LogicalKeyboardKey.keyG);
      expect(session.current, 0);
      await t.sendKeyEvent(LogicalKeyboardKey.escape);
      await t.pumpAndSettle();
      expect(find.byKey(const Key('find.bar')), findsNothing);
      expect(c.read(findSessionsProvider), isEmpty);
      expect(tab.selection.highlights, isEmpty);
      // The keyboard is back in the terminal (A10 D23 F2).
      expect(FocusManager.instance.primaryFocus?.debugLabel, 'terminal');
      await disposeWorkspace(t, c);
    },
    variant: TargetPlatformVariant.only(TargetPlatform.macOS),
  );

  testWidgets(
    '⌘V in the bar pastes into the bar, not the terminal',
    (t) async {
      final rig = await pumpConfiguredWorkspace(t);
      final c = rig.container;
      final tab = c.read(terminalTabsProvider).selected!;
      t.binding.defaultBinaryMessenger.setMockMethodCallHandler(
        SystemChannels.platform,
        (call) async => call.method == 'Clipboard.getData'
            ? <String, Object?>{'text': 'needle'}
            : null,
      );
      addTearDown(
        () => t.binding.defaultBinaryMessenger.setMockMethodCallHandler(
          SystemChannels.platform,
          null,
        ),
      );
      await pressMeta(t, LogicalKeyboardKey.keyF);
      await pressMeta(t, LogicalKeyboardKey.keyV);
      await t.pump();
      expect(rig.local.views[tab.focusedPaneId]!.writes, isEmpty);
      expect(
        t
            .widget<TextField>(find.byKey(const Key('find.field')))
            .controller!
            .text,
        'needle',
      );
      await disposeWorkspace(t, c);
    },
    variant: TargetPlatformVariant.only(TargetPlatform.macOS),
  );

  testWidgets(
    'closing the pane closes its bar; no anchors stay',
    (t) async {
      final rig = await pumpConfiguredWorkspace(t);
      final c = rig.container;
      final tab = c.read(terminalTabsProvider).selected!;
      tab.terminal.write('x x x');
      await pressMeta(t, LogicalKeyboardKey.keyF);
      await t.enterText(find.byKey(const Key('find.field')), 'x');
      await t.pump();
      final terminal = tab.terminal;
      expect(terminal.buffer.lines[0].anchors, isNotEmpty);
      c.read(terminalTabsProvider.notifier).close(tab.id);
      await t.pump();
      expect(c.read(findSessionsProvider), isEmpty);
      expect(terminal.buffer.lines[0].anchors, isEmpty);
      await disposeWorkspace(t, c);
    },
    variant: TargetPlatformVariant.only(TargetPlatform.macOS),
  );

  testWidgets(
    'the menu has Find… with its shortcut',
    (t) async {
      final rig = await pumpConfiguredWorkspace(t);
      final c = rig.container;
      final tab = c.read(terminalTabsProvider).selected!;
      // Right click on the terminal.
      await t.tapAt(
        t.getCenter(find.byKey(ValueKey('terminal.${tab.id}'))),
        buttons: kSecondaryButton,
      );
      await t.pumpAndSettle();
      expect(find.byKey(const Key('menu.find')), findsOneWidget);
      expect(find.text('Find…'), findsOneWidget);
      expect(find.text('⌘F'), findsOneWidget);
      await t.tapAt(const Offset(2, 2));
      await t.pumpAndSettle();
      await disposeWorkspace(t, c);
    },
    variant: TargetPlatformVariant.only(TargetPlatform.macOS),
  );
}

/// A10 D4: ⌘F — opens (or refocuses) the find bar of the focused pane.
class StartSearchIntent extends Intent {
  const StartSearchIntent();
}

/// A10 D4: ⌘G / ⌘⇧G — the next / previous match of the focused pane's bar.
class NavigateSearchIntent extends Intent {
  const NavigateSearchIntent({required this.next});
  final bool next;
}

/// A10 D4: closes the focused pane's find bar.
class EndSearchIntent extends Intent {
  const EndSearchIntent();
}

- [ ] **Step 5: The provider and the bar**


File: `apps/desktop/lib/src/terminal/find_sessions.dart`

```dart
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';

import '../ui/theme/shellx_desktop_theme.dart' show ShellxDesktopColors;
import 'find_session.dart';
import 'terminal_tabs.dart';

/// A10 D3: the panes that have a find bar open, by session id. At most one
/// bar per pane; a pane that goes away takes its bar (highlights and
/// anchors included) with it.
final findSessionsProvider =
    NotifierProvider<FindSessions, Map<int, FindSession>>(FindSessions.new);

class FindSessions extends Notifier<Map<int, FindSession>> {
  final _all = <int, FindSession>{};

  @override
  Map<int, FindSession> build() {
    ref.onDispose(() {
      for (final s in _all.values) {
        s.close();
      }
      _all.clear();
    });
    ref.listen<TerminalTabsState>(terminalTabsProvider, (_, next) {
      final live = {for (final t in next.tabs) ...t.panes.keys};
      final gone = [
        for (final id in _all.keys)
          if (!live.contains(id)) id,
      ];
      for (final id in gone) {
        close(id);
      }
    });
    return const {};
  }

  /// Opens pane [id]'s bar, or returns the open one. A one-line selection
  /// (at most [kMaxFindQuery] characters) is the starting query.
  FindSession? open(int id) {
    final existing = _all[id];
    if (existing != null) return existing;
    final pane = ref.read(terminalTabsProvider).tabOfSession(id)?.panes[id];
    if (pane == null) return null;
    final session = FindSession(
      terminal: pane.terminal,
      controller: pane.selection,
      matchColor: ShellxDesktopColors.accent.withValues(alpha: 0.25),
      currentColor: ShellxDesktopColors.accent.withValues(alpha: 0.55),
    );
    final range = pane.selection.selection;
    if (range != null) {
      final text = pane.terminal.buffer.getText(range);
      if (text.isNotEmpty &&
          !text.contains('\n') &&
          text.length <= kMaxFindQuery) {
        session.setQuery(text);
      }
    }
    _all[id] = session;
    state = Map.of(_all);
    return session;
  }

  void close(int id) {
    final session = _all.remove(id);
    if (session == null) return;
    session.close();
    if (ref.mounted) state = Map.of(_all);
  }
}
```

File: `apps/desktop/lib/src/ui/terminal/find_bar.dart`

```dart
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';

import '../../../l10n/app_localizations.dart';
import '../../terminal/find_session.dart';
import '../text_editing_shortcuts.dart';
import '../theme/shellx_desktop_theme.dart';

/// A10 D3: the find bar of one pane, over the top-right corner of its
/// terminal. It overlays: the terminal's grid never changes. Enter / ↓ the
/// next match, Shift+Enter / ↑ the previous, Esc closes ([onClose] gives
/// the keyboard back to the terminal).
class FindBar extends StatefulWidget {
  const FindBar({
    super.key,
    required this.session,
    required this.focusNode,
    required this.onClose,
    this.seed,
  });

  final FindSession session;

  /// The input's node: focused when the bar opens or ⌘F is pressed again.
  final FocusNode focusNode;
  final VoidCallback onClose;

  /// Text to start from (one selected line, A10 D3).
  final String? seed;

  static const double width = 360;
  static const double height = 36;

  @override
  State<FindBar> createState() => _FindBarState();
}

class _FindBarState extends State<FindBar> {
  late final TextEditingController _text = TextEditingController(
    text: widget.session.query.isEmpty
        ? widget.seed ?? ''
        : widget.session.query,
  );

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    widget.session.addListener(_changed);
    if (_text.text != widget.session.query) {
      widget.session.setQuery(_text.text);
    }
    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      widget.focusNode.requestFocus();
      _text.selection = TextSelection(
        baseOffset: 0,
        extentOffset: _text.text.length,
      );
    });
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    widget.session.removeListener(_changed);
    _text.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _changed() {
    if (mounted) setState(() {});
  }

  KeyEventResult _onKey(FocusNode node, KeyEvent event) {
    if (event is! KeyDownEvent && event is! KeyRepeatEvent) {
      return KeyEventResult.ignored;
    }
    final key = event.logicalKey;
    if (key == LogicalKeyboardKey.escape) {
      widget.onClose();
      return KeyEventResult.handled;
    }
    if (key == LogicalKeyboardKey.enter ||
        key == LogicalKeyboardKey.numpadEnter) {
      HardwareKeyboard.instance.isShiftPressed
          ? widget.session.previous()
          : widget.session.next();
      return KeyEventResult.handled;
    }
    if (key == LogicalKeyboardKey.arrowDown) {
      widget.session.next();
      return KeyEventResult.handled;
    }
    if (key == LogicalKeyboardKey.arrowUp) {
      widget.session.previous();
      return KeyEventResult.handled;
    }
    return KeyEventResult.ignored;
  }

  String _count(AppLocalizations l10n) {
    final s = widget.session;
    if (!s.hasQuery) return '';
    if (s.matches.isEmpty) return l10n.findNoMatches;
    return s.capped
        ? l10n.findCountCapped(s.current + 1, s.matches.length)
        : l10n.findCount(s.current + 1, s.matches.length);
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final l10n = AppLocalizations.of(context);
    final s = widget.session;
    final none = s.hasQuery && s.matches.isEmpty;
    final count = _count(l10n);
    return Semantics(
      container: true,
      label: l10n.findLabel,
      child: Container(
        key: const Key('find.bar'),
        width: FindBar.width,
        height: FindBar.height,
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: ShellxSpace.s8),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: ShellxDesktopColors.surface,
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(ShellxRadius.r8),
          border: Border.all(color: ShellxDesktopColors.border),
          boxShadow: ShellxShadows.e1,
        ),
        child: Row(
          children: [
            Expanded(
              child: Focus(
                canRequestFocus: false,
                skipTraversal: true,
                onKeyEvent: _onKey,
                child: Shortcuts(
                  // The window's paste and copy must not take these keys.
                  shortcuts: textEditingShortcuts(),
                  child: TextField(
                    key: const Key('find.field'),
                    controller: _text,
                    focusNode: widget.focusNode,
                    style: ShellxText.body.copyWith(
                      color: ShellxDesktopColors.textTitle,
                    ),
                    cursorColor: ShellxDesktopColors.accent,
                    maxLines: 1,
                    maxLength: kMaxFindQuery,
                    onChanged: s.setQuery,
                    decoration: InputDecoration(
                      isDense: true,
                      counterText: '',
                      border: InputBorder.none,
                      hintText: l10n.findPlaceholder,
                      hintStyle: ShellxText.body.copyWith(
                        color: ShellxDesktopColors.textPlaceholder,
                      ),
                      semanticCounterText: '',
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),
            // Read aloud when it changes ("3 of 17").
            Semantics(
              liveRegion: true,
              child: Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: ShellxSpace.s4),
                child: Text(
                  count,
                  key: const Key('find.count'),
                  style: ShellxText.caption.copyWith(
                    color: none
                        ? ShellxDesktopColors.err
                        : ShellxDesktopColors.textSecondary,
                    fontFeatures: const [FontFeature.tabularFigures()],
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),
            _Toggle(
              key: const Key('find.matchCase'),
              label: 'Aa',
              tooltip: l10n.findMatchCase,
              on: s.matchCase,
              onPressed: s.toggleMatchCase,
            ),
            _IconButton(
              key: const Key('find.previous'),
              icon: Icons.keyboard_arrow_up,
              tooltip: l10n.findPrevious,
              onPressed: s.matches.isEmpty ? null : s.previous,
            ),
            _IconButton(
              key: const Key('find.next'),
              icon: Icons.keyboard_arrow_down,
              tooltip: l10n.findNext,
              onPressed: s.matches.isEmpty ? null : s.next,
            ),
            _IconButton(
              key: const Key('find.close'),
              icon: Icons.close,
              tooltip: l10n.findClose,
              onPressed: widget.onClose,
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _IconButton extends StatelessWidget {
  const _IconButton({
    super.key,
    required this.icon,
    required this.tooltip,
    required this.onPressed,
  });
  final IconData icon;
  final String tooltip;
  final VoidCallback? onPressed;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => SizedBox(
    width: 24,
    height: 24,
    child: IconButton(
      padding: EdgeInsets.zero,
      iconSize: 16,
      tooltip: tooltip,
      onPressed: onPressed,
      color: ShellxDesktopColors.textSecondary,
      disabledColor: ShellxDesktopColors.textSecondary.withValues(
        alpha: ShellxDesktopColors.disabledOpacity,
      ),
      icon: Icon(icon),
    ),
  );
}

class _Toggle extends StatelessWidget {
  const _Toggle({
    super.key,
    required this.label,
    required this.tooltip,
    required this.on,
    required this.onPressed,
  });
  final String label;
  final String tooltip;
  final bool on;
  final VoidCallback onPressed;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => Tooltip(
    message: tooltip,
    child: Semantics(
      button: true,
      toggled: on,
      label: tooltip,
      excludeSemantics: true,
      child: InkWell(
        onTap: onPressed,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(ShellxRadius.r4),
        child: Container(
          width: 24,
          height: 24,
          alignment: Alignment.center,
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            color: on ? ShellxDesktopColors.primarySoft : null,
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(ShellxRadius.r4),
          ),
          child: Text(
            label,
            style: ShellxText.caption.copyWith(
              color: on
                  ? ShellxDesktopColors.accent
                  : ShellxDesktopColors.textSecondary,
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    ),
  );
}
```

  /// A10 D4: the find bar of the selected tab's focused pane, if open.
  FindSession? _focusedFind() {

    return tab == null

Seven edits:

1. In `_PaneTreeViewState`, after `_keyFor`: a view key per pane, the reveal, the close.

```dart
  /// A10 D5: each pane's TerminalView state, to find where a row is drawn.
  final _viewKeys = <int, GlobalKey<TerminalViewState>>{};

  GlobalKey<TerminalViewState> _viewKeyFor(int id) =>
      _viewKeys.putIfAbsent(id, GlobalKey<TerminalViewState>.new);

  /// A10 D5: brings match [m] to the middle of pane [id]'s view unless it
  /// is already in it. Reads the render object's own row offsets, so it
  /// assumes nothing about the scroll controller's units.
  void _reveal(int id, SearchMatch m) {
    final render = _viewKeys[id]?.currentState?.renderTerminal;
    final scroll = widget.scrollFor(id);
    if (render == null || !scroll.hasClients) return;
    final y = render.getOffset(CellOffset(m.startCol, m.startRow)).dy;
    final line = render.lineHeight;
    final view = scroll.position.viewportDimension;
    if (y >= 0 && y + line <= view) return;
    final target = scroll.position.pixels + y - (view - line) / 2;
    scroll.jumpTo(
      target.clamp(
        scroll.position.minScrollExtent,
        scroll.position.maxScrollExtent,
      ),
    );
  }

  void _closeFind(int id) {
    ref.read(findSessionsProvider.notifier).close(id);
    // The keyboard goes back to the terminal (A10 D23 F2).
    ref.read(terminalFocusRequestProvider.notifier).request();
  }
```

### Task 6: Desktop — link finding under a cell: OSC 8, logical lines, paths (D7, D9, D10, D11) — **M**

The engine behind Task 7; nothing becomes visible. `DesktopTerminal.onPrivateOSC` (nobody set it) records OSC 8 hyperlinks as cell anchors; `logicalLineAt` turns the rows around a cell into one string with a unit-to-cell map; `findLinkAt` answers "which link is under this cell" (an OSC 8 link first, else a URL or — for a local pane — a path); `LinkResolver` checks that a path exists and makes it absolute.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/desktop/lib/src/terminal/{hyperlink_registry,terminal_buffer_text,link_finder,link_resolver}.dart`
- Modify: `apps/desktop/lib/src/terminal/desktop_terminal.dart`
- Test: `apps/desktop/test/terminal/{hyperlink_registry,link_finder,link_resolver}_test.dart`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 1 (`webUriOf`, `isSafeHyperlinkUri`, `hyperlinkTextMatches`, `linkAt`), xterm `CellAnchor`, `Terminal.onPrivateOSC`, `package:path`.
- Produces:
  - `HyperlinkRange(begin, end, uri)` (`contains(row, col)`, `toString() == 'HyperlinkRange(…)'`), `HyperlinkRegistry({maxRanges = 2000}).handle(Buffer, List<String> args)`, `.at(row, col)`, `.length`, `.dispose()`; `DesktopTerminal.hyperlinks`.
  - `LogicalLine` (`text`, `firstRow`, `indexOfCell(row, col)`, `beginOf(i)`, `endAfter(i)`), `LogicalLine logicalLineAt(Buffer, int row, {maxRows = 8})`.
  - `enum LinkSource { detected, hyperlink }`, `LinkHit` (`source`, `kind`, `target`, `begin`, `end`, `needsConfirm`, `visible`, `isWeb`; `toString()` hides the target), `LinkHit? findLinkAt(DesktopTerminal, CellOffset, {required bool paths})`.
  - `ResolvedPath(path, isDirectory)`, `LinkResolver({home, windows, typeOf, now, ttl = 2 s, maxEntries = 64}).absoluteOf(target, {cwd})` / `.resolve(target, {cwd})`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**


File: `apps/desktop/test/terminal/hyperlink_registry_test.dart`

```dart
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:xterm/core.dart' show BufferRangeLine;
import 'package:shellx_desktop/src/terminal/desktop_terminal.dart';
import 'package:shellx_desktop/src/terminal/terminal_buffer_text.dart';

const _open = '\x1b]8;;';
const _st = '\x1b\\';

DesktopTerminal term({int cols = 40, int rows = 5}) =>
    DesktopTerminal(maxLines: 200)..resize(cols, rows);

void main() {
  test('text between open and close points at the address', () {
    final t = term();
    t.write(
      'go ${_open}https://example.com/a$_st'
      'here$_open$_st now',
    );
    final link = t.hyperlinks.at(0, 4)!;
    expect(link.uri, 'https://example.com/a');
    expect(t.hyperlinks.at(0, 2), isNull, reason: 'before the link');
    expect(t.hyperlinks.at(0, 3), isNotNull, reason: 'the first cell');
    expect(t.hyperlinks.at(0, 6), isNotNull, reason: 'the last cell');
    expect(t.hyperlinks.at(0, 7), isNull, reason: 'the end is exclusive');
    expect(t.hyperlinks.at(0, 20), isNull);
  });

  test('a URI that holds a semicolon arrives whole', () {
    final t = term();
    t.write(
      '${_open}https://example.com/a;b=1$_st'
      'x$_open$_st',
    );
    expect(t.hyperlinks.at(0, 0)!.uri, 'https://example.com/a;b=1');
  });

  test('params such as id= do not matter', () {
    final t = term();
    t.write(
      '\x1b]8;id=7;https://example.com$_st'
      'x$_open$_st',
    );
    expect(t.hyperlinks.at(0, 0)!.uri, 'https://example.com');
  });

  test('anything but http and https is never recorded (A10 D10)', () {
    final t = term();
    for (final uri in [
      'javascript:alert(1)',
      'file:///etc/passwd',
      'ssh://h',
      'https://a.b/\x01',
    ]) {
      t.write('$_open$uri${_st}x$_open$_st');
    }
    expect(t.hyperlinks.length, 0);
  });

  test('a link split over a wrapped row is one link', () {
    final t = term(cols: 10);
    t.write(
      '${_open}https://example.com$_st'
      '0123456789ABCDEF$_open$_st',
    );
    expect(t.hyperlinks.at(0, 5), isNotNull);
    expect(t.hyperlinks.at(1, 5), isNotNull);
    expect(t.hyperlinks.at(1, 5)!.uri, t.hyperlinks.at(0, 5)!.uri);
  });

  test('an unclosed link makes no range; a new open replaces it', () {
    final t = term();
    t.write(
      '${_open}https://a.b/1${_st}one ${_open}https://a.b/2$_st'
      'two$_open$_st',
    );
    expect(t.hyperlinks.length, 1);
    expect(t.hyperlinks.at(0, 5)!.uri, 'https://a.b/2');
  });

  test('2 000 ranges at most; ranges that left the scrollback go', () {
    final t = DesktopTerminal(maxLines: 50)..resize(20, 3);
    for (var i = 0; i < 2200; i++) {
      t.write('${_open}https://a.b/$i${_st}x$_open$_st\r\n');
    }
    expect(
      t.hyperlinks.length,
      lessThanOrEqualTo(60),
      reason: 'only lines still in the buffer keep a link',
    );
  });

  test('the visible text of a range reads back for the confirmation', () {
    final t = term();
    t.write(
      '${_open}https://evil.example$_st'
      'https://paypal.com$_open$_st',
    );
    final link = t.hyperlinks.at(0, 1)!;
    final shown = t.buffer.getText(
      BufferRangeLine(link.begin.offset, link.end.offset),
    );
    expect(shown, 'https://paypal.com');
  });

  group('logicalLineAt (A10 D9)', () {
    test('joins wrapped rows and maps units to cells', () {
      final t = term(cols: 10);
      t.write('see https://example.com/path now');
      final line = logicalLineAt(t.buffer, 1);
      expect(line.text, 'see https://example.com/path now');
      expect(line.firstRow, 0);
      final at = line.text.indexOf('example');
      expect(line.beginOf(at), isNot(line.beginOf(0)));
      expect(line.indexOfCell(1, 2), 12);
      expect(line.indexOfCell(3, 9), isNull, reason: 'past the text');
    });

    test('a wide character is one index for both of its cells', () {
      final t = term();
      t.write('日本 x');
      final line = logicalLineAt(t.buffer, 0);
      expect(line.text, '日本 x');
      expect(line.indexOfCell(0, 0), 0);
      expect(line.indexOfCell(0, 1), 0);
      expect(line.indexOfCell(0, 2), 1);
      expect(line.endAfter(0).x, 2);
    });

    test('never reads more than 8 rows', () {
      final t = term(cols: 5, rows: 20);
      t.write('x' * 100);
      expect(logicalLineAt(t.buffer, 10).text.length, lessThanOrEqualTo(8 * 5));
    });

    test('a row past the buffer is empty', () {
      expect(logicalLineAt(term().buffer, 99).isEmpty, isTrue);
    });
  });

  test('toString never shows the address', () {
    final t = term();
    t.write(
      '${_open}https://secret.example$_st'
      'x$_open$_st',
    );
    expect(t.hyperlinks.at(0, 0).toString(), isNot(contains('secret')));
  });
}
```

File: `apps/desktop/test/terminal/link_finder_test.dart`

```dart
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:shellx_core/shellx_core.dart';
import 'package:shellx_desktop/src/terminal/desktop_terminal.dart';
import 'package:shellx_desktop/src/terminal/link_finder.dart';
import 'package:xterm/core.dart' show CellOffset;

DesktopTerminal term(String text, {int cols = 40}) =>
    DesktopTerminal(maxLines: 100)
      ..resize(cols, 6)
      ..write(text);

void main() {
  test('a URL in the text; the last character is part, the dot is not', () {
    final t = term('see https://example.com/a?b=1.');
    final hit = findLinkAt(t, const CellOffset(10, 0), paths: false)!;
    expect(hit.target, 'https://example.com/a?b=1');
    expect(hit.source, LinkSource.detected);
    expect(hit.begin, const CellOffset(4, 0));
    expect(hit.end, const CellOffset(29, 0));
    expect(findLinkAt(t, const CellOffset(29, 0), paths: false), isNull);
    expect(findLinkAt(t, const CellOffset(3, 0), paths: false), isNull);
  });

  test('a URL wrapped over rows is one link on every row', () {
    final t = term('https://example.com/a/very/long/path/that/wraps', cols: 20);
    final a = findLinkAt(t, const CellOffset(3, 0), paths: false)!;
    final b = findLinkAt(t, const CellOffset(3, 2), paths: false)!;
    expect(a.target, b.target);
    expect(a.begin, const CellOffset(0, 0));
    expect(b.end.y, 2);
  });

  test('a path is a link only when paths are asked for', () {
    final t = term('open /usr/local/bin/dart:12 now');
    expect(findLinkAt(t, const CellOffset(8, 0), paths: false), isNull);
    final hit = findLinkAt(t, const CellOffset(8, 0), paths: true)!;
    expect(hit.kind, TerminalLinkKind.path);
    expect(hit.target, '/usr/local/bin/dart');
  });

  test(
    'OSC 8: the address, the shown text, and a confirmation when they differ',
    () {
      final t = term(
        '\x1b]8;;https://example.com\x1b\\bấm vào đây\x1b]8;;\x1b\\ x',
      );
      final hit = findLinkAt(t, const CellOffset(2, 0), paths: false)!;
      expect(hit.source, LinkSource.hyperlink);
      expect(hit.target, 'https://example.com');
      expect(hit.visible, 'bấm vào đây');
      expect(hit.needsConfirm, isTrue);
    },
  );

  test('OSC 8 whose text is its own address needs no confirmation', () {
    final t = term(
      '\x1b]8;;https://example.com/a\x1b\\https://example.com/a\x1b]8;;\x1b\\',
    );
    expect(
      findLinkAt(t, const CellOffset(3, 0), paths: false)!.needsConfirm,
      isFalse,
    );
  });

  test('a cell past the text, or past the buffer, has none', () {
    final t = term('https://a.b');
    expect(findLinkAt(t, const CellOffset(30, 0), paths: true), isNull);
    expect(findLinkAt(t, const CellOffset(0, 500), paths: true), isNull);
    expect(findLinkAt(t, const CellOffset(0, -1), paths: true), isNull);
  });

  test('finding a link takes well under a millisecond-ish (5x slack)', () {
    final t = term('x ' * 100 + 'https://example.com/path');
    final w = Stopwatch()..start();
    for (var i = 0; i < 200; i++) {
      findLinkAt(t, const CellOffset(5, 0), paths: true);
    }
    expect(w.elapsedMicroseconds / 200, lessThan(5000));
  });

  test('toString never shows the target', () {
    final t = term('https://secret.example');
    expect(
      findLinkAt(t, const CellOffset(1, 0), paths: false).toString(),
      isNot(contains('secret')),
    );
  });
}

```dart
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:shellx_desktop/src/terminal/link_resolver.dart';

void main() {
  group('absoluteOf (A10 D7)', () {
    final r = LinkResolver(home: '/Users/me', windows: false);

    test('home, absolute, relative to the pane folder', () {
      expect(r.absoluteOf('~/a.txt'), '/Users/me/a.txt');
      expect(r.absoluteOf('/etc/hosts'), '/etc/hosts');
      expect(
        r.absoluteOf('src/a.dart', cwd: '/work/app'),
        '/work/app/src/a.dart',
      );
      expect(r.absoluteOf('../x.md', cwd: '/work/app'), '/work/x.md');
      expect(r.absoluteOf('./x', cwd: '/work/app'), '/work/app/x');
    });

    test('no folder known: a relative path has no answer', () {
      expect(r.absoluteOf('src/a.dart'), isNull);
      expect(r.absoluteOf('src/a.dart', cwd: 'relative/cwd'), isNull);
    });

    test('control characters and an empty path have no answer', () {
      expect(r.absoluteOf('/tmp/a\nb'), isNull);
      expect(r.absoluteOf('/tmp/a\x00'), isNull);
      expect(r.absoluteOf(''), isNull);
    });

    test('the result is never something `open` could take for an option', () {
      for (final t in ['-rf', '--help', '-a', '../-x']) {
        final a = r.absoluteOf(t, cwd: '/work');
        expect(a == null || a.startsWith('/'), isTrue, reason: t);
      }
    });

    test('no home: ~ has no answer', () {
      expect(
        LinkResolver(home: null, windows: false).absoluteOf('~/a'),
        isNull,
      );
    });

    test('Windows paths', () {
      final w = LinkResolver(home: r'C:\Users\me', windows: true);
      expect(w.absoluteOf(r'C:\a\..\b.txt'), r'C:\b.txt');
      expect(
        w.absoluteOf(r'src\a.dart', cwd: r'C:\work'),
        r'C:\work\src\a.dart',
      );
    });
  });

  group('resolve against the real file system', () {
    late Directory dir;
    setUp(() async => dir = await Directory.systemTemp.createTemp('links'));
    tearDown(() => dir.delete(recursive: true));

    test('a file, a folder, a missing one, a link', () async {
      File('${dir.path}/a.txt').writeAsStringSync('x');
      Directory('${dir.path}/sub').createSync();
      Link('${dir.path}/ln').createSync('${dir.path}/a.txt');
      final r = LinkResolver(home: dir.path, windows: false);
      final file = await r.resolve('a.txt', cwd: dir.path);
      expect(file!.isDirectory, isFalse);
      expect((await r.resolve('sub', cwd: dir.path))!.isDirectory, isTrue);
      expect(await r.resolve('nope.txt', cwd: dir.path), isNull);
      expect(await r.resolve('ln', cwd: dir.path), isNotNull);
    }, testOn: '!windows');
  });

  test('answers are remembered for two seconds', () async {
    var asked = 0;
    var now = DateTime(2026, 10, 7, 9);
    final r = LinkResolver(
      home: '/h',
      windows: false,
      now: () => now,
      typeOf: (_) async {
        asked++;
        return FileSystemEntityType.file;
      },
    );
    await r.resolve('/a');
    await r.resolve('/a');
    expect(asked, 1);
    now = now.add(const Duration(seconds: 3));
    await r.resolve('/a');
    expect(asked, 2);
  });

  test('a file system error means not there', () async {
    final r = LinkResolver(
      home: '/h',
      windows: false,
      typeOf: (_) async => throw const FileSystemException('denied'),
    );
    expect(await r.resolve('/a'), isNull);
  });

  test('toString never shows the path', () async {
    final r = LinkResolver(
      home: '/h',
      windows: false,
      typeOf: (_) async => FileSystemEntityType.file,
    );
    expect(
      (await r.resolve('/secret/a'))!.toString(),
      isNot(contains('secret')),
    );
  });
}

```dart
import 'package:shellx_core/shellx_core.dart' show isSafeHyperlinkUri;
import 'package:xterm/core.dart';

/// One OSC 8 link: the cells between [begin] and [end] (exclusive) point
/// at [uri]. The anchors follow the text as it scrolls.
final class HyperlinkRange {
  HyperlinkRange(this.begin, this.end, this.uri);
  final CellAnchor begin;
  final CellAnchor end;
  final String uri;

  bool get attached => begin.attached && end.attached;

  bool contains(int row, int col) {
    if (!attached) return false;
    final b = begin.offset;
    final e = end.offset;
    final afterBegin = row > b.y || (row == b.y && col >= b.x);
    final beforeEnd = row < e.y || (row == e.y && col < e.x);
    return afterBegin && beforeEnd;
  }

  /// Never shows the address (A10 §5).
  @override
  String toString() => 'HyperlinkRange(…)';
}

/// A10 D11: remembers where the program put OSC 8 hyperlinks. xterm 4.0.0
/// hands the sequence over (`onPrivateOSC`) but stores nothing per cell, so
/// the open and close are noted as anchors at the cursor.
final class HyperlinkRegistry {
  HyperlinkRegistry({this.maxRanges = 2000});

  final int maxRanges;
  final _ranges = <HyperlinkRange>[];
  CellAnchor? _openAt;
  String? _openUri;

  int get length => _ranges.length;

  /// OSC 8 as xterm passes it: [args] is `params`, then the URI split at
  /// every `;` (a URI may hold one).
  void handle(Buffer buffer, List<String> args) {
    if (args.length < 2) return;
    final uri = args.sublist(1).join(';');
    if (uri.isEmpty) {
      _close(buffer);
    } else {
      _open(buffer, uri);
    }
  }

  void _open(Buffer buffer, String uri) {
    _openAt?.dispose();
    _openAt = null;
    _openUri = null;
    // A10 D10: nothing but http/https is ever recorded.
    if (!isSafeHyperlinkUri(uri)) return;
    _openAt = buffer.createAnchorFromCursor();
    _openUri = uri;
  }

  void _close(Buffer buffer) {
    final begin = _openAt;
    final uri = _openUri;
    _openAt = null;
    _openUri = null;
    if (begin == null || uri == null) return;
    final end = buffer.createAnchorFromCursor();
    if (!begin.attached || !end.attached) {
      begin.dispose();
      end.dispose();
      return;
    }
    _ranges.add(HyperlinkRange(begin, end, uri));
    _prune();
  }

  void _prune() {
    _ranges.removeWhere((r) {
      final gone = !r.attached;
      if (gone) {
        r.begin.dispose();
        r.end.dispose();
      }
      return gone;
    });
    while (_ranges.length > maxRanges) {
      final old = _ranges.removeAt(0);
      old.begin.dispose();
      old.end.dispose();
    }
  }

  /// The link under cell ([row], [col]), the newest first.
  HyperlinkRange? at(int row, int col) {
    for (var i = _ranges.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      if (_ranges[i].contains(row, col)) return _ranges[i];
    }
    return null;
  }

  void dispose() {
    _openAt?.dispose();
    for (final r in _ranges) {
      r.begin.dispose();
      r.end.dispose();
    }
    _ranges.clear();
  }
}
```

File: `apps/desktop/lib/src/terminal/terminal_buffer_text.dart`

```dart
import 'package:xterm/core.dart';

/// A logical line of the terminal: the rows the terminal wrapped
/// (`isWrapped`) joined into one string, with the cell of every UTF-16
/// unit (A10 D9). Link detection runs on [text]; the cells map the result
/// back to the screen.
final class LogicalLine {
  LogicalLine._(this.firstRow, this.text, this._rows, this._cols, this._widths);

  /// Buffer row of the first row.
  final int firstRow;
  final String text;
  final List<int> _rows;
  final List<int> _cols;
  final List<int> _widths;

  bool get isEmpty => text.isEmpty;

  /// The index into [text] of the character drawn in cell ([row], [col]);
  /// null when the cell is past the text.
  int? indexOfCell(int row, int col) {
    for (var i = 0; i < _rows.length; i++) {
      if (_rows[i] == row && col >= _cols[i] && col < _cols[i] + _widths[i]) {
        return i;
      }
    }
    return null;
  }

  /// The first cell of the character at [index].
  CellOffset beginOf(int index) => CellOffset(_cols[index], _rows[index]);

  /// The cell after the character at [lastIndex] (end exclusive).
  CellOffset endAfter(int lastIndex) =>
      CellOffset(_cols[lastIndex] + _widths[lastIndex], _rows[lastIndex]);
}

/// The logical line that contains buffer row [row]: back to its first row
/// and on to its last, at most [maxRows] rows (A10 D9: 4 096 characters at
/// 512 columns is more than any link needs). Trailing blanks of the last
/// row are not part of the text; a row that wraps on is full.
LogicalLine logicalLineAt(Buffer buffer, int row, {int maxRows = 8}) {
  final height = buffer.height;
  if (row < 0 || row >= height) {
    return LogicalLine._(row, '', const [], const [], const []);
  }
  var first = row;
  while (first > 0 &&
      buffer.lines[first].isWrapped &&
      row - first < maxRows - 1) {
    first--;
  }
  var last = row;
  while (last + 1 < height &&
      buffer.lines[last + 1].isWrapped &&
      last - first + 1 < maxRows) {
    last++;
  }
  final text = StringBuffer();
  final rows = <int>[];
  final cols = <int>[];
  final widths = <int>[];
  for (var r = first; r <= last; r++) {
    final line = buffer.lines[r];
    final wrapsOn = r < last;
    final cells = wrapsOn ? line.length : line.getTrimmedLength();
    for (var c = 0; c < cells; c++) {
      final width = line.getWidth(c);
      if (width == 0) continue;
      final code = line.getCodePoint(c);
      final unit = code == 0 ? ' ' : String.fromCharCode(code);
      for (var u = 0; u < unit.length; u++) {
        rows.add(r);
        cols.add(c);
        widths.add(width);
      }
      text.write(unit);
    }
  }
  return LogicalLine._(first, text.toString(), rows, cols, widths);
}

```dart
import 'package:shellx_core/shellx_core.dart';
import 'package:xterm/core.dart';

import 'desktop_terminal.dart';
import 'terminal_buffer_text.dart';

/// Where a link came from.
enum LinkSource { detected, hyperlink }

/// The link under a cell: [begin] and [end] (exclusive) are buffer cells.
final class LinkHit {
  const LinkHit({
    required this.source,
    required this.kind,
    required this.target,
    required this.begin,
    required this.end,
    this.needsConfirm = false,
    this.visible,
  });

  final LinkSource source;
  final TerminalLinkKind kind;

  /// The address, or the path as written (without its `:line`).
  final String target;
  final CellOffset begin;
  final CellOffset end;

  /// An OSC 8 link whose text is not its address (A10 D10).
  final bool needsConfirm;

  /// The text shown for an OSC 8 link.
  final String? visible;

  bool get isWeb => kind == TerminalLinkKind.url;

  /// Never shows the target (A10 §5).
  @override
  String toString() => 'LinkHit(…)';
}

/// A10 D6–D11: the link under a cell of [terminal]: an OSC 8 hyperlink
/// first, else a URL (or, with [paths], a path) found in the logical line.
LinkHit? findLinkAt(
  DesktopTerminal terminal,
  CellOffset cell, {
  required bool paths,
}) {
  final buffer = terminal.buffer;
  if (cell.y < 0 || cell.y >= buffer.height) return null;
  final osc = terminal.hyperlinks.at(cell.y, cell.x);
  if (osc != null) {
    final begin = osc.begin.offset;
    final end = osc.end.offset;
    final visible = buffer.getText(BufferRangeLine(begin, end));
    return LinkHit(
      source: LinkSource.hyperlink,
      kind: TerminalLinkKind.url,
      target: osc.uri,
      begin: begin,
      end: end,
      visible: visible,
      needsConfirm: !hyperlinkTextMatches(visible, osc.uri),
    );
  }
  final line = logicalLineAt(buffer, cell.y);
  final index = line.indexOfCell(cell.y, cell.x);
  if (index == null) return null;
  final link = linkAt(line.text, index, paths: paths);
  if (link == null) return null;
  return LinkHit(
    source: LinkSource.detected,
    kind: link.kind,
    target: link.target,
    begin: line.beginOf(link.start),
    end: line.endAfter(link.end - 1),
  );
}

```dart
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:path/path.dart' as p;

/// A path that exists, absolute and normalised.
final class ResolvedPath {
  const ResolvedPath(this.path, {required this.isDirectory});
  final String path;
  final bool isDirectory;

  /// Never shows the path (A10 §5).
  @override
  String toString() => 'ResolvedPath(…)';
}

/// What [LinkResolver] asks the file system.
typedef EntityTypeOf = Future<FileSystemEntityType> Function(String path);

/// A10 D7: turns a path found in the terminal into one that exists on this
/// computer. `~/` is [home], a relative one is [cwd]'s (null cwd: no
/// answer), `..` is normalised away, a symbolic link is followed. Answers
/// are remembered for [ttl] (hovering asks again and again).
final class LinkResolver {
  LinkResolver({
    required this.home,
    required this.windows,
    EntityTypeOf? typeOf,
    DateTime Function()? now,
    this.ttl = const Duration(seconds: 2),
    this.maxEntries = 64,
  }) : _typeOf =
           typeOf ?? ((path) => FileSystemEntity.type(path, followLinks: true)),
       _now = now ?? DateTime.now;

  final String? home;
  final bool windows;
  final EntityTypeOf _typeOf;
  final DateTime Function() _now;
  final Duration ttl;
  final int maxEntries;
  final _cache = <String, (DateTime, ResolvedPath?)>{};

  static final RegExp _control = RegExp(r'[\u0000-\u001f\u007f]');

  p.Context get _ctx => windows ? p.windows : p.posix;

  /// The absolute form of [target] before any file system check; null when
  /// it cannot be made one.
  String? absoluteOf(String target, {String? cwd}) {
    if (target.isEmpty || _control.hasMatch(target)) return null;
    String joined;
    if (target == '~' || target.startsWith('~/')) {
      final h = home;
      if (h == null || h.isEmpty) return null;
      joined = target == '~' ? h : _ctx.join(h, target.substring(2));
    } else if (_ctx.isAbsolute(target)) {
      joined = target;
    } else {
      if (cwd == null || cwd.isEmpty || !_ctx.isAbsolute(cwd)) return null;
      joined = _ctx.join(cwd, target);
    }
    final normal = _ctx.normalize(joined);
    // Never an option for `open` / `explorer`, whatever came in.
    return _ctx.isAbsolute(normal) ? normal : null;
  }

  /// The existing file or folder [target] means, or null.
  Future<ResolvedPath?> resolve(String target, {String? cwd}) async {
    final absolute = absoluteOf(target, cwd: cwd);
    if (absolute == null) return null;
    final at = _now();
    final hit = _cache[absolute];
    if (hit != null && at.difference(hit.$1) < ttl) return hit.$2;
    ResolvedPath? found;
    try {
      final type = await _typeOf(absolute);
      if (type != FileSystemEntityType.notFound) {
        found = ResolvedPath(
          absolute,
          isDirectory: type == FileSystemEntityType.directory,
        );
      }
    } on Object {
      found = null; // unreadable is "not there"
    }
    if (_cache.length >= maxEntries) _cache.remove(_cache.keys.first);
    _cache[absolute] = (at, found);
    return found;
  }
}

---

### Task 7: Desktop — clickable links: hover, ⌘/Ctrl-click, menu (D6–D8, D10) — **M · ⚠ MANUAL**

Hover with ⌘ (Windows Ctrl) held underlines the link under the pointer and shows a hand; ⌘-click opens a URL in the default browser (an OSC 8 link whose text is not its address asks first) or reveals an **existing** local path in Finder / Explorer; the right-click menu gets "Open link" / "Copy link address" / "Show in Finder". A pane on a server links URLs only. `UrlOpener.open` stays https-only: two new methods carry the new rules.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/desktop/lib/src/ui/terminal/link_layer.dart`
- Modify: `apps/desktop/lib/src/platform/url_opener.dart`, `apps/desktop/test/support/fakes.dart` (`RecordingUrlOpener`), `apps/desktop/lib/src/terminal/terminal_tabs.dart` (`LinkOpenFailedNotice`), `apps/desktop/lib/src/ui/terminal/terminal_overlays.dart`, `apps/desktop/lib/src/ui/terminal/pane_tree_view.dart`, `apps/desktop/lib/l10n/app_{en,vi}.arb` (+ generated)
- Test: `apps/desktop/test/ui/link_layer_test.dart`, `apps/desktop/test/platform/url_opener_web_link_test.dart`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 6, `desktopServicesProvider.urlOpener`, `WindowRouter.confirm`, `terminalTabsProvider.notifier.showNotice`.
- Produces:
  - `UrlOpener.openWebLink(Uri)` (http/https via `webUriOf`, else `ArgumentError`, no command), `UrlOpener.reveal(String absolutePath, {required bool isDirectory})` (`open -R` / `open` on macOS, `explorer.exe /select,` / folder on Windows; relative, option-like or control-bearing paths refused).
  - `linkResolverProvider`, `linkModifierDown`, `paneHasLocalPaths(PaneSession)`, `shortenAddress(String)`, `Future<void> activateLink(WidgetRef, BuildContext, LinkHit, PaneSession)` (never throws; failure → `LinkOpenFailedNotice`), `LinkLayer({pane, viewKey, scrollController, builder})`.
  - `PaneTerminal.onMenu(Offset, CellOffset)` (changed signature).

```dart
import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:shellx_desktop/src/platform/url_opener.dart';

void main() {
  late List<String> ran;
  Future<ProcessResult> run(String exe, List<String> args) async {
    ran.add(jsonEncode([exe, ...args]));
    return ProcessResult(0, 0, '', '');
  }

  setUp(() => ran = []);

  group('openWebLink (A10 D6)', () {
    test('http and https open; the arguments are the address alone', () async {
      final mac = SystemUrlOpener(isWindows: false, run: run);
      await mac.openWebLink(Uri.parse('https://example.com/a?b=1'));
      await mac.openWebLink(Uri.parse('http://localhost:3000/'));
      expect(ran, [
        '["/usr/bin/open","https://example.com/a?b=1"]',
        '["/usr/bin/open","http://localhost:3000/"]',
      ]);
      ran.clear();
      await SystemUrlOpener(
        isWindows: true,
        run: run,
      ).openWebLink(Uri.parse('https://example.com/'));
      expect(
        ran.single,
        '["rundll32","url.dll,FileProtocolHandler","https://example.com/"]',
      );
    });

    test('nothing else ever reaches a command', () async {
      final opener = SystemUrlOpener(isWindows: false, run: run);
      for (final bad in [
        'file:///etc/passwd',
        'javascript:alert(1)',
        'ssh://host',
        'mailto:a@b.c',
        'x-apple.systempreferences:com.apple.preference.security',
        '/usr/bin/open',
        'https://',
      ]) {
        await expectLater(
          opener.openWebLink(Uri.parse(bad)),
          throwsArgumentError,
          reason: bad,
        );
      }
      expect(ran, isEmpty);
    });

    test('open() is still https only (the app\'s own links)', () async {
      final opener = SystemUrlOpener(isWindows: false, run: run);
      await expectLater(
        opener.open(Uri.parse('http://example.com')),
        throwsArgumentError,
      );
    });
  });

  group('reveal (A10 D7)', () {
    test('macOS: -R for a file, the folder itself for a folder', () async {
      final mac = SystemUrlOpener(isWindows: false, run: run);
      await mac.reveal('/Users/me/a b.txt', isDirectory: false);
      await mac.reveal('/Users/me/dir', isDirectory: true);
      expect(ran, [
        '["/usr/bin/open","-R","/Users/me/a b.txt"]',
        '["/usr/bin/open","/Users/me/dir"]',
      ]);
    });

    test('Windows: explorer /select,', () async {
      final win = SystemUrlOpener(isWindows: true, run: run);
      await win.reveal(r'C:\Users\me\a b.txt', isDirectory: false);
      await win.reveal(r'C:\Users\me', isDirectory: true);
      expect(ran, [
        r'["explorer.exe","/select,C:\\Users\\me\\a b.txt"]',
        r'["explorer.exe","C:\\Users\\me"]',
      ]);
    });

    test(
      'a relative path, an option or a control character is refused',
      () async {
        final mac = SystemUrlOpener(isWindows: false, run: run);
        for (final bad in ['a.txt', '-a', '../x', '/tmp/a\nb', '']) {
          await expectLater(
            mac.reveal(bad, isDirectory: false),
            throwsArgumentError,
            reason: bad,
          );
        }
        await expectLater(
          SystemUrlOpener(
            isWindows: true,
            run: run,
          ).reveal('a.txt', isDirectory: false),
          throwsArgumentError,
        );
        expect(ran, isEmpty);
      },
    );
  });
}

```dart
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:flutter/gestures.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:shellx_agent/shellx_agent.dart';
import 'package:shellx_desktop/src/agent/desktop_services.dart';
import 'package:shellx_desktop/src/terminal/link_resolver.dart';
import 'package:shellx_desktop/src/terminal/terminal_tabs.dart';
import 'package:shellx_desktop/src/ui/terminal/link_layer.dart';
import 'package:shellx_desktop/src/ui/window_router.dart';
import 'package:xterm/xterm.dart';

import '../support/config_workspace.dart';
import '../support/fakes.dart';

/// The global position of the middle of cell ([col], [row]) of the view.
Offset cellCentre(WidgetTester t, int col, int row) {
  final state = t.state<TerminalViewState>(find.byType(TerminalView));
  final render = state.renderTerminal;
  final local = render.getOffset(CellOffset(col, row));
  return t.getTopLeft(find.byType(TerminalView)) +
      local +
      Offset(render.cellSize.width / 2, render.cellSize.height / 2);
}

RecordingUrlOpener opener(dynamic w) =>
    w.container.read(desktopServicesProvider).urlOpener as RecordingUrlOpener;

/// A mouse click (the default `tapAt` is a touch).
Future<void> click(WidgetTester t, Offset at) =>
    t.tapAt(at, kind: PointerDeviceKind.mouse);

Future<TestGesture> hover(WidgetTester t, Offset at) async {
  final g = await t.createGesture(kind: PointerDeviceKind.mouse);
  await g.addPointer(location: const Offset(1, 1));
  addTearDown(g.removePointer);
  await g.moveTo(at);
  await t.pump();
  return g;
}

void main() {
  final mac = TargetPlatformVariant.only(TargetPlatform.macOS);

  testWidgets('hover with ⌘ underlines a URL; without it nothing', (t) async {
    final w = await pumpConfiguredWorkspace(t);
    final tab = w.container.read(terminalTabsProvider).selected!;
    tab.terminal.write('visit https://example.com/a?b=1.');
    await t.pump();
    await hover(t, cellCentre(t, 10, 0));
    expect(find.byKey(const Key('link.underline')), findsNothing);
    await t.sendKeyDownEvent(LogicalKeyboardKey.metaLeft);
    await t.pump();
    await t.pump();
    expect(find.byKey(const Key('link.underline')), findsOneWidget);
    await t.sendKeyUpEvent(LogicalKeyboardKey.metaLeft);
    await t.pump();
    await t.pump();
    expect(find.byKey(const Key('link.underline')), findsNothing);
    await disposeWorkspace(t, w.container);
  }, variant: mac);

  testWidgets('⌘-click opens the URL without its full stop; a plain click '
      'does not', (t) async {
    final w = await pumpConfiguredWorkspace(t);
    final tab = w.container.read(terminalTabsProvider).selected!;
    tab.terminal.write('visit https://example.com/a?b=1.');
    await t.pump();
    final at = cellCentre(t, 10, 0);
    await click(t, at);
    await t.pump(const Duration(milliseconds: 400));
    expect(opener(w).webLinks, isEmpty);
    await t.sendKeyDownEvent(LogicalKeyboardKey.metaLeft);
    await click(t, at);
    await t.sendKeyUpEvent(LogicalKeyboardKey.metaLeft);
    await t.pump(const Duration(milliseconds: 400));
    expect(opener(w).webLinks.map((u) => u.toString()), [
      'https://example.com/a?b=1',
    ]);
    await disposeWorkspace(t, w.container);
  }, variant: mac);

  testWidgets('an OSC 8 link whose text differs asks first', (t) async {
    final w = await pumpConfiguredWorkspace(t);
    final tab = w.container.read(terminalTabsProvider).selected!;
    tab.terminal.write(
      '\x1b]8;;https://example.com\x1b\\click here\x1b]8;;\x1b\\',
    );
    await t.pump();
    final at = cellCentre(t, 2, 0);
    await t.sendKeyDownEvent(LogicalKeyboardKey.metaLeft);
    await click(t, at);
    await t.sendKeyUpEvent(LogicalKeyboardKey.metaLeft);
    await t.pump(const Duration(milliseconds: 400));
    final ask = w.container.read(windowRouterProvider).confirm!;
    expect(ask.title, 'Open this link?');
    expect(ask.body, contains('https://example.com'));
    expect(opener(w).webLinks, isEmpty);
    ask.complete(true);
    await t.pump(const Duration(milliseconds: 400));
    expect(opener(w).webLinks.single.toString(), 'https://example.com');
    await disposeWorkspace(t, w.container);
  }, variant: mac);

  testWidgets('a failing browser is a toast, not an error', (t) async {
    final w = await pumpConfiguredWorkspace(t);
    final tab = w.container.read(terminalTabsProvider).selected!;
    tab.terminal.write('https://example.com');
    await t.pump();
    opener(w).fail = true;
    await t.sendKeyDownEvent(LogicalKeyboardKey.metaLeft);
    await click(t, cellCentre(t, 3, 0));
    await t.sendKeyUpEvent(LogicalKeyboardKey.metaLeft);
    await t.pump(const Duration(milliseconds: 400));
    expect(
      w.container.read(terminalTabsProvider).notice,
      isA<LinkOpenFailedNotice>(),
    );
    await t.pump(const Duration(seconds: 7));
    await disposeWorkspace(t, w.container);
  }, variant: mac);

  testWidgets('a failing open logs the error type, never the address', (
    t,
  ) async {
    final w = await pumpConfiguredWorkspace(t);
    final tab = w.container.read(terminalTabsProvider).selected!;
    tab.terminal.write('https://example.com/SECRET-LINK-TEXT');
    await t.pump();
    opener(w).fail = true;
    final lines = <String>[];
    final original = debugPrint;
    debugPrint = (message, {wrapWidth}) => lines.add(message ?? '');
    await t.sendKeyDownEvent(LogicalKeyboardKey.metaLeft);
    await click(t, cellCentre(t, 3, 0));
    await t.sendKeyUpEvent(LogicalKeyboardKey.metaLeft);
    await t.pump(const Duration(milliseconds: 400));
    debugPrint =
        original; // the framework insists it is back before the test ends
    expect(lines.join(), contains('ProcessException'));
    expect(lines.join(), isNot(contains('SECRET')));
    await t.pump(const Duration(seconds: 7));
    await disposeWorkspace(t, w.container);
  }, variant: mac);

  testWidgets('an existing file is revealed; a missing one is no link', (
    t,
  ) async {
    final resolver = LinkResolver(
      home: '/Users/me',
      windows: false,
      typeOf: (path) async => path == '/work/notes.txt'
          ? FileSystemEntityType.file
          : FileSystemEntityType.notFound,
    );
    final w = await pumpConfiguredWorkspace(
      t,
      extra: [linkResolverProvider.overrideWithValue(resolver)],
    );
    final tab = w.container.read(terminalTabsProvider).selected!;
    tab.terminal.write('/work/notes.txt /work/missing.txt');
    await t.pump();
    await t.sendKeyDownEvent(LogicalKeyboardKey.metaLeft);
    await click(t, cellCentre(t, 20, 0)); // on /work/missing.txt
    await t.pump(const Duration(milliseconds: 400));
    expect(opener(w).revealed, isEmpty);
    await click(t, cellCentre(t, 3, 0)); // on /work/notes.txt
    await t.pump(const Duration(milliseconds: 400));
    await t.sendKeyUpEvent(LogicalKeyboardKey.metaLeft);
    expect(opener(w).revealed.single, ('/work/notes.txt', false));
    await disposeWorkspace(t, w.container);
  }, variant: mac);

  testWidgets('a server pane links URLs but never paths', (t) async {
    final w = await pumpConfiguredWorkspace(t);
    final c = w.container;
    final ssh = SessionRemote(
      launch: SshLaunch(SshTarget.parse('devbox')!),
      connected: true,
    );
    w.local.add(w.local.summary(7, origin: SessionOrigin.desktop, remote: ssh));
    await t.pump();
    final tab = c.read(terminalTabsProvider).tabs.firstWhere((x) => x.id == 7);
    c.read(terminalTabsProvider.notifier).focusPane(7);
    await t.pump();
    tab.terminal.write('/etc/hosts https://example.com');
    await t.pump();
    await t.sendKeyDownEvent(LogicalKeyboardKey.metaLeft);
    await click(t, cellCentre(t, 3, 0));
    await t.pump(const Duration(milliseconds: 400));
    expect(opener(w).revealed, isEmpty);
    await click(t, cellCentre(t, 15, 0));
    await t.pump(const Duration(milliseconds: 400));
    await t.sendKeyUpEvent(LogicalKeyboardKey.metaLeft);
    expect(opener(w).webLinks, hasLength(1));
    await disposeWorkspace(t, c);
  }, variant: mac);

  testWidgets('the menu on a link has Open link and Copy link address', (
    t,
  ) async {
    final w = await pumpConfiguredWorkspace(t);
    final tab = w.container.read(terminalTabsProvider).selected!;
    tab.terminal.write('https://example.com/x');
    await t.pump();
    await t.tapAt(cellCentre(t, 4, 0), buttons: kSecondaryButton);
    await t.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.byKey(const Key('menu.openLink')), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('Copy link address'), findsOneWidget);
    await t.tap(find.byKey(const Key('menu.openLink')));
    await t.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(opener(w).webLinks.single.toString(), 'https://example.com/x');
    await disposeWorkspace(t, w.container);
  }, variant: mac);

  testWidgets('the menu away from a link has neither', (t) async {
    final w = await pumpConfiguredWorkspace(t);
    final tab = w.container.read(terminalTabsProvider).selected!;
    tab.terminal.write('plain words');
    await t.pump();
    await t.tapAt(cellCentre(t, 2, 0), buttons: kSecondaryButton);
    await t.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.byKey(const Key('menu.openLink')), findsNothing);
    await t.tapAt(const Offset(2, 2));
    await t.pumpAndSettle();
    await disposeWorkspace(t, w.container);
  }, variant: mac);
}

- [ ] **Step 3: The opener.** In `url_opener.dart` add `import 'package:shellx_core/shellx_core.dart' show webUriOf;`, two members to `UrlOpener`, and implement them in `SystemUrlOpener`:

```dart
  /// A10 D6: a link the user clicked in the terminal — `http` or `https`
  /// only. Throws [ArgumentError] for anything else without running a
  /// command. [open] stays https-only: it is for links the app itself
  /// builds.
  Future<void> openWebLink(Uri uri);

  /// A10 D7: shows [path] (absolute) in Finder / Explorer; a folder is
  /// opened. Never runs the file. Throws [ArgumentError] for a path that is
  /// not absolute or holds a control character.
  Future<void> reveal(String path, {required bool isDirectory});
```

```dart
  @override
  Future<void> openWebLink(Uri uri) async {
    if (webUriOf(uri.toString()) == null) {
      throw ArgumentError.value(uri.scheme, 'uri', 'Only http(s) links open');
    }
    if (_isWindows) {
      await _run('rundll32', ['url.dll,FileProtocolHandler', uri.toString()]);
    } else {
      await _run('/usr/bin/open', [uri.toString()]);
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<void> reveal(String path, {required bool isDirectory}) async {
    final absolute = _isWindows
        ? RegExp(r'^[A-Za-z]:\\').hasMatch(path)
        : path.startsWith('/');
    if (!absolute || RegExp(r'[\u0000-\u001f\u007f]').hasMatch(path)) {
      throw ArgumentError.value('path', 'path', 'Not an absolute path');
    }
    if (_isWindows) {
      await _run('explorer.exe', [isDirectory ? path : '/select,$path']);
    } else {
      await _run('/usr/bin/open', [if (!isDirectory) '-R', path]);
    }
  }
```

In `test/support/fakes.dart`, `RecordingUrlOpener` gets the two members (every implementer in the same task):

  /// A10: links clicked in the terminal.
  final List<Uri> webLinks = [];

  @override
  Future<void> openWebLink(Uri uri) async {
    if (fail) throw const ProcessException('/usr/bin/open', [], 'failed');
    webLinks.add(uri);
  }

  /// A10: paths shown in Finder / Explorer (path, isDirectory).
  final List<(String, bool)> revealed = [];

  @override
  Future<void> reveal(String path, {required bool isDirectory}) async {
    if (fail) throw const ProcessException('/usr/bin/open', [], 'failed');
    revealed.add((path, isDirectory));
  }

- [ ] **Step 5: The layer**


File: `apps/desktop/lib/src/ui/terminal/link_layer.dart`

```dart
import 'dart:async';
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:flutter/gestures.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:shellx_core/shellx_core.dart' show TerminalLinkKind;
import 'package:xterm/xterm.dart';

import '../../../l10n/app_localizations.dart';
import '../../agent/desktop_services.dart';
import '../../terminal/link_finder.dart';
import '../../terminal/link_resolver.dart';
import '../../terminal/terminal_tabs.dart';
import '../theme/shellx_desktop_theme.dart';
import '../window_router.dart';

/// A10 D7: how paths found in the terminal are checked and shown.
final linkResolverProvider = Provider<LinkResolver>(
  (ref) => LinkResolver(
    home: Platform.environment['HOME'] ?? Platform.environment['USERPROFILE'],
    windows: Platform.isWindows,
  ),
);

/// Whether the key that opens links is down: ⌘ on macOS, Ctrl on Windows
/// (A10 D8).
bool get linkModifierDown => defaultTargetPlatform == TargetPlatform.macOS
    ? HardwareKeyboard.instance.isMetaPressed
    : HardwareKeyboard.instance.isControlPressed;

/// A pane has local paths (not a server's, not a tmux on a server).
bool paneHasLocalPaths(PaneSession pane) => pane.remote?.launch.host == null;

/// The address shown in the question about an OSC 8 link: 200 characters at
/// most, the middle cut, so the host at the start and the tail both show.
String shortenAddress(String uri) => uri.length <= 200
    ? uri
    : '${uri.substring(0, 120)}…${uri.substring(uri.length - 79)}';

/// Opens or reveals [hit] (A10 D6, D7, D10). Never throws; a failure is a
/// toast.
Future<void> activateLink(
  WidgetRef ref,
  BuildContext context,
  LinkHit hit,
  PaneSession pane,
) async {
  final l10n = AppLocalizations.of(context);
  final opener = ref.read(desktopServicesProvider).urlOpener;
  final tabs = ref.read(terminalTabsProvider.notifier);
  final router = ref.read(windowRouterProvider.notifier);
  try {
    if (hit.kind == TerminalLinkKind.url) {
      if (hit.needsConfirm) {
        final ok = await router.confirm(
          ConfirmRequest(
            title: l10n.linkConfirmTitle,
            body: l10n.linkConfirmBody(shortenAddress(hit.target)),
            confirmLabel: l10n.linkConfirmAction,
            destructive: false,
          ),
        );
        if (!ok) return;
      }
      await opener.openWebLink(Uri.parse(hit.target));
    } else {
      final found = await ref
          .read(linkResolverProvider)
          .resolve(hit.target, cwd: pane.cwd);
      if (found == null) return;
      await opener.reveal(found.path, isDirectory: found.isDirectory);
    }
  } on Object catch (error) {
    debugPrint('ShellX: opening a link failed: ${error.runtimeType}');
    tabs.showNotice(const LinkOpenFailedNotice());
  }
}

typedef LinkViewBuilder =
    Widget Function(BuildContext context, MouseCursor cursor);

/// A10 D8: hover with ⌘ (Ctrl) held underlines the link under the pointer
/// and shows a hand; ⌘-click opens it. [builder] builds the TerminalView
/// with the cursor it must use. Plain clicks are untouched.
///
/// The click is read from a [Listener] (outside the gesture arena), not from
/// `TerminalView.onTapUp`: xterm 4.0.0 never calls that callback (its
/// gesture detector only forwards `onSingleTapUp`, which `TerminalView` does
/// not set).
class LinkLayer extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const LinkLayer({
    super.key,
    required this.pane,
    required this.viewKey,
    required this.scrollController,
    required this.builder,
  });

  final PaneSession pane;
  final GlobalKey<TerminalViewState> viewKey;
  final ScrollController scrollController;
  final LinkViewBuilder builder;

  @override
  ConsumerState<LinkLayer> createState() => _LinkLayerState();
}

class _LinkLayerState extends ConsumerState<LinkLayer> {
  Offset? _pointer;
  Offset? _down;
  LinkHit? _hit;
  int _ask = 0;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    HardwareKeyboard.instance.addHandler(_onKey);
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    HardwareKeyboard.instance.removeHandler(_onKey);
    super.dispose();
  }

  bool _onKey(KeyEvent event) {
    final k = event.logicalKey;
    if (k == LogicalKeyboardKey.metaLeft ||
        k == LogicalKeyboardKey.metaRight ||
        k == LogicalKeyboardKey.controlLeft ||
        k == LogicalKeyboardKey.controlRight) {
      // After the key state has been updated.
      WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) => _refresh());
      WidgetsBinding.instance.ensureVisualUpdate();
    }
    return false;
  }

  CellOffset? _cellAt(Offset local) =>
      widget.viewKey.currentState?.renderTerminal.getCellOffset(local);

  void _set(LinkHit? hit) {
    if (hit == null && _hit == null) return;
    setState(() => _hit = hit);
  }

  Future<void> _refresh() async {
    if (!mounted) return;
    final pointer = _pointer;
    final cell = pointer == null ? null : _cellAt(pointer);
    if (cell == null || !linkModifierDown) {
      _ask++;
      _set(null);
      return;
    }
    final hit = findLinkAt(
      widget.pane.terminal,
      cell,
      paths: paneHasLocalPaths(widget.pane),
    );
    if (hit == null || hit.kind == TerminalLinkKind.url) {
      _ask++;
      _set(hit);
      return;
    }
    // A path is a link only when it exists (A10 D7): ask, then show.
    final ask = ++_ask;
    final found = await ref
        .read(linkResolverProvider)
        .resolve(hit.target, cwd: widget.pane.cwd);
    if (!mounted || ask != _ask) return;
    _set(found == null ? null : hit);
  }

  void _click(Offset local) {
    if (!linkModifierDown) return;
    final cell = _cellAt(local);
    if (cell == null) return;
    final hit = findLinkAt(
      widget.pane.terminal,
      cell,
      paths: paneHasLocalPaths(widget.pane),
    );
    if (hit == null) return;
    unawaited(activateLink(ref, context, hit, widget.pane));
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final hit = _hit;
    return MouseRegion(
      onHover: (e) {
        _pointer = e.localPosition;
        unawaited(_refresh());
      },
      onExit: (_) {
        _pointer = null;
        unawaited(_refresh());
      },
      child: Listener(
        behavior: HitTestBehavior.translucent,
        onPointerDown: (e) => _down =
            e.kind == PointerDeviceKind.mouse && e.buttons == kPrimaryButton
            ? e.localPosition
            : null,
        onPointerUp: (e) {
          final down = _down;
          _down = null;
          if (down != null && (e.localPosition - down).distance < kTouchSlop) {
            _click(e.localPosition);
          }
        },
        child: Stack(
          fit: StackFit.passthrough,
          children: [
            widget.builder(
              context,
              hit == null ? SystemMouseCursors.text : SystemMouseCursors.click,
            ),
            if (hit != null)
              Positioned.fill(
                child: IgnorePointer(
                  child: CustomPaint(
                    key: const Key('link.underline'),
                    painter: _UnderlinePainter(
                      hit: hit,
                      viewKey: widget.viewKey,
                      terminal: widget.pane.terminal,
                      repaint: widget.scrollController,
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _UnderlinePainter extends CustomPainter {
  _UnderlinePainter({
    required this.hit,
    required this.viewKey,
    required this.terminal,
    required Listenable repaint,
  }) : super(repaint: repaint);

  final LinkHit hit;
  final GlobalKey<TerminalViewState> viewKey;
  final Terminal terminal;

  @override
  void paint(Canvas canvas, Size size) {
    final render = viewKey.currentState?.renderTerminal;
    if (render == null) return;
    final paint = Paint()
      ..color = ShellxDesktopColors.accent
      ..strokeWidth = 1;
    final line = render.lineHeight;
    for (var y = hit.begin.y; y <= hit.end.y; y++) {
      final x0 = y == hit.begin.y ? hit.begin.x : 0;
      final x1 = y == hit.end.y ? hit.end.x : terminal.viewWidth;
      final a = render.getOffset(CellOffset(x0, y));
      final b = render.getOffset(CellOffset(x1, y));
      if (a.dy + line < 0 || a.dy > size.height) continue;
      canvas.drawLine(
        Offset(a.dx, a.dy + line - 1.5),
        Offset(b.dx, a.dy + line - 1.5),
        paint,
      );
    }
  }

  @override
  bool shouldRepaint(_UnderlinePainter old) =>
      old.hit != hit || old.terminal != terminal;
}
```


Why a `Listener` and not `TerminalView.onTapUp`: xterm 4.0.0's `TerminalGestureDetector` never invokes `onTapUp` (only `onSingleTapUp`, which `TerminalView` does not set) — a test that tapped with `onTapUp` stayed red until this was found. The `Listener` sits outside the gesture arena, so selection, scrolling and the double-click word select are untouched.

- [ ] **Step 6: Use it in the pane** (`pane_tree_view.dart`; imports `package:flutter/foundation.dart show defaultTargetPlatform`, `package:flutter/services.dart show Clipboard, ClipboardData`, `../../terminal/link_finder.dart`, `link_layer.dart`). Three edits:

1. `PaneTerminal.build` — wrap the view; the layer provides the cursor; the right-click callback now carries the cell:

```dart
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => ClipRect(
    // A buffer scrolled by part of a row paints that row outside the
    // view; the clip keeps it off the signed-out strip and pane header.
    child: ShellxScrollbar(
      controller: scrollController,
      // A10 D8: ⌘-click opens a link; the layer gives the view its cursor.
      child: LinkLayer(
        pane: pane,
        viewKey: viewKey,
        scrollController: scrollController,
        builder: (context, cursor) => TerminalView(
          pane.terminal,
          key: viewKey,
          controller: pane.selection,
          scrollController: scrollController,
          focusNode: focusNode,
          autofocus: focusNode != null,
          theme: theme,
          textStyle: style,
          cursorType: cursorType,
          padding: padding,
          shortcuts: shortcuts,
          mouseCursor: cursor,
          onKeyEvent: onKeyEvent == null
              ? null
              : (_, event) => onKeyEvent!(event),
          // Only when the program did not take the mouse (xterm reports
          // the click to it first): vim or htop with mouse mode keep it.
          onSecondaryTapUp: (details, cell) =>
              onMenu(details.globalPosition, cell),
        ),
      ),
    ),
  );
```

with `final void Function(Offset globalPosition, CellOffset cell) onMenu;` in the fields and `onMenu: (at, cell) => _menu(id, at, cell),` where `PaneTerminal` is built.

2. `_menu(int id, Offset at, CellOffset cell)` — find the link first (a path only counts when it exists), add its entries before `menu.copy`, and handle the two new choices:

      final found = await ref
          .read(linkResolverProvider)
          .resolve(link.target, cwd: pane.cwd);
      if (found == null) link = null;
    }
    if (!mounted) return;

### Task 8: Desktop — shell escaping and the pasted-image store (D15, D18) — **S**

Two pure/IO helpers for Task 9; nothing becomes visible. `escapePath` writes a file name as one word for the pane's shell family (a control character refuses the lot); `PasteImageStore` keeps a pasted image as a 0600 file in a 0700 temp folder and cleans up after itself.

- Create: `apps/desktop/lib/src/terminal/shell_escape.dart`, `apps/desktop/lib/src/terminal/paste_image_store.dart`
- Test: `apps/desktop/test/terminal/shell_escape_test.dart`, `apps/desktop/test/terminal/paste_image_store_test.dart`

- Produces:

import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:shellx_desktop/src/terminal/shell_escape.dart';

void main() {
  group('posix and fish (A10 D18)', () {
    String e(String s) => escapePath(s, ShellFamily.posix);

    test('plain paths are left alone', () {
      expect(e('/Users/me/a.png'), '/Users/me/a.png');
      expect(e('/tmp/a-b_c.d,e@f+g=h%i:j'), '/tmp/a-b_c.d,e@f+g=h%i:j');
    });

    test('spaces and shell characters get a backslash', () {
      expect(
        e('/Users/me/My Docs/a (1).png'),
        r'/Users/me/My\ Docs/a\ \(1\).png',
      );
      expect(e(r"/tmp/$(rm -rf ~)"), r'/tmp/\$\(rm\ -rf\ ~\)');
      expect(e("/tmp/it's"), r"/tmp/it\'s");
      expect(
        e('/tmp/a;b&c|d>e<f*g?h[i]{j}!k#l\\m"n`o'),
        r'/tmp/a\;b\&c\|d\>e\<f\*g\?h\[i\]\{j\}\!k\#l\\m\"n\`o',
      );
    });

    test('Vietnamese, CJK and emoji stay as they are', () {
      expect(e('/Users/me/Tệp ảnh.png'), r'/Users/me/Tệp\ ảnh.png');
      expect(e('/tmp/日本語😀'), '/tmp/日本語😀');
    });

    test('a tilde is only special at the start', () {
      expect(e('~/a'), r'\~/a');
      expect(e('/tmp/a~b'), '/tmp/a~b');
    });

    test('control characters refuse the whole path', () {
      for (final bad in [
        '/tmp/a\nb',
        '/tmp/a\x00b',
        '/tmp/a\x1bb',
        '/tmp/a\x7fb',
      ]) {
        expect(hasUnsafeCharacters(bad), isTrue);
        expect(() => e(bad), throwsFormatException);
      }
    });
  });

  group('PowerShell and cmd', () {
    test('safe names are bare, others quoted', () {
      expect(
        escapePath(r'C:\Users\me\a.png', ShellFamily.powershell),
        r'C:\Users\me\a.png',
      );
      expect(
        escapePath(r'C:\My Docs\a.png', ShellFamily.powershell),
        r"'C:\My Docs\a.png'",
      );
      expect(
        escapePath(r"C:\it's\a.png", ShellFamily.powershell),
        r"'C:\it''s\a.png'",
      );
      expect(
        escapePath(r'C:\My Docs\a.png', ShellFamily.cmd),
        r'"C:\My Docs\a.png"',
      );
    });
  });

  test('several paths end with a space, so typing can go on', () {
    expect(escapePaths(['/a b', '/c'], ShellFamily.posix), r'/a\ b /c ');
    expect(
      () => escapePaths(['/a', '/b\n'], ShellFamily.posix),
      throwsFormatException,
    );
  });

  test('the family follows the shell and the platform', () {
    expect(shellFamilyOf('zsh', windows: false), ShellFamily.posix);
    expect(shellFamilyOf('fish', windows: false), ShellFamily.posix);
    expect(shellFamilyOf('pwsh.exe', windows: true), ShellFamily.powershell);
    expect(shellFamilyOf('cmd.exe', windows: true), ShellFamily.cmd);
    expect(shellFamilyOf('nu', windows: true), ShellFamily.powershell);
    expect(shellFamilyOf('nu', windows: false), ShellFamily.posix);
  });
}
