```dart
import 'dart:io';
import 'dart:typed_data';

import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:shellx_desktop/src/terminal/paste_image_store.dart';

Uint8List png([int extra = 0]) => Uint8List.fromList([
  0x89,
  0x50,
  0x4E,
  0x47,
  0x0D,
  0x0A,
  ...List.filled(extra, 7),
]);

String mode(String path) =>
    (Process.runSync('stat', [
              ...(Platform.isMacOS ? ['-f', '%Lp'] : ['-c', '%a']),
              path,
            ]).stdout
            as String)
        .trim();

void main() {
  late Directory base;
  var now = DateTime(2026, 10, 7, 9, 5, 3);
  late PasteImageStore store;

  setUp(() async {
    base = await Directory.systemTemp.createTemp('paste_images');
    now = DateTime(2026, 10, 7, 9, 5, 3);
    store = PasteImageStore(base, now: () => now);
  });
  tearDown(() => base.delete(recursive: true));

  test('the extension follows the first bytes', () {
    expect(imageExtension(png()), 'png');
    expect(imageExtension(Uint8List.fromList([0xFF, 0xD8, 0xFF, 0xE0])), 'jpg');
    expect(imageExtension(Uint8List.fromList([0x47, 0x49, 0x46, 0x38])), 'gif');
    expect(imageExtension(Uint8List.fromList([0x42, 0x4D, 0, 0])), 'bmp');
    expect(
      imageExtension(Uint8List.fromList([0x49, 0x49, 0x2A, 0x00])),
      'tiff',
    );
    expect(imageExtension(Uint8List.fromList([1, 2, 3, 4])), isNull);
  });

  test('saves a timestamped file with the right extension', () async {
    final path = await store.save(png());
    expect(path, matches(RegExp(r'paste-20261007-090503-[0-9a-f]{8}\.png$')));
    expect(File(path).readAsBytesSync(), png());
  });

  test('0700 folder, 0600 file', () async {
    final path = await store.save(png());
    expect(mode(store.directory.path), '700');
    expect(mode(path), '600');
  }, testOn: '!windows');

  test(
    'too large, empty and not-an-image are refused; nothing is written',
    () async {
      await expectLater(
        store.save(Uint8List(kMaxPasteImageBytes + 1)..[0] = 0x89),
        throwsA(isA<PasteImageTooLarge>()),
      );
      await expectLater(
        store.save(Uint8List(0)),
        throwsA(isA<PasteImageUnreadable>()),
      );
      await expectLater(
        store.save(Uint8List.fromList([1, 2, 3])),
        throwsA(isA<PasteImageUnreadable>()),
      );
      expect(store.directory.existsSync(), isFalse);
    },
  );

  test('files older than 24 hours go when the next one is saved', () async {
    final old = await store.save(png());
    File(old).setLastModifiedSync(now.subtract(const Duration(hours: 25)));
    final fresh = await store.save(png(1));
    expect(File(old).existsSync(), isFalse);
    expect(File(fresh).existsSync(), isTrue);
  });

  test('at most 50 are kept, the oldest first to go', () async {
    final paths = <String>[];
    for (var i = 0; i < 52; i++) {
      final path = await store.save(png(i));
      File(path).setLastModifiedSync(now.subtract(Duration(minutes: 100 - i)));
      paths.add(path);
    }
    final left = store.directory.listSync().whereType<File>().length;
    expect(left, lessThanOrEqualTo(kMaxPasteImageFiles));
    expect(File(paths.first).existsSync(), isFalse);
    expect(File(paths.last).existsSync(), isTrue);
  });

  test('deleteAll empties the folder', () async {
    await store.save(png());
    await store.deleteAll();
    expect(store.directory.existsSync(), isFalse);
  });

  test('a failing chmod refuses the file and leaves none', () async {
    final failing = PasteImageStore(
      base,
      now: () => now,
      posix: true,
      chmod: (mode, paths) async => mode != '600',
    );
    await expectLater(failing.save(png()), throwsA(isA<FileSystemException>()));
    expect(failing.directory.listSync(), isEmpty);
  });
}
```

File: `apps/desktop/lib/src/terminal/shell_escape.dart`

```dart
/// How a dropped or pasted file name is written on a command line (A10
/// D18). Pure; the pane's shell decides the family.
library;

enum ShellFamily { posix, powershell, cmd }

/// The family of the shell a pane runs: [shell] is `PaneSession.shell` (the
/// program's base name). Unknown shells are POSIX on macOS and PowerShell
/// on Windows.
ShellFamily shellFamilyOf(String shell, {required bool windows}) {
  final name = shell.toLowerCase().replaceAll(RegExp(r'\.exe$'), '');
  if (name == 'cmd') return ShellFamily.cmd;
  if (name == 'powershell' || name == 'pwsh') return ShellFamily.powershell;
  const posix = {'zsh', 'bash', 'sh', 'fish', 'dash', 'ksh', 'tcsh', 'csh'};
  if (posix.contains(name)) return ShellFamily.posix;
  return windows ? ShellFamily.powershell : ShellFamily.posix;
}

final RegExp _control = RegExp(r'[\u0000-\u001f\u007f]');
final RegExp _posixSafe = RegExp(r'^[A-Za-z0-9_.,:/@%+=~\-\u0080-\uFFFF]$');
final RegExp _psSafe = RegExp(r'^[A-Za-z0-9_.,:/\\@%+=\-\u0080-\uFFFF]+$');

/// True when [path] cannot be written safely for any shell (a control
/// character, an NUL, a line break): the whole drop is refused.
bool hasUnsafeCharacters(String path) => _control.hasMatch(path);

/// [path] as one word of a command line for [family]. Throws
/// [FormatException] for a path [hasUnsafeCharacters].
String escapePath(String path, ShellFamily family) {
  if (hasUnsafeCharacters(path)) {
    throw const FormatException('unsafe characters in a file name');
  }
  switch (family) {
    case ShellFamily.posix:
      final out = StringBuffer();
      for (final unit in path.split('')) {
        // `~` is only special at the start of a word.
        final plain =
            _posixSafe.hasMatch(unit) && !(unit == '~' && out.isEmpty);
        if (!plain) out.write(r'\');
        out.write(unit);
      }
      return out.toString();
    case ShellFamily.powershell:
      if (path.isNotEmpty && _psSafe.hasMatch(path)) return path;
      return "'${path.replaceAll("'", "''")}'";
    case ShellFamily.cmd:
      if (path.isNotEmpty && _psSafe.hasMatch(path)) return path;
      return '"${path.replaceAll('"', '')}"';
  }
}

/// [paths] ready to paste: escaped, one space between, one space after.
/// Throws [FormatException] when any is unsafe.
String escapePaths(Iterable<String> paths, ShellFamily family) =>
    '${paths.map((p) => escapePath(p, family)).join(' ')} ';
```

File: `apps/desktop/lib/src/terminal/paste_image_store.dart`

```dart
import 'dart:io';
import 'dart:math';
import 'dart:typed_data';

import 'package:path/path.dart' as p;

/// An image bigger than this is not pasted (A10 D15).
const int kMaxPasteImageBytes = 20 * 1024 * 1024;

/// At most this many pasted images are kept; older ones go first.
const int kMaxPasteImageFiles = 50;

/// A pasted image older than this is deleted.
const Duration kPasteImageMaxAge = Duration(hours: 24);

final class PasteImageTooLarge implements Exception {
  const PasteImageTooLarge();
}

final class PasteImageUnreadable implements Exception {
  const PasteImageUnreadable();
}

/// The file extension for the image whose first bytes are [bytes], or null
/// when they are none of the formats a clipboard gives.
String? imageExtension(Uint8List bytes) {
  bool starts(List<int> magic) =>
      bytes.length >= magic.length &&
      [
        for (var i = 0; i < magic.length; i++) bytes[i] == magic[i],
      ].every((e) => e);
  if (starts(const [0x89, 0x50, 0x4E, 0x47])) return 'png';
  if (starts(const [0xFF, 0xD8, 0xFF])) return 'jpg';
  if (starts(const [0x47, 0x49, 0x46, 0x38])) return 'gif';
  if (starts(const [0x42, 0x4D])) return 'bmp';
  if (starts(const [0x49, 0x49, 0x2A, 0x00]) ||
      starts(const [0x4D, 0x4D, 0x00, 0x2A])) {
    return 'tiff';
  }
  return null;
}

/// A10 D15: keeps a pasted image as a file in a private temp folder so its
/// path can be pasted. The folder is 0700 and the files 0600 (POSIX); files
/// older than [kPasteImageMaxAge] and any beyond [kMaxPasteImageFiles] are
/// deleted whenever a new one is saved. Nothing here logs a path or a size.
class PasteImageStore {
  PasteImageStore(
    this.base, {
    DateTime Function()? now,
    Random? random,
    bool? posix,
    Future<bool> Function(String mode, List<String> paths)? chmod,
  }) : _now = now ?? DateTime.now,
       _random = random ?? Random.secure(),
       _posix = posix ?? !Platform.isWindows,
       _chmod = chmod ?? _systemChmod;

  /// The system temp folder (`path_provider`'s `getTemporaryDirectory`).
  final Directory base;
  final DateTime Function() _now;
  final Random _random;
  final bool _posix;
  final Future<bool> Function(String mode, List<String> paths) _chmod;

  Directory get directory => Directory(p.join(base.path, 'shellx-paste'));

  /// Saves [bytes] and returns the file's path. Throws
  /// [PasteImageTooLarge], [PasteImageUnreadable] (empty, or not an image),
  /// or a [FileSystemException].
  Future<String> save(Uint8List bytes) async {
    if (bytes.length > kMaxPasteImageBytes) throw const PasteImageTooLarge();
    final ext = imageExtension(bytes);
    if (bytes.isEmpty || ext == null) throw const PasteImageUnreadable();
    await directory.create(recursive: true);
    if (_posix && !await _chmod('700', [directory.path])) {
      throw const FileSystemException('chmod 700 failed');
    }
    await _clean();
    final at = _now();
    String two(int n) => n.toString().padLeft(2, '0');
    final stamp =
        '${at.year}${two(at.month)}${two(at.day)}-'
        '${two(at.hour)}${two(at.minute)}${two(at.second)}';
    final id = _random.nextInt(1 << 32).toRadixString(16).padLeft(8, '0');
    final file = File(p.join(directory.path, 'paste-$stamp-$id.$ext'));
    // 0600 before any content goes in.
    await file.create(exclusive: true);
    if (_posix && !await _chmod('600', [file.path])) {
      await _delete(file);
      throw const FileSystemException('chmod 600 failed');
    }
    await file.writeAsBytes(bytes, flush: true);
    return file.path;
  }

  /// Deletes what is too old, then the oldest beyond the cap (room for the
  /// one about to be saved).
  Future<void> _clean() async {
    final files = <(File, DateTime)>[];
    try {
      await for (final entity in directory.list(followLinks: false)) {
        if (entity is! File) continue;
        files.add((entity, (await entity.stat()).modified));
      }
    } on FileSystemException {
      return;
    }
    final cutoff = _now().subtract(kPasteImageMaxAge);
    final keep = <(File, DateTime)>[];
    for (final f in files) {
      if (f.$2.isBefore(cutoff)) {
        await _delete(f.$1);
      } else {
        keep.add(f);
      }
    }
    keep.sort((a, b) => a.$2.compareTo(b.$2));
    while (keep.length >= kMaxPasteImageFiles) {
      await _delete(keep.removeAt(0).$1);
    }
  }

  /// Quit: nothing a closed app needs is left.
  Future<void> deleteAll() async {
    try {
      if (await directory.exists()) await directory.delete(recursive: true);
    } on FileSystemException {
      // Best effort.
    }
  }

  static Future<void> _delete(File file) async {
    try {
      await file.delete();
    } on FileSystemException {
      // Already gone.
    }
  }

  static Future<bool> _systemChmod(String mode, List<String> paths) async {
    try {
      final result = await Process.run('/bin/chmod', [mode, ...paths]);
      return result.exitCode == 0;
    } on ProcessException {
      return false;
    }
  }
}
```


Note the two decisions the tests pin: a `~` is escaped only at the start of a word (`/tmp/rm -rf ~` as one escaped word needs no `\~` after the escaped space — verified against zsh/bash rules), and Unicode (Vietnamese, CJK, emoji) is left as is.

### Task 9: Desktop — drag and drop files, paste a screenshot (D15–D17, D19) — **L · ⚠ MANUAL**

Drop files on a pane: their shell-escaped paths go in as a paste (bracketed when the program asked; no Enter). ⌘V with files on the clipboard (Finder ⌘C) pastes their paths; with only an image, it saves a temp file and pastes its path (text always wins over an image; files win over text). A pane on a server **refuses** both with a toast (no scp in A10, spec D17). Two native plugins join: `desktop_drop` and `pasteboard`.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/desktop/lib/src/terminal/{clipboard_media,pane_input}.dart`, `apps/desktop/lib/src/ui/terminal/pane_drop_target.dart`
- Modify: `apps/desktop/pubspec.yaml`, `apps/desktop/macos/Flutter/GeneratedPluginRegistrant.swift`, `apps/desktop/windows/flutter/generated_plugin_registrant.cc`, `apps/desktop/windows/flutter/generated_plugins.cmake`, `apps/desktop/macos/Podfile.lock`, `apps/desktop/lib/src/terminal/terminal_tabs.dart` (`InputNotice`), `apps/desktop/lib/src/ui/terminal/terminal_overlays.dart`, `apps/desktop/lib/src/ui/terminal/pane_tree_view.dart`, `apps/desktop/lib/src/ui/terminal/terminal_workspace_page.dart` (`_paste`), `apps/desktop/lib/l10n/app_{en,vi}.arb` (+ generated), `apps/desktop/test/support/fakes.dart`, `apps/desktop/test/support/config_workspace.dart`, `apps/desktop/test/ui/terminal_workspace_test.dart`, `apps/desktop/test/ui/terminal_shortcuts_test.dart`
- Test: `apps/desktop/test/ui/drop_and_image_test.dart`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Tasks 3, 8; `desktop_drop` (`DropTarget`, `DropDoneDetails.files[].path`, `DropEventDetails`), `pasteboard` (`Pasteboard.files()`, `Pasteboard.image`).
- Produces:
  - `abstract interface class ClipboardMedia { Future<List<String>> files(); Future<Uint8List?> image(); }`, `SystemClipboardMedia` (2 s limit, failure = nothing), `clipboardMediaProvider`, `pasteImageStoreProvider`.
  - `enum InputOutcome { sent, ssh, refusedName, imageTooLarge, imageFailed, gone }`, `paneInputProvider` → `PaneInput.insertPaths(int paneId, List<String> paths, {bool focus = false})`, `.pasteImage(int paneId, Uint8List)`, `static bool isRemote(PaneSession)`.
  - `enum InputNoticeKind { dropSsh, dropRefused, imageSsh, imageTooLarge, imageFailed }`, `final class InputNotice extends TabNotice` (the two SSH kinds are neutral toasts).
  - `PaneDropTarget({paneId, enabled, remote, child})` — outline + hint while hovering (keys `drop.hint`), cleared on exit, drop, window blur, disable.
  - Test harness: `pumpConfiguredWorkspace(..., ClipboardMedia clipboard = const EmptyClipboardMedia())`.

```dart
import 'dart:async';
import 'dart:io';
import 'package:desktop_drop/desktop_drop.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:shellx_agent/shellx_agent.dart';
import 'package:shellx_desktop/src/terminal/clipboard_media.dart';
import 'package:shellx_desktop/src/terminal/paste_image_store.dart';
import 'package:shellx_desktop/src/terminal/terminal_tabs.dart';
import 'package:shellx_desktop/src/ui/window_focus.dart';
import 'package:shellx_desktop/src/ui/window_router.dart';

import '../support/config_workspace.dart';

final class FakeMedia implements ClipboardMedia {
  List<String> fileList = const [];
  Uint8List? imageBytes;
  @override
  Future<List<String>> files() async => fileList;
  @override
  Future<Uint8List?> image() async => imageBytes;
}

final class FakeImageStore extends PasteImageStore {
  FakeImageStore() : super(Directory.systemTemp);
  final saved = <Uint8List>[];
  Object? error;
  @override
  Future<String> save(Uint8List bytes) async {
    if (error != null) throw error!;
    saved.add(bytes);
    return '/tmp/shellx-paste/paste-1.png';
  }
}

void mockClipboard(WidgetTester t, String? text) {
  t.binding.defaultBinaryMessenger.setMockMethodCallHandler(
    SystemChannels.platform,
    (call) async => call.method == 'Clipboard.getData'
        ? (text == null ? null : <String, Object?>{'text': text})
        : null,
  );
  addTearDown(
    () => t.binding.defaultBinaryMessenger.setMockMethodCallHandler(
      SystemChannels.platform,
      null,
    ),
  );
}

Future<void> pasteChord(WidgetTester t) async {
  await t.sendKeyDownEvent(LogicalKeyboardKey.metaLeft);
  await t.sendKeyEvent(LogicalKeyboardKey.keyV);
  await t.sendKeyUpEvent(LogicalKeyboardKey.metaLeft);
  await t.pump();
  await t.pump();
}

DropDoneDetails dropped(List<String> paths) => DropDoneDetails(
  files: [for (final p in paths) DropItemFile(p)],
  localPosition: Offset.zero,
  globalPosition: Offset.zero,
);

DropTarget targetOf(WidgetTester t, {int index = 0}) =>
    t.widgetList<DropTarget>(find.byType(DropTarget)).elementAt(index);

void main() {
  final mac = TargetPlatformVariant.only(TargetPlatform.macOS);

  testWidgets(
    'files dropped on the pane arrive escaped, with a space, no Enter',
    (t) async {
      final w = await pumpConfiguredWorkspace(t);
      final tab = w.container.read(terminalTabsProvider).selected!;
      targetOf(t).onDragDone!(
        dropped(['/Users/me/My Docs/a.png', '/Users/me/Tệp ảnh.txt']),
      );
      await t.pump();
      await t.pump();
      final writes = w.local.views[tab.focusedPaneId]!.writes.join();
      expect(writes, r'/Users/me/My\ Docs/a.png /Users/me/Tệp\ ảnh.txt ');
      expect(writes, isNot(contains('\r')));
      await disposeWorkspace(t, w.container);
    },
    variant: mac,
  );

  testWidgets('a program with bracketed paste gets the paths bracketed', (
    t,
  ) async {
    final w = await pumpConfiguredWorkspace(t);
    final tab = w.container.read(terminalTabsProvider).selected!;
    tab.terminal.write('\x1b[?2004h');
    targetOf(t).onDragDone!(dropped(['/a b']));
    await t.pump();
    await t.pump();
    expect(
      w.local.views[tab.focusedPaneId]!.writes.join(),
      '\x1b[200~/a\\ b \x1b[201~',
    );
    await disposeWorkspace(t, w.container);
  }, variant: mac);

  testWidgets('a name with a line break refuses the whole drop with a toast', (
    t,
  ) async {
    final w = await pumpConfiguredWorkspace(t);
    final tab = w.container.read(terminalTabsProvider).selected!;
    targetOf(t).onDragDone!(dropped(['/ok.txt', '/bad\nname.txt']));
    await t.pump();
    await t.pump();
    expect(w.local.views[tab.focusedPaneId]!.writes, isEmpty);
    expect(
      (w.container.read(terminalTabsProvider).notice as InputNotice).kind,
      InputNoticeKind.dropRefused,
    );
    await t.pump(const Duration(seconds: 7));
    await disposeWorkspace(t, w.container);
  }, variant: mac);

  testWidgets('hover shows the hint; leaving, a drop or losing the window '
      'removes it', (t) async {
    final w = await pumpConfiguredWorkspace(t);
    expect(find.byKey(const Key('drop.hint')), findsNothing);
    final details = DropEventDetails(
      localPosition: Offset.zero,
      globalPosition: Offset.zero,
    );
    targetOf(t).onDragEntered!(details);
    await t.pump();
    expect(find.byKey(const Key('drop.hint')), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('Drop to insert the path'), findsOneWidget);
    targetOf(t).onDragExited!(details);
    await t.pump();
    expect(find.byKey(const Key('drop.hint')), findsNothing);
    targetOf(t).onDragEntered!(details);
    await t.pump();
    w.container.read(windowFocusProvider.notifier).set(true);
    w.container.read(windowFocusProvider.notifier).set(false);
    await t.pump();
    expect(find.byKey(const Key('drop.hint')), findsNothing);
    targetOf(t).onDragEntered!(details);
    await t.pump();
    targetOf(t).onDragDone!(dropped(['/a']));
    await t.pump();
    expect(find.byKey(const Key('drop.hint')), findsNothing);
    await disposeWorkspace(t, w.container);
  }, variant: mac);

  testWidgets('no drops while a dialog is open', (t) async {
    final w = await pumpConfiguredWorkspace(t);
    expect(targetOf(t).enable, isTrue);
    unawaited(
      w.container
          .read(windowRouterProvider.notifier)
          .confirm(ConfirmRequest(title: 't', body: 'b', confirmLabel: 'ok')),
    );
    await t.pump();
    expect(targetOf(t).enable, isFalse);
    w.container.read(windowRouterProvider).confirm!.complete(false);
    await t.pump();
    await disposeWorkspace(t, w.container);
  }, variant: mac);

  testWidgets('a server pane refuses: a different hint, a toast, no text', (
    t,
  ) async {
    final w = await pumpConfiguredWorkspace(t);
    final ssh = SessionRemote(
      launch: SshLaunch(SshTarget.parse('devbox')!),
      connected: true,
    );
    w.local.add(w.local.summary(7, origin: SessionOrigin.desktop, remote: ssh));
    await t.pump();
    w.container.read(terminalTabsProvider.notifier).focusPane(7);
    await t.pump();
    await t.pump();
    final index = t.widgetList<DropTarget>(find.byType(DropTarget)).length - 1;
    final details = DropEventDetails(
      localPosition: Offset.zero,
      globalPosition: Offset.zero,
    );
    targetOf(t, index: index).onDragEntered!(details);
    await t.pump();
    expect(
      find.text('Files can’t be dropped into an SSH session'),
      findsOneWidget,
    );
    targetOf(t, index: index).onDragDone!(dropped(['/a']));
    await t.pump();
    await t.pump();
    expect(w.local.views[7]!.writes, isEmpty);
    expect(
      (w.container.read(terminalTabsProvider).notice as InputNotice).kind,
      InputNoticeKind.dropSsh,
    );
    await t.pump(const Duration(seconds: 7));
    await disposeWorkspace(t, w.container);
  }, variant: mac);

  group('the paste key', () {
    testWidgets('files on the clipboard win over their name as text', (
      t,
    ) async {
      final media = FakeMedia()..fileList = ['/Users/me/a b.png'];
      final w = await pumpConfiguredWorkspace(t, clipboard: media);
      mockClipboard(t, 'a b.png');
      final tab = w.container.read(terminalTabsProvider).selected!;
      await pasteChord(t);
      expect(
        w.local.views[tab.focusedPaneId]!.writes.join(),
        r'/Users/me/a\ b.png ',
      );
      await disposeWorkspace(t, w.container);
    }, variant: mac);

    testWidgets('text wins over an image', (t) async {
      final media = FakeMedia()..imageBytes = Uint8List.fromList([0x89, 0x50]);
      final store = FakeImageStore();
      final w = await pumpConfiguredWorkspace(
        t,
        clipboard: media,
        extra: [pasteImageStoreProvider.overrideWithValue(store)],
      );
      mockClipboard(t, 'hello');
      final tab = w.container.read(terminalTabsProvider).selected!;
      await pasteChord(t);
      expect(w.local.views[tab.focusedPaneId]!.writes.join(), 'hello');
      expect(store.saved, isEmpty);
      await disposeWorkspace(t, w.container);
    }, variant: mac);

    testWidgets('an image alone becomes a temp file and its path', (t) async {
      final media = FakeMedia()..imageBytes = Uint8List.fromList([0x89, 0x50]);
      final store = FakeImageStore();
      final w = await pumpConfiguredWorkspace(
        t,
        clipboard: media,
        extra: [pasteImageStoreProvider.overrideWithValue(store)],
      );
      mockClipboard(t, null);
      final tab = w.container.read(terminalTabsProvider).selected!;
      await pasteChord(t);
      expect(store.saved, hasLength(1));
      expect(
        w.local.views[tab.focusedPaneId]!.writes.join(),
        '/tmp/shellx-paste/paste-1.png ',
      );
      await disposeWorkspace(t, w.container);
    }, variant: mac);

    testWidgets('an image over 20 MB, or an unreadable one, is a toast', (
      t,
    ) async {
      final media = FakeMedia()..imageBytes = Uint8List.fromList([0x89, 0x50]);
      final store = FakeImageStore()..error = const PasteImageTooLarge();
      final w = await pumpConfiguredWorkspace(
        t,
        clipboard: media,
        extra: [pasteImageStoreProvider.overrideWithValue(store)],
      );
      mockClipboard(t, null);
      await pasteChord(t);
      InputNoticeKind kind() =>
          (w.container.read(terminalTabsProvider).notice as InputNotice).kind;
      expect(kind(), InputNoticeKind.imageTooLarge);
      store.error = const FileSystemException('disk full');
      await pasteChord(t);
      expect(kind(), InputNoticeKind.imageFailed);
      await t.pump(const Duration(seconds: 7));
      await disposeWorkspace(t, w.container);
    }, variant: mac);

    testWidgets('an image into a server pane is refused and nothing is saved', (
      t,
    ) async {
      final media = FakeMedia()..imageBytes = Uint8List.fromList([0x89, 0x50]);
      final store = FakeImageStore();
      final w = await pumpConfiguredWorkspace(
        t,
        clipboard: media,
        extra: [pasteImageStoreProvider.overrideWithValue(store)],
      );
      final ssh = SessionRemote(
        launch: SshLaunch(SshTarget.parse('devbox')!),
        connected: true,
      );
      w.local.add(
        w.local.summary(7, origin: SessionOrigin.desktop, remote: ssh),
      );
      await t.pump();
      w.container.read(terminalTabsProvider.notifier).focusPane(7);
      await t.pump();
      mockClipboard(t, null);
      await pasteChord(t);
      expect(store.saved, isEmpty);
      expect(
        (w.container.read(terminalTabsProvider).notice as InputNotice).kind,
        InputNoticeKind.imageSsh,
      );
      await t.pump(const Duration(seconds: 7));
      await disposeWorkspace(t, w.container);
    }, variant: mac);

    testWidgets('text into a server pane still pastes', (t) async {
      final w = await pumpConfiguredWorkspace(t);
      final ssh = SessionRemote(
        launch: SshLaunch(SshTarget.parse('devbox')!),
        connected: true,
      );
      w.local.add(
        w.local.summary(7, origin: SessionOrigin.desktop, remote: ssh),
      );
      await t.pump();
      w.container.read(terminalTabsProvider.notifier).focusPane(7);
      await t.pump();
      mockClipboard(t, 'ls');
      await pasteChord(t);
      expect(w.local.views[7]!.writes.join(), 'ls');
      await disposeWorkspace(t, w.container);
    }, variant: mac);
  });
}
```

```dart
import 'dart:async';
import 'dart:io';
import 'dart:typed_data';

import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:pasteboard/pasteboard.dart';

import 'paste_image_store.dart';

/// What the system clipboard holds besides text (A10 D15). Flutter's own
/// `Clipboard` reads text only.
abstract interface class ClipboardMedia {
  /// Absolute paths of files copied in Finder / Explorer; empty when none.
  Future<List<String>> files();

  /// An image on the clipboard (PNG, JPEG, BMP, ...); null when none.
  Future<Uint8List?> image();
}

/// `pasteboard` (macOS and Windows). A plugin that fails or hangs is "no
/// files / no image": a paste never waits on it for more than 2 s.
final class SystemClipboardMedia implements ClipboardMedia {
  const SystemClipboardMedia();

  static const _limit = Duration(seconds: 2);

  @override
  Future<List<String>> files() async {
    try {
      return await Pasteboard.files().timeout(_limit);
    } on Object {
      return const [];
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<Uint8List?> image() async {
    try {
      return await Pasteboard.image.timeout(_limit);
    } on Object {
      return null;
    }
  }
}

final clipboardMediaProvider = Provider<ClipboardMedia>(
  (ref) => const SystemClipboardMedia(),
);

/// Pasted images live in the system temp folder (A10 D15).
final pasteImageStoreProvider = Provider<PasteImageStore>((ref) {
  final store = PasteImageStore(Directory.systemTemp);
  return store;
});
```

File: `apps/desktop/lib/src/terminal/pane_input.dart`

```dart
import 'dart:io';
import 'dart:typed_data';

import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';

import '../ui/window_focus.dart' show terminalFocusRequestProvider;
import 'clipboard_media.dart';
import 'pane_paste.dart';
import 'paste_image_store.dart';
import 'shell_escape.dart';
import 'terminal_tabs.dart';

/// What happened to a drop or an image paste (A10 D15–D17).
enum InputOutcome { sent, ssh, refusedName, imageTooLarge, imageFailed, gone }

/// A10 D17: pane input that is not text. Every refusal is a toast.
final paneInputProvider = Provider<PaneInput>((ref) => PaneInput(ref));

class PaneInput {
  PaneInput(this._ref);
  final Ref _ref;

  PaneSession? _pane(int id) =>
      _ref.read(terminalTabsProvider).tabOfSession(id)?.panes[id];

  /// A pane of a server (SSH, or tmux on a server): its files are not ours.
  static bool isRemote(PaneSession pane) => pane.remote?.launch.host != null;

  void _notice(InputNoticeKind kind) =>
      _ref.read(terminalTabsProvider.notifier).showNotice(InputNotice(kind));

  /// Writes [paths], shell-escaped, into pane [paneId] as a paste (so a
  /// program that asked for bracketed paste gets them bracketed, and
  /// nothing runs): each followed by a space, no Enter. [focus] gives the
  /// pane the keyboard (a drop).
  Future<InputOutcome> insertPaths(
    int paneId,
    List<String> paths, {
    bool focus = false,
  }) async {
    final pane = _pane(paneId);
    if (pane == null || pane.exited || paths.isEmpty) return InputOutcome.gone;
    if (isRemote(pane)) {
      _notice(InputNoticeKind.dropSsh);
      return InputOutcome.ssh;
    }
    final String text;
    try {
      text = escapePaths(
        paths,
        shellFamilyOf(pane.shell, windows: Platform.isWindows),
      );
    } on FormatException {
      _notice(InputNoticeKind.dropRefused);
      return InputOutcome.refusedName;
    }
    if (focus) {
      _ref.read(terminalTabsProvider.notifier).focusPane(paneId);
      _ref.read(terminalFocusRequestProvider.notifier).request();
    }
    await _ref
        .read(pasteSenderProvider)
        .send(
          paneId,
          text,
          PanePasteSink(
            () => _ref.read(terminalTabsProvider),
            paneId,
            pane.terminal,
          ),
        );
    return InputOutcome.sent;
  }

  /// Saves the clipboard image [bytes] to a private temp file and pastes
  /// its path (A10 D15).
  Future<InputOutcome> pasteImage(int paneId, Uint8List bytes) async {
    final pane = _pane(paneId);
    if (pane == null || pane.exited) return InputOutcome.gone;
    if (isRemote(pane)) {
      _notice(InputNoticeKind.imageSsh);
      return InputOutcome.ssh;
    }
    final String path;
    try {
      path = await _ref.read(pasteImageStoreProvider).save(bytes);
    } on PasteImageTooLarge {
      _notice(InputNoticeKind.imageTooLarge);
      return InputOutcome.imageTooLarge;
    } on Object {
      _notice(InputNoticeKind.imageFailed);
      return InputOutcome.imageFailed;
    }
    return insertPaths(paneId, [path]);
  }
}
```

```dart
/// A10 D15–D17: why a drop or an image paste did nothing.
enum InputNoticeKind {
  dropSsh,
  dropRefused,
  imageSsh,
  imageTooLarge,
  imageFailed,
}

/// A10: a file drop or an image paste was refused or failed. The two SSH
/// kinds are neutral (information); the others are errors.
final class InputNotice extends TabNotice {
  const InputNotice(this.kind);
  final InputNoticeKind kind;
}
```

File: `apps/desktop/lib/src/ui/terminal/pane_drop_target.dart`

```dart
import 'dart:async';

import 'package:desktop_drop/desktop_drop.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';

import '../../../l10n/app_localizations.dart';
import '../../terminal/pane_input.dart';
import '../theme/shellx_desktop_theme.dart';
import '../window_focus.dart';

/// A10 D16: files dropped on a pane become their paths on its command line
/// (shell-escaped, no Enter). While files are over the pane it is outlined
/// with a hint; the hint goes when they leave, are dropped, the window
/// loses focus, or the pane stops accepting (hidden, a dialog open, ended).
class PaneDropTarget extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const PaneDropTarget({
    super.key,
    required this.paneId,
    required this.enabled,
    required this.remote,
    required this.child,
  });

  final int paneId;

  /// False for a pane that is hidden (another tab, under a zoom), ended, or
  /// behind a dialog: `DropTarget` keeps receiving events while invisible.
  final bool enabled;

  /// A pane on a server: it shows the refusal while files hover (D17).
  final bool remote;
  final Widget child;

  @override
  ConsumerState<PaneDropTarget> createState() => _PaneDropTargetState();
}

class _PaneDropTargetState extends ConsumerState<PaneDropTarget> {
  bool _over = false;

  void _set(bool over) {
    if (_over != over && mounted) setState(() => _over = over);
  }

  @override
  void didUpdateWidget(PaneDropTarget old) {
    super.didUpdateWidget(old);
    if (!widget.enabled) _over = false;
  }

  void _done(DropDoneDetails details) {
    _set(false);
    final paths = [
      for (final f in details.files)
        if (f.path.isNotEmpty) f.path,
    ];
    if (paths.isEmpty) return;
    unawaited(
      ref
          .read(paneInputProvider)
          .insertPaths(widget.paneId, paths, focus: true),
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    // The window lost the keyboard (Cmd-Tab mid-drag): no stuck hint.
    ref.listen<bool>(windowFocusProvider, (_, focused) {
      if (!focused) _set(false);
    });
    final l10n = AppLocalizations.of(context);
    return DropTarget(
      enable: widget.enabled,
      onDragEntered: (_) => _set(true),
      onDragExited: (_) => _set(false),
      onDragDone: _done,
      child: Stack(
        fit: StackFit.passthrough,
        children: [
          widget.child,
          if (_over && widget.enabled)
            Positioned.fill(
              child: IgnorePointer(
                child: Semantics(
                  liveRegion: true,
                  label: widget.remote ? l10n.dropHintSsh : l10n.dropHint,
                  child: DecoratedBox(
                    key: const Key('drop.hint'),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: ShellxDesktopColors.accent.withValues(alpha: 0.08),
                      border: Border.all(
                        color: widget.remote
                            ? ShellxDesktopColors.warn
                            : ShellxDesktopColors.accent,
                        width: 2,
                      ),
                    ),
                    child: Align(
                      alignment: Alignment.topCenter,
                      child: Container(
                        margin: const EdgeInsets.only(top: ShellxSpace.s12),
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                          horizontal: ShellxSpace.s12,
                          vertical: ShellxSpace.s4,
                        ),
                        decoration: BoxDecoration(
                          color: ShellxDesktopColors.surface,
                          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(ShellxRadius.r8),
                          border: Border.all(color: ShellxDesktopColors.border),
                        ),
                        child: Text(
                          widget.remote ? l10n.dropHintSsh : l10n.dropHint,
                          style: ShellxText.body.copyWith(
                            color: ShellxDesktopColors.textTitle,
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

`terminal_workspace_page.dart` — `_paste` gains the two other sources (imports `../../terminal/clipboard_media.dart`, `../../terminal/pane_input.dart`); replace the version of Task 3 with:

```dart
  Future<void> _paste() async {
    final tab = ref.read(terminalTabsProvider).selected;
    if (tab == null) return;
    final paneId = tab.focusedPaneId;
    final terminal = tab.focused.terminal;
    final media = ref.read(clipboardMediaProvider);
    final input = ref.read(paneInputProvider);
    // A10 D15: files copied in Finder / Explorer first (their name is also
    // on the clipboard as text), then text, then an image.
    final files = await media.files();
    if (!mounted) return;
    if (files.isNotEmpty) {
      await input.insertPaths(paneId, files);
      return;
    }
    final data = await Clipboard.getData(Clipboard.kTextPlain);
    final text = data?.text;
    if (!mounted) return;
    if (text == null || text.isEmpty) {
      final image = await media.image();
      if (image != null && mounted) await input.pasteImage(paneId, image);
      return;
    }
    final sink = PanePasteSink(
      () => ref.read(terminalTabsProvider),
      paneId,
      terminal,
    );
    // An exited pane closes on Enter (UI §2.6): a paste must not do that.
    if (!sink.alive) return;
    final l10n = AppLocalizations.of(context);
    final outcome = await ref
        .read(pasteSenderProvider)
        .send(
          paneId,
          text,
          sink,
          confirm: (bytes) => ref
              .read(windowRouterProvider.notifier)
              .confirm(
                ConfirmRequest(
                  title: l10n.pasteLargeTitle(
                    formatByteSize(bytes, locale: l10n.localeName),
                  ),
                  body: l10n.pasteLargeBody,
                  confirmLabel: l10n.pasteLargeAction,
                  destructive: false,
                ),
              ),
        );
    if (outcome == PasteOutcome.tooLarge && mounted) {
      ref
          .read(terminalTabsProvider.notifier)
          .showNotice(const PasteTooLargeNotice());
    }
```

Pins the invariants of spec D23 with widget tests; on the first run only **F3** failed (when the window became active again the keyboard stayed on the workspace scope) and gets a three-line fix. N1 pins what spec D24 found already working (a background tab's title follows its session; an unread notice names the pane that sent it).

**Files:**
- Modify: `apps/desktop/lib/src/ui/terminal/terminal_workspace_page.dart` (F3)
- Test: `apps/desktop/test/ui/focus_invariants_test.dart`, `apps/desktop/test/ui/focus_dialogs_test.dart`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `windowFocusProvider`, `pumpConfiguredWorkspace`, `ShellxDesktopApp` (the dialog host).
- Produces: nothing new; F6 (a CLI command never steals focus) is already pinned by `test/cli/ui_command_handler_test.dart` ("split: any tab, … keeps focus and selection unless …", "new tab: not selected by default …") and `ui_command_handler_ssh_test.dart`.

File: `apps/desktop/test/ui/focus_invariants_test.dart`

```dart
import 'dart:async';

import 'package:flutter/gestures.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:shellx_agent/shellx_agent.dart';
import 'package:shellx_desktop/src/terminal/pane_layout.dart';
import 'package:shellx_desktop/src/terminal/tab_attention.dart';
import 'package:shellx_desktop/src/terminal/terminal_tabs.dart';
import 'package:shellx_desktop/src/ui/window_focus.dart';
import 'package:shellx_desktop/src/ui/window_router.dart';
import 'package:xterm/xterm.dart';

import '../support/config_workspace.dart';

/// A10 D23: the focus invariants F1–F7. Each is one test; a red one is a
/// bug to fix, not a test to loosen.
String? focusLabel() => FocusManager.instance.primaryFocus?.debugLabel;

Future<void> chord(
  WidgetTester t,
  List<LogicalKeyboardKey> held,
  LogicalKeyboardKey key,
) async {
  for (final k in held) {
    await t.sendKeyDownEvent(k);
  }
  await t.sendKeyEvent(key);
  for (final k in held.reversed) {
    await t.sendKeyUpEvent(k);
  }
  await t.pump();
  await t.pump();
}

const cmd = LogicalKeyboardKey.metaLeft;
const shift = LogicalKeyboardKey.shiftLeft;

void main() {
  final mac = TargetPlatformVariant.only(TargetPlatform.macOS);

  testWidgets('F1: only the selected tab\'s focused pane owns the focus node', (
    t,
  ) async {
    final w = await pumpConfiguredWorkspace(t);
    final tabs = w.container.read(terminalTabsProvider.notifier);
    await tabs.openTab(); // tab 2, selected
    await t.pumpAndSettle();
    final views = t
        .widgetList<TerminalView>(
          find.byType(TerminalView, skipOffstage: false),
        )
        .toList();
    expect(views, hasLength(2));
    expect(views.where((v) => v.focusNode != null), hasLength(1));
    final selected = w.container.read(terminalTabsProvider).selected!;
    expect(
      views.singleWhere((v) => v.focusNode != null).terminal,
      same(selected.terminal),
    );
    await disposeWorkspace(t, w.container);
  }, variant: mac);

  testWidgets('F1: a pane hidden by a zoom has no focus node either', (
    t,
  ) async {
    final w = await pumpConfiguredWorkspace(t);
    final tabs = w.container.read(terminalTabsProvider.notifier);
    await tabs.split(SplitAxis.row);
    await t.pump();
    tabs.toggleZoom();
    await t.pump();
    final tab = w.container.read(terminalTabsProvider).selected!;
    final owner = t
        .widgetList<TerminalView>(find.byType(TerminalView))
        .where((v) => v.focusNode != null)
        .toList();
    expect(owner, hasLength(1));
    expect(owner.single.terminal, same(tab.terminal));
    await disposeWorkspace(t, w.container);
  }, variant: mac);

  testWidgets('F2: the command palette closing gives the keyboard back', (
    t,
  ) async {
    final w = await pumpConfiguredWorkspace(t);
    await chord(t, [cmd, shift], LogicalKeyboardKey.keyP);
    expect(focusLabel(), isNot('terminal'));
    await t.sendKeyEvent(LogicalKeyboardKey.escape);
    await t.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(focusLabel(), 'terminal');
    await disposeWorkspace(t, w.container);
  }, variant: mac);

  testWidgets('F2: the notifications panel closing gives the keyboard back', (
    t,
  ) async {
    final w = await pumpConfiguredWorkspace(t);
    await chord(t, [cmd, shift], LogicalKeyboardKey.keyI);
    await t.sendKeyEvent(LogicalKeyboardKey.escape);
    await t.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(focusLabel(), 'terminal');
    await disposeWorkspace(t, w.container);
  }, variant: mac);

  testWidgets('F2: the find bar closing gives the keyboard back', (t) async {
    final w = await pumpConfiguredWorkspace(t);
    await chord(t, [cmd], LogicalKeyboardKey.keyF);
    expect(focusLabel(), 'find');
    await t.sendKeyEvent(LogicalKeyboardKey.escape);
    await t.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(focusLabel(), 'terminal');
    await disposeWorkspace(t, w.container);
  }, variant: mac);

  testWidgets('F3: the window coming back puts the keyboard in the terminal', (
    t,
  ) async {
    final w = await pumpConfiguredWorkspace(t);
    w.container.read(windowFocusProvider.notifier).set(true);
    await t.pump();
    // Whatever held it went away while the window was in the background.
    FocusManager.instance.primaryFocus?.unfocus();
    await t.pump();
    expect(focusLabel(), isNot('terminal'));
    w.container.read(windowFocusProvider.notifier).set(false);
    w.container.read(windowFocusProvider.notifier).set(true);
    await t.pump();
    await t.pump();
    expect(focusLabel(), 'terminal');
    await disposeWorkspace(t, w.container);
  }, variant: mac);

  testWidgets('F3: coming back leaves a field the user is typing in alone', (
    t,
  ) async {
    final w = await pumpConfiguredWorkspace(t);
    await chord(t, [cmd], LogicalKeyboardKey.keyF);
    expect(focusLabel(), 'find');
    w.container.read(windowFocusProvider.notifier).set(false);
    w.container.read(windowFocusProvider.notifier).set(true);
    await t.pump();
    await t.pump();
    expect(focusLabel(), 'find');
    await disposeWorkspace(t, w.container);
  }, variant: mac);

  testWidgets('F4: a click on the other pane focuses it first time', (t) async {
    final w = await pumpConfiguredWorkspace(t);
    final tabs = w.container.read(terminalTabsProvider.notifier);
    await tabs.split(SplitAxis.row);
    await t.pump();
    final tab = w.container.read(terminalTabsProvider).selected!;
    final other = tab.paneOrder.firstWhere((id) => id != tab.focusedPaneId);
    final view = t
        .widgetList<TerminalView>(find.byType(TerminalView))
        .firstWhere((v) => identical(v.terminal, tab.panes[other]!.terminal));
    await t.tapAt(
      t.getCenter(find.byWidget(view)),
      kind: PointerDeviceKind.mouse,
    );
    await t.pump(const Duration(milliseconds: 400)); // xterm's double-tap timer
    expect(
      w.container.read(terminalTabsProvider).selected!.focusedPaneId,
      other,
    );
    expect(focusLabel(), 'terminal');
    await disposeWorkspace(t, w.container);
  }, variant: mac);

  testWidgets('F5: a click on a sidebar row puts the keyboard in that tab', (
    t,
  ) async {
    final w = await pumpConfiguredWorkspace(t);
    final tabs = w.container.read(terminalTabsProvider.notifier);
    await tabs.openTab();
    await t.pumpAndSettle();
    final first = w.container.read(terminalTabsProvider).tabs.first;
    await t.tap(find.byKey(ValueKey('tab.${first.id}')));
    await t.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(w.container.read(terminalTabsProvider).selectedId, first.id);
    expect(focusLabel(), 'terminal');
    await disposeWorkspace(t, w.container);
  }, variant: mac);

  testWidgets('F7: the first terminal has the keyboard without a keystroke', (
    t,
  ) async {
    final w = await pumpConfiguredWorkspace(t);
    expect(focusLabel(), 'terminal');
    await disposeWorkspace(t, w.container);
  }, variant: mac);

  testWidgets('N1: a background tab\'s title follows its session; an unread '
      'notice names the pane that sent it, not the one in front', (t) async {
    final w = await pumpConfiguredWorkspace(t);
    final tabs = w.container.read(terminalTabsProvider.notifier);
    w.container.read(tabAttentionProvider);
    final first = w.container.read(terminalTabsProvider).selected!;
    await tabs.openTab();
    await t.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(w.container.read(terminalTabsProvider).selectedId, isNot(first.id));
    w.local.replace(
      w.local.summary(first.focusedPaneId, title: 'vim notes.md'),
    );
    w.local.notice(
      first.focusedPaneId,
      SessionNoticeKind.message,
      body: 'done',
    );
    await t.pump();
    await t.pump();
    final tab = w.container
        .read(terminalTabsProvider)
        .tabs
        .firstWhere((x) => x.id == first.id);
    expect(tab.panes[first.focusedPaneId]!.title, 'vim notes.md');
    expect(w.container.read(tabAttentionProvider).unreadPanes(first.id), {
      first.focusedPaneId,
    });
    await disposeWorkspace(t, w.container);
  }, variant: mac);
}
```

```dart
import 'dart:async';

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:shellx_desktop/src/agent/agent_controller.dart';
import 'package:shellx_desktop/src/agent/desktop_services.dart';
import 'package:shellx_desktop/src/terminal/local_sessions_provider.dart';
import 'package:shellx_desktop/src/terminal/terminal_tabs.dart';
import 'package:shellx_desktop/src/ui/app.dart';
import 'package:shellx_desktop/src/ui/terminal/ssh_connect_dialog.dart';
import 'package:shellx_desktop/src/ui/window_router.dart';

import '../support/config_harness.dart';
import '../support/fake_local_sessions.dart';
import '../support/fakes.dart';
import '../support/memory_config_fs.dart';

/// A10 D23 F2 for the dialogs, which only the whole app (not the bare
/// workspace page) hosts. Same setup as `ssh_connect_route_test.dart`.
late ProviderContainer container;

String? focusLabel() => FocusManager.instance.primaryFocus?.debugLabel;

Future<void> pumpApp(WidgetTester tester) async {
  final h = TestHarness(settings: {'onboarding_done': true});
  container = ProviderContainer(
    overrides: [
      desktopServicesProvider.overrideWithValue(h.services()),
      localSessionsProvider.overrideWithValue(FakeLocalSessions()),
      ...configOverrides(MemoryConfigFs()),
    ],
  );
  await h.seedSignedIn();
  await container
      .read(agentControllerProvider.notifier)
      .bootstrap(watchForWake: false);
  await loadConfig(container);
  container.read(windowRouterProvider.notifier).showTerminal();
  tester.view.physicalSize = const Size(1100, 720);
  tester.view.devicePixelRatio = 1;
  addTearDown(tester.view.reset);
  await tester.pumpWidget(
    UncontrolledProviderScope(
      container: container,
      child: const ShellxDesktopApp(locale: Locale('en')),
    ),
  );
  await container.read(terminalTabsProvider.notifier).openTab();
  await tester.pumpAndSettle();
}

void testApp(String description, Future<void> Function(WidgetTester) body) =>
    testWidgets(
      description,
      variant: TargetPlatformVariant.only(TargetPlatform.macOS),
      (tester) async {
        await pumpApp(tester);
        try {
          await body(tester);
        } finally {
          await tester.pumpWidget(const SizedBox());
          container.dispose();
        }
      },
    );

void main() {
  testApp('F2: the connect dialog closing gives the keyboard back', (
    tester,
  ) async {
    expect(focusLabel(), 'terminal');
    await tester.sendKeyDownEvent(LogicalKeyboardKey.metaLeft);
    await tester.sendKeyDownEvent(LogicalKeyboardKey.shiftLeft);
    await tester.sendKeyEvent(LogicalKeyboardKey.keyK);
    await tester.sendKeyUpEvent(LogicalKeyboardKey.shiftLeft);
    await tester.sendKeyUpEvent(LogicalKeyboardKey.metaLeft);
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.byType(SshConnectDialog), findsOneWidget);
    expect(focusLabel(), isNot('terminal'));
    await tester.sendKeyEvent(LogicalKeyboardKey.escape);
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.byType(SshConnectDialog), findsNothing);
    expect(focusLabel(), 'terminal');
  });

  testApp('F2: a confirmation answered gives the keyboard back', (
    tester,
  ) async {
    final ask = container
        .read(windowRouterProvider.notifier)
        .confirm(
          ConfirmRequest(title: 'Sure?', body: 'Body', confirmLabel: 'Yes'),
        );
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.text('Sure?'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(focusLabel(), isNot('terminal'));
    await tester.tap(find.text('Yes'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(await ask, isTrue);
    expect(focusLabel(), 'terminal');
  });

  testApp('F2: a confirmation cancelled with Esc gives the keyboard back', (
    tester,
  ) async {
    final ask = container
        .read(windowRouterProvider.notifier)
        .confirm(
          ConfirmRequest(title: 'Sure?', body: 'Body', confirmLabel: 'Yes'),
        );
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    await tester.sendKeyEvent(LogicalKeyboardKey.escape);
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(await ask, isFalse);
    expect(focusLabel(), 'terminal');
  });
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Fix F3.** In `terminal_workspace_page.dart`'s `build`, next to the other `ref.listen`s (before the `terminalFocusRequestProvider` listener):

```dart
    // A10 D23 F3: the window is active again. If whatever had the keyboard
    // went away meanwhile (it fell back to the workspace scope itself, or
    // to nothing), the keyboard goes to the terminal; a field the user is
    // typing in keeps it.
    ref.listen<bool>(windowFocusProvider, (_, focused) {
      if (!focused) return;
      final current = FocusManager.instance.primaryFocus;
      if (current == null || current is FocusScopeNode) focusTerminal();
    });
```

### Task 11: Desktop — restore hardening: layout generations and a smarter choice (D25, D26) — **M · ⚠ MANUAL**

`layout.json` is already written atomically, a corrupt one goes to `.bak`, and nothing is written until a restore has finished. What was missing (cmux #14824, #14788): a layout that is **empty after a crash** — the user closed every tab, the app died — overwrote the last good state, and a damaged `layout.json` meant starting from nothing. Now each run that starts with tabs keeps a copy (`layout.json.1`, then `.2`), and `pickSnapshot` chooses: a layout with tabs; else (empty **and** not a clean exit) the last run that had tabs; else the first readable generation; an empty layout after a **clean** quit is respected.

**Files:**
- Modify: `apps/desktop/lib/src/restore/restore_store.dart`
- Test: `apps/desktop/test/restore/restore_store_rotation_test.dart`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `RestoreSnapshot` (`tryParse`, `sameLayoutAs`, `copyWith`, `tabs`, `cleanExit`), the store's `_atomic` writer (0600, temp + rename).
- Produces: `RestoreLoad.recovered` (new field, default false); top-level `({RestoreSnapshot? snapshot, bool recovered}) pickSnapshot({required RestoreSnapshot? current, required List<RestoreSnapshot?> generations})` — a recovered snapshot is returned **unclean** so the A5a strip shows; `RestoreStore.loadLayout` rotates and recovers.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**


File: `apps/desktop/test/restore/restore_store_rotation_test.dart`

```dart
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:shellx_desktop/src/restore/restore_snapshot.dart';
import 'package:shellx_desktop/src/restore/restore_store.dart';

final k1 = 'a' * 32, k2 = 'b' * 32, k3 = 'c' * 32;

RestoreSnapshot snap(String key, {bool clean = false, bool empty = false}) =>
    RestoreSnapshot(
      savedAt: DateTime.utc(2026, 10, 7),
      cleanExit: clean,
      selectedTab: empty ? null : key,
      workspaces: [
        SavedWorkspace(id: 'default', tabs: empty ? [] : [key]),
      ],
      tabs: empty
          ? []
          : [
              SavedTab(
                key: key,
                focused: key,
                layout: SavedPane(key: key, cwd: '/tmp'),
              ),
            ],
    );

void main() {
  group('pickSnapshot (A10 D25)', () {
    test('a layout with tabs always wins', () {
      final r = pickSnapshot(current: snap(k1), generations: [snap(k2)]);
      expect(r.snapshot!.tabs.single.key, k1);
      expect(r.recovered, isFalse);
    });

    test('empty after a crash gives way to the last run that had tabs', () {
      final r = pickSnapshot(
        current: snap(k1, empty: true),
        generations: [snap(k2, clean: true), null],
      );
      expect(r.snapshot!.tabs.single.key, k2);
      expect(r.recovered, isTrue);
      expect(r.snapshot!.cleanExit, isFalse, reason: 'the strip shows');
    });

    test('empty after a clean quit is respected', () {
      final r = pickSnapshot(
        current: snap(k1, empty: true, clean: true),
        generations: [snap(k2)],
      );
      expect(r.snapshot!.tabs, isEmpty);
      expect(r.recovered, isFalse);
    });

    test('unreadable: the first generation that has tabs', () {
      final r = pickSnapshot(
        current: null,
        generations: [snap(k2, empty: true), snap(k3)],
      );
      expect(r.snapshot!.tabs.single.key, k3);
      expect(r.recovered, isTrue);
    });

    test('nothing at all', () {
      final r = pickSnapshot(current: null, generations: [null, null]);
      expect(r.snapshot, isNull);
      expect(r.recovered, isFalse);
    });
  });

  group('RestoreStore generations', () {
    late Directory root;
    late RestoreStore store;
    final logs = <String>[];

    setUp(() async {
      root = await Directory.systemTemp.createTemp('restore_rotation_test');
      logs.clear();
      store = RestoreStore(
        Directory('${root.path}/restore'),
        excludeFromBackup: (_) async {},
        log: logs.add,
      );
      await store.prepare();
    });
    tearDown(() => root.delete(recursive: true));

    String path(String name) => '${store.directory.path}/$name';

    test('each run that starts with tabs shifts the generations', () async {
      await store.writeLayout(snap(k1));
      await store.loadLayout(); // run 2 starts from k1
      expect(File(path('layout.json.1')).existsSync(), isTrue);
      expect(File(path('layout.json.2')).existsSync(), isFalse);

      await store.writeLayout(snap(k2));
      await store.loadLayout(); // run 3 starts from k2
      expect(
        RestoreSnapshot.tryParse(
          File(path('layout.json.1')).readAsStringSync(),
        )!.tabs.single.key,
        k2,
      );
      expect(
        RestoreSnapshot.tryParse(
          File(path('layout.json.2')).readAsStringSync(),
        )!.tabs.single.key,
        k1,
      );

      expect(load.snapshot!.tabs.single.key, k1);

      'a corrupt layout falls back to a generation and keeps .bak',
      () async {

        final load = await store.loadLayout();
        expect(load.corrupt, isTrue);
        expect(load.recovered, isTrue);

      },

      File(path('layout.json')).writeAsStringSync('garbage');

      expect(load.snapshot!.tabs.single.key, k1);

    test('a clean empty layout is not replaced by a generation', () async {

      await store.loadLayout();
      await store.writeLayout(snap(k1, empty: true, clean: true));
      final load = await store.loadLayout();
      expect(load.recovered, isFalse);
      expect(load.snapshot!.tabs, isEmpty);
    });
  });
}
```

```diff
 const int kRestoreMaxLayoutBytes = 1048576;
 
 final class RestoreLoad {
-  const RestoreLoad({this.snapshot, this.corrupt = false});
+  const RestoreLoad({
+    this.snapshot,

   final RestoreSnapshot? snapshot;
 
   /// The file existed but was not ours (D11); it is now `layout.json.bak`.
   final bool corrupt;
+
+  /// A10 D25: [snapshot] comes from an earlier generation
+  /// (`layout.json.1` / `.2`) because `layout.json` was unreadable, or empty
+  /// after a crash. It is marked unclean, so the A5a strip shows.
+  final bool recovered;
+}
+
+/// A10 D25: which layout to restore. [current] is `layout.json` parsed
+/// (null: absent or unreadable), [generations] are `.1`, `.2` parsed (null
+/// for a missing or unreadable one).
+///
+/// 1. [current] with at least one tab;
+/// 2. [current] with none after a crash (`cleanExit` false), while the
+///    newest generation has tabs: that generation (closing every tab and
+///    quitting cleanly leaves a clean empty layout, which is respected);
+/// 3. [current] unreadable: the first generation with tabs;
+/// 4. otherwise [current] (possibly empty, possibly null).
+({RestoreSnapshot? snapshot, bool recovered}) pickSnapshot({
+  required RestoreSnapshot? current,
+  required List<RestoreSnapshot?> generations,
+}) {
+  if (current != null && current.tabs.isNotEmpty) {
+    return (snapshot: current, recovered: false);
+  }
+  final fallback = generations.where((g) => g != null && g.tabs.isNotEmpty);
+  if (current == null) {
+    return fallback.isEmpty
+        ? (snapshot: null, recovered: false)
+        : (
+            snapshot: fallback.first!.copyWith(cleanExit: false),
+            recovered: true,
+          );
+  }
+  if (!current.cleanExit && generations.isNotEmpty) {
+    final newest = generations.first;
+    if (newest != null && newest.tabs.isNotEmpty) {
+      return (snapshot: newest.copyWith(cleanExit: false), recovered: true);
+    }
+  }
+  return (snapshot: current, recovered: false);
 }

```


Why recovery marks the snapshot unclean (`copyWith(cleanExit: false)`): `RestorePersistence` shows the abnormal-end strip only for `!snapshot.cleanExit` and also uses the flag to decide whether to write its "unclean" marker back — a recovered layout must read as unclean in both places.

### Task 12: Desktop — a saved window frame is clamped to the display (D27) — **S · ⚠ MANUAL**

`restorableBounds` only checked that the window's centre was on some display. Now the home display is the one holding the centre, else the one holding the title bar's corner; the frame is shrunk to its visible area and moved inside it.

**Files:**
- Modify: `apps/desktop/lib/src/ui/window_bounds.dart`, `apps/desktop/test/ui/window_bounds_test.dart`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes/Produces: `Rect? restorableBounds(Rect? saved, List<Rect> displays)` (same signature; `displays` are visible areas — `window_host.dart` already passes `screen_retriever`'s `visiblePosition & visibleSize`).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests.** In `test/ui/window_bounds_test.dart`, in the group "saved bounds must still be on a display", replace the test "only an edge on a display (centre off every display): null" with:

- [ ] **Step 2: Run to see them fail**

Run: `cd apps/desktop && PATH=~/fvm/versions/3.41.4/bin:$PATH flutter test test/ui/window_bounds_test.dart`
Expected: FAIL (the old function returns the frame unchanged, or null for the oversized one).

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** — replace `restorableBounds` and its doc comment in `window_bounds.dart`:

```dart
/// [saved] if the window would still be on one of [displays] (its centre is
/// on a display's visible area, or else its title bar's corner), else null
/// so the host centres the default size: a window saved on a monitor that
/// is gone must not open off screen. A10 D27: a window that is too big for
/// its display (the resolution dropped since) shrinks to the display's
/// visible area and one that hangs over an edge moves inside it, so it is
/// never cut off or unreachable. An empty [displays] (the query failed)
/// keeps [saved].
Rect? restorableBounds(Rect? saved, List<Rect> displays) {
  if (saved == null) return null;
  if (displays.isEmpty) return saved;
  final corner = saved.topLeft + const Offset(24, 24);
  final home =
      displays.where((d) => d.contains(saved.center)).firstOrNull ??
      displays.where((d) => d.contains(corner)).firstOrNull;
  if (home == null) return null;
  final width = saved.width > home.width ? home.width : saved.width;
  final height = saved.height > home.height ? home.height : saved.height;
  final left = saved.left.clamp(home.left, home.right - width);
  final top = saved.top.clamp(home.top, home.bottom - height);
  return Rect.fromLTWH(left, top, width, height);
}
```

### Task 13: Mobile — a literal composer and typing that reaches the terminal exactly once (D30) — **S · ⚠ MANUAL**

The voice composer's `TextField` had no `autocorrect`, `smartDashesType` or capitalisation settings (its comment even says `--` must stay `--`); the terminal view itself (xterm's `CustomTextEdit`) already turns autocorrect and suggestions off. This task also pins "input arrives exactly once" with `TestTextInput` (soft keyboard, Telex composition, hardware key) and fixes what that test found: a keyboard that repeats its reset state made xterm call `textInput('')`, which sent an **empty** input message.

**Files:**
- Modify: `apps/mobile/lib/features/voice/presentation/widgets/voice_composer.dart`, `apps/mobile/lib/features/terminal/domain/shellx_terminal.dart`
- Test: `apps/mobile/test/features/terminal/terminal_input_once_test.dart` (new), `apps/mobile/test/features/terminal/terminal_page_test.dart` (modify)

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**


File: `apps/mobile/test/features/terminal/terminal_input_once_test.dart`

```dart
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:shellx_mobile/features/terminal/domain/shellx_terminal.dart';
import 'package:shellx_mobile/features/terminal/presentation/controllers/extra_keys_controller.dart';
import 'package:xterm/xterm.dart';

/// A10 D30 (gap analysis §2, cmux #15432): whatever the soft keyboard or an
/// IME sends reaches the terminal exactly once.
void main() {
  late ExtraKeysController keys;
  late ShellxTerminal terminal;
  late FocusNode focus;
  late List<String> out;

  setUp(() {
    keys = ExtraKeysController();
    terminal = ShellxTerminal(modifiers: keys);
    out = [];
    terminal.onOutput = out.add;
    focus = FocusNode();
  });

  tearDown(() => keys.dispose());

  Future<void> pumpView(WidgetTester tester) async {
    await tester.pumpWidget(
      MaterialApp(
        home: Scaffold(
          body: TerminalView(terminal, focusNode: focus, autofocus: true, deleteDetection: true),
        ),
      ),
    );
    addTearDown(() async {
      await tester.pumpWidget(const SizedBox());
      focus.dispose();
    });
    focus.requestFocus();
    await tester.pump();
    await tester.pump();
  }

  /// What the platform's keyboard says after the user typed: the two guard spaces the view
  /// plants, then [typed], [composing] marking the part still being composed.
  TextEditingValue keyboard(String typed, {TextRange composing = TextRange.empty}) => TextEditingValue(
    text: '  $typed',
    selection: TextSelection.collapsed(offset: 2 + typed.length),
    composing: composing,
  );

  testWidgets('a plain letter from the soft keyboard is sent once', (tester) async {
    await pumpView(tester);
    tester.testTextInput.updateEditingValue(keyboard('a'));
    await tester.pump();
    expect(out, ['a']);
  });

  testWidgets('a Telex word is held while it is composed and sent once when it is committed', (tester) async {
    await pumpView(tester);
    for (final stage in ['v', 'vi', 'viê']) {
      tester.testTextInput.updateEditingValue(keyboard(stage, composing: TextRange(start: 2, end: 2 + stage.length)));
      await tester.pump();
    }
    expect(out, isEmpty, reason: 'nothing leaves the phone while the word is still being composed');
    tester.testTextInput.updateEditingValue(keyboard('viê'));
    await tester.pump();
    expect(out, ['viê']);
  });

  testWidgets('a committed word is not sent again when the keyboard repeats the state', (tester) async {
    await pumpView(tester);
    tester.testTextInput.updateEditingValue(keyboard('xin'));
    await tester.pump();
    // After a commit the view resets the field to its two guard spaces; a keyboard that repeats
    // the reset state (no text after them) must send nothing.
    tester.testTextInput.updateEditingValue(keyboard(''));
    await tester.pump();
    expect(out, ['xin']);
  });

  testWidgets('a physical key that also arrives as text is sent once', (tester) async {
    await pumpView(tester);
    // Hardware keyboards deliver the character through the text channel; the key event for a
    // plain letter must not send it a second time.
    await tester.sendKeyDownEvent(LogicalKeyboardKey.keyA, character: 'a');
    tester.testTextInput.updateEditingValue(keyboard('a'));
    await tester.sendKeyUpEvent(LogicalKeyboardKey.keyA);
    await tester.pump();
    expect(out.join(), 'a');
  });

  testWidgets('Enter from a physical keyboard is one carriage return', (tester) async {
    await pumpView(tester);
    await tester.sendKeyDownEvent(LogicalKeyboardKey.enter);
    await tester.sendKeyUpEvent(LogicalKeyboardKey.enter);
    await tester.pump();
    expect(out, ['\r']);
  });

  testWidgets('Backspace is one DEL', (tester) async {
    await pumpView(tester);
    tester.testTextInput.updateEditingValue(
      const TextEditingValue(text: ' ', selection: TextSelection.collapsed(offset: 1)),
    );
    await tester.pump();
    expect(out, ['\x7f']);
  });
}
```

### Task 14: Mobile — "View as text" sheet (D28) — **S · ⚠ MANUAL**

Selecting text on a phone is painful. A new button next to "Aa" opens a full-height sheet with a **static snapshot** of the active tab's recent text (≤ 2 000 lines / 200 000 characters, wrapped rows rejoined, trailing blanks removed; only the screen while a full-screen program is on the alternate buffer), selectable with the platform's handles, with "Copy all" and "Refresh". The sheet overlays; the terminal and the computer's size are untouched.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/mobile/lib/features/terminal/domain/buffer_text.dart`, `apps/mobile/lib/features/terminal/presentation/widgets/view_as_text_sheet.dart`
- Modify: `apps/mobile/lib/features/terminal/presentation/pages/terminal_page.dart`, `apps/mobile/lib/l10n/intl_{en,vi}.arb` (+ generated `lib/shared/src/l10n.dart`, `lib/shared/src/intl/messages_{en,vi}.dart`)
- Test: `apps/mobile/test/features/terminal/buffer_text_test.dart` (new), `apps/mobile/test/features/terminal/terminal_page_test.dart` (modify)

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `({String text, bool truncated}) terminalText(Terminal, {maxLines = 2000, maxChars = 200000})`, `kViewAsTextMaxLines`, `kViewAsTextMaxChars`; `Future<void> showViewAsTextSheet(BuildContext, Terminal)`; keys `view-as-text` (the button), `view-as-text-body`, `view-as-text-copy`, `view-as-text-refresh`, `view-as-text-empty`, `view-as-text-truncated`.

```dart
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:shellx_mobile/features/terminal/domain/buffer_text.dart';
import 'package:xterm/core.dart';

Terminal term(String text, {int cols = 20, int rows = 5, int maxLines = 10000}) => Terminal(maxLines: maxLines)
  ..resize(cols, rows)
  ..write(text);

void main() {
  test('screen and scrollback, trailing blanks and empty lines removed', () {
    final t = term('one   \r\ntwo\r\n\r\nthree\r\n\r\n\r\n', rows: 3);
    expect(terminalText(t).text, 'one\ntwo\n\nthree');
    expect(terminalText(t).truncated, isFalse);
  });

  test('rows the terminal wrapped come back as one line', () {
    final t = term('0123456789abcdefghijWRAPPED TEXT here\r\nnext', cols: 20);
    expect(terminalText(t).text, '0123456789abcdefghijWRAPPED TEXT here\nnext');
  });

  test('a space at the end of a wrapped row is kept (it is inside the line)', () {
    final t = term('aaaaaaaaa bbbbbbbbbb ccc', cols: 10);
    expect(terminalText(t).text, 'aaaaaaaaa bbbbbbbbbb ccc');
  });

  test('the last 2000 lines only, and it says so', () {
    final t = term('', rows: 5, maxLines: 5000);
    for (var i = 0; i < 2500; i++) {
      t.write('line $i\r\n');
    }
    final r = terminalText(t);
    expect(r.truncated, isTrue);
    final lines = r.text.split('\n');
    expect(lines, hasLength(2000));
    expect(lines.last, 'line 2499');
    expect(lines.first, 'line 500');
  });

  test('over 200 000 characters the start is cut at a line', () {
    final t = term('', cols: 200, rows: 5, maxLines: 5000);
    for (var i = 0; i < 1500; i++) {
      t.write('${'x' * 150}$i\r\n');
    }
    final r = terminalText(t);
    expect(r.text.length, lessThanOrEqualTo(kViewAsTextMaxChars));
    expect(r.truncated, isTrue);
    expect(r.text.startsWith('x'), isTrue);
    expect(r.text.split('\n').first.length, greaterThan(150));
  });

  test('only the screen while a full-screen program uses the alternate buffer', () {
    final t = term('history 1\r\nhistory 2\r\n', rows: 3);
    t.write('\x1b[?1049hvim screen');
    expect(terminalText(t).text, 'vim screen');
    t.write('\x1b[?1049l');
    expect(terminalText(t).text, contains('history 1'));
  });

  test('Vietnamese, CJK and emoji are kept', () {
    final t = term('Xin chào 日本語 😀');
    expect(terminalText(t).text, 'Xin chào 日本語 😀');
  });

  test('an empty terminal is empty', () {
    expect(terminalText(Terminal()).text, '');
  });
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**


File: `apps/mobile/lib/features/terminal/domain/buffer_text.dart`

```dart
import 'package:xterm/core.dart';

/// The most lines "View as text" shows (A10 D28).
const int kViewAsTextMaxLines = 2000;

/// The most characters it shows; the start is cut first.
const int kViewAsTextMaxChars = 200000;

/// The terminal's text as plain lines (A10 D28): scrollback and screen
/// (just the screen while a full-screen program runs on the alternate
/// screen), the last [maxLines] lines, rows the terminal wrapped joined
/// into one line, trailing blanks of every line and trailing empty lines
/// removed, at most [maxChars] characters (the start is cut, at a line).
/// [truncated] says something was cut.
({String text, bool truncated}) terminalText(
  Terminal terminal, {
  int maxLines = kViewAsTextMaxLines,
  int maxChars = kViewAsTextMaxChars,
}) {
  final buffer = terminal.buffer;
  final logical = <String>[];
  var current = StringBuffer();
  var open = false;
  for (var row = 0; row < buffer.height; row++) {
    final line = buffer.lines[row];
    if (line.isWrapped && open) {
      // The previous row wrapped into this one: no trimming between them.
    } else {
      if (open) logical.add(_trimEnd(current.toString()));
      current = StringBuffer();
      open = true;
    }
    final nextWraps = row + 1 < buffer.height && buffer.lines[row + 1].isWrapped;
    current.write(nextWraps ? line.getText() : line.getText(0, line.getTrimmedLength()));
  }
  if (open) logical.add(_trimEnd(current.toString()));
  while (logical.isNotEmpty && logical.last.isEmpty) {
    logical.removeLast();
  }
  var truncated = false;
  var lines = logical;
  if (lines.length > maxLines) {
    lines = lines.sublist(lines.length - maxLines);
    truncated = true;
  }
  var text = lines.join('\n');
  if (text.length > maxChars) {
    final cut = text.indexOf('\n', text.length - maxChars);
    text = cut < 0 ? text.substring(text.length - maxChars) : text.substring(cut + 1);
    truncated = true;
  }
  return (text: text, truncated: truncated);
}

String _trimEnd(String s) {
  var end = s.length;
  while (end > 0 && (s.codeUnitAt(end - 1) == 0x20 || s.codeUnitAt(end - 1) == 0)) {
    end--;
  }
  return s.substring(0, end);
}
```

File: `apps/mobile/lib/features/terminal/presentation/widgets/view_as_text_sheet.dart`

```dart
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import 'package:shellx_mobile/core/core.dart';
import 'package:shellx_mobile/features/terminal/domain/buffer_text.dart';
import 'package:shellx_mobile/shared/shared.dart';
import 'package:xterm/core.dart';

/// "View as text" (A10 D28): the terminal's recent text as a snapshot the user can select and copy
/// with the platform's own handles; one tap copies all of it. Static: a refresh button takes
/// another snapshot. Nothing here is logged.
Future<void> showViewAsTextSheet(BuildContext context, Terminal terminal) => showModalBottomSheet<void>(
  context: context,
  isScrollControlled: true,
  useSafeArea: true,
  showDragHandle: true,
  backgroundColor: context.colors.surface,
  builder: (context) => ViewAsTextSheet(terminal: terminal),
);

class ViewAsTextSheet extends StatefulWidget {
  const ViewAsTextSheet({super.key, required this.terminal});

  final Terminal terminal;

  @override
  State<ViewAsTextSheet> createState() => _ViewAsTextSheetState();
}

class _ViewAsTextSheetState extends State<ViewAsTextSheet> {
  late ({String text, bool truncated}) _snapshot = terminalText(widget.terminal);
  final _scroll = ScrollController();

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _toEnd();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _scroll.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  /// The newest output is at the end: that is where the user wants to start.
  void _toEnd() => WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
    if (_scroll.hasClients) _scroll.jumpTo(_scroll.position.maxScrollExtent);
  });

  void _refresh() {
    setState(() => _snapshot = terminalText(widget.terminal));
    _toEnd();
  }

  Future<void> _copyAll() async {
    final done = S.of(context).copied;
    try {
      await Clipboard.setData(ClipboardData(text: _snapshot.text));
    } catch (error) {
      if (kDebugMode) debugPrint('Clipboard error: ${error.runtimeType}');
      if (mounted) getIt<PopupHandler>().showError(title: ErrorText(S.of(context)).describe(error));
      return;
    }
    if (mounted) getIt<PopupHandler>().showSuccess(title: done);
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final strings = S.of(context);
    final empty = _snapshot.text.isEmpty;
    return FractionallySizedBox(
      heightFactor: 0.92,
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 0, 16, 12),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
          children: [
            Row(
              children: [
                Expanded(child: Text(strings.viewAsTextTitle, style: context.typo.subHeader.bold)),
                IconButton(
                  key: const ValueKey('view-as-text-refresh'),
                  tooltip: strings.viewAsTextRefresh,
                  icon: const Icon(Icons.refresh),
                  onPressed: _refresh,
                ),
                TextButton.icon(
                  key: const ValueKey('view-as-text-copy'),
                  onPressed: empty ? null : _copyAll,
                  icon: const Icon(Icons.copy, size: 18),
                  label: Text(strings.viewAsTextCopyAll),
                ),
              ],
            ),
            if (_snapshot.truncated)
              Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 4),
                child: Text(
                  strings.viewAsTextTruncated(kViewAsTextMaxLines),
                  key: const ValueKey('view-as-text-truncated'),
                  style: context.typo.metadata11.copyWith(color: context.colors.secondaryText),
                ),
              ),
            Expanded(
              child: empty
                  ? Center(
                      child: Text(
                        strings.viewAsTextEmpty,
                        key: const ValueKey('view-as-text-empty'),
                        style: context.typo.paragraph.copyWith(color: context.colors.secondaryText),
                      ),
                    )
                  : Scrollbar(
                      controller: _scroll,
                      child: SingleChildScrollView(
                        controller: _scroll,
                        // Long lines scroll sideways instead of wrapping: a command stays one line.
                        child: SingleChildScrollView(
                          scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
                          child: SelectableText(
                            _snapshot.text,
                            key: const ValueKey('view-as-text-body'),
                            style: TextStyle(
                              fontFamily: FontFamily.jetBrainsMono,
                              fontSize: 13,
                              height: 1.35,
                              color: context.colors.primaryText,
                            ),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

```dart
  void _showViewAsText() {

    unawaited(showViewAsTextSheet(context, tab.terminal.peek()));
```

### Task 15: Mobile — tap a web address to open or copy it (D12) — **S · ⚠ MANUAL**

A tap on a URL offers a bottom sheet (Open / Copy link) — never opens straight away, since a stray tap in a full-screen program is common. Only http/https, found with Task 1's detector on the row-joined line around the tapped cell.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/mobile/lib/features/terminal/domain/terminal_links.dart`, `apps/mobile/lib/features/terminal/presentation/widgets/link_sheet.dart`
- Modify: `apps/mobile/lib/features/terminal/presentation/pages/terminal_page.dart`, `apps/mobile/lib/l10n/intl_{en,vi}.arb` (+ generated)
- Test: `apps/mobile/test/features/terminal/terminal_links_test.dart` (new), `apps/mobile/test/features/terminal/terminal_page_test.dart` (modify)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 1 (`linkAt`, `webUriOf`), `ExternalLinks.openOrToast`, `PopupHandler.showSuccess`.
- Produces: `String? urlAt(Terminal, CellOffset, {maxRows = 8})`, `Future<void> showLinkSheet(BuildContext, String url)` (keys `link-sheet-url`, `link-sheet-open`, `link-sheet-copy`); `_TapListener.onTap` now takes the tap's local position.
