```dart
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:shellx_mobile/features/terminal/domain/terminal_links.dart';
import 'package:xterm/core.dart';

Terminal term(String text, {int cols = 40}) => Terminal(maxLines: 100)
  ..resize(cols, 6)
  ..write(text);

void main() {
  test('the address under a cell; the full stop after it is not part', () {
    final t = term('see https://example.com/a?b=1.');
    expect(urlAt(t, const CellOffset(10, 0)), 'https://example.com/a?b=1');
    expect(urlAt(t, const CellOffset(3, 0)), isNull);
    expect(urlAt(t, const CellOffset(29, 0)), isNull);
  });

  test('an address that wraps over rows is one address on every row', () {
    final t = term('https://example.com/a/long/path/that/wraps/over', cols: 20);
    const url = 'https://example.com/a/long/path/that/wraps/over';
    expect(urlAt(t, const CellOffset(2, 0)), url);
    expect(urlAt(t, const CellOffset(2, 2)), url);
  });

  test('wide characters before the address do not shift the answer', () {
    final t = term('日本語 https://example.com/x');
    expect(urlAt(t, const CellOffset(12, 0)), 'https://example.com/x');
    expect(urlAt(t, const CellOffset(1, 0)), isNull);
  });

  test('only http and https; no paths', () {
    final t = term('file:///etc/passwd /usr/bin ftp://x.y javascript:alert(1)');
    for (var x = 0; x < 40; x += 3) {
      expect(urlAt(t, CellOffset(x, 0)), isNull);
    }
  });

  test('cells outside the buffer have none', () {
    final t = term('https://a.b');
    expect(urlAt(t, const CellOffset(0, 99)), isNull);
    expect(urlAt(t, const CellOffset(0, -1)), isNull);
    expect(urlAt(t, const CellOffset(30, 0)), isNull);
  });
}
```

File: `apps/mobile/lib/features/terminal/domain/terminal_links.dart`

```dart
import 'package:shellx_core/shellx_core.dart';
import 'package:xterm/core.dart';

/// The web address drawn in cell [cell] of [terminal] (A10 D12), or null. Rows the terminal wrapped
/// are one line, so an address that wraps is one address. At most 8 rows around the cell are read.
/// Mirrors the desktop's `logicalLineAt` (the two apps share no package that has xterm).
String? urlAt(Terminal terminal, CellOffset cell, {int maxRows = 8}) {
  final buffer = terminal.buffer;
  if (cell.y < 0 || cell.y >= buffer.height) return null;
  var first = cell.y;
  while (first > 0 && buffer.lines[first].isWrapped && cell.y - first < maxRows - 1) {
    first--;
  }
  var last = cell.y;
  while (last + 1 < buffer.height && buffer.lines[last + 1].isWrapped && last - first + 1 < maxRows) {
    last++;
  }
  final text = StringBuffer();
  var index = -1;
  for (var row = first; row <= last; row++) {
    final line = buffer.lines[row];
    final cells = row < last ? line.length : line.getTrimmedLength();
    for (var col = 0; col < cells; col++) {
      final width = line.getWidth(col);
      if (width == 0) continue;
      final code = line.getCodePoint(col);
      final unit = code == 0 ? ' ' : String.fromCharCode(code);
      if (row == cell.y && col <= cell.x && cell.x < col + width) index = text.length;
      text.write(unit);
    }
  }
  if (index < 0) return null;
  return linkAt(text.toString(), index)?.target;
}
```

```dart
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import 'package:shellx_core/shellx_core.dart' show webUriOf;
import 'package:shellx_mobile/core/core.dart';
import 'package:shellx_mobile/shared/shared.dart';

/// A tap on a web address in the terminal (A10 D12): never opened straight away — a stray tap in a
/// full-screen program is common — but offered: Open or Copy link. Only http and https get here.
Future<void> showLinkSheet(BuildContext context, String url) => showModalBottomSheet<void>(
  context: context,
  showDragHandle: true,
  backgroundColor: context.colors.surface,
  builder: (context) => _LinkSheet(url: url),
);

class _LinkSheet extends StatelessWidget {
  const _LinkSheet({required this.url});

  final String url;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final strings = S.of(context);
    return SafeArea(
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(20, 0, 20, 16),
        child: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
          children: [
            Text(strings.linkSheetTitle, style: context.typo.subHeader.bold),
            const SizedBox(height: 8),
            SelectableText(
              url,
              key: const ValueKey('link-sheet-url'),
              maxLines: 6,
              style: TextStyle(fontFamily: FontFamily.jetBrainsMono, fontSize: 13, color: context.colors.primaryText),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            Row(
              children: [
                Expanded(
                  child: OutlinedButton(
                    key: const ValueKey('link-sheet-copy'),
                    onPressed: () async {
                      final done = strings.copied;
                      Navigator.of(context).pop();
                      try {
                        await Clipboard.setData(ClipboardData(text: url));
                        getIt<PopupHandler>().showSuccess(title: done);
                      } catch (_) {
                        // Nothing to say about a clipboard that cannot be written; never log the link.
                      }
                    },
                    child: Text(strings.linkSheetCopy),
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(width: 12),
                Expanded(
                  child: FilledButton(
                    key: const ValueKey('link-sheet-open'),
                    onPressed: () {
                      Navigator.of(context).pop();
                      final uri = webUriOf(url);
                      if (uri != null) getIt<ExternalLinks>().openOrToast(uri);
                    },
                    child: Text(strings.linkSheetOpen),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

### Task 16: Mobile — budgeted output, foreground first, echo never held back (D29, D22) — **M · ⚠ MANUAL**

Every `TabOutput` was written to xterm at once, for every tab. Now each tab has a `TerminalOutputFeed`: the tab in front writes the first piece at once, then at most 128 KiB per 16 ms; a tab in the back writes at most 64 KiB every 250 ms (cap 2 MiB, newest 1 MiB kept, cut at a line); the echo of a key the user just typed (≤ 4 KiB, within 250 ms, nothing waiting) is written at once; an exit banner is preceded by a full drain; a `session.reset` forgets what waits. The resize rules of A1/the existing mobile tests are unchanged; D22 adds one burst test.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/mobile/lib/features/terminal/domain/terminal_output_feed.dart`
- Modify: `apps/mobile/lib/features/terminal/presentation/controllers/terminal_tab_controller.dart`, `apps/mobile/lib/features/terminal/presentation/controllers/terminal_workspace.dart`
- Test: `apps/mobile/test/features/terminal/terminal_output_feed_test.dart` (new), `apps/mobile/test/features/terminal/terminal_tab_controller_test.dart` (modify)

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `TerminalOutputFeed(void Function(String) write, {activeBudget, activeTick, idleBudget, idleTick, cap, keep, echoWindow, echoMax, now})` with `add`, `drain`, `clear`, `dispose`, `noteInput`, `isActive` (setter), `pendingLength`, `dropped`; `TerminalTabController.setActive(bool)`.

```dart
import 'package:shellx_mobile/features/terminal/domain/terminal_output_feed.dart';

void main() {
  test('the front tab writes the first piece at once, then 128 KiB per 16 ms', () {
    fakeAsync((async) {
      final writes = <String>[];
      final feed = TerminalOutputFeed(writes.add);
      feed.add('x' * 300000);
      expect(writes.map((w) => w.length), [128 * 1024]);
      async.elapse(const Duration(milliseconds: 16));
      expect(writes.map((w) => w.length), [128 * 1024, 128 * 1024]);
      async.elapse(const Duration(milliseconds: 16));
      expect(writes.join().length, 300000);
      expect(feed.pendingLength, 0);
      async.elapse(const Duration(seconds: 1));
      expect(writes, hasLength(3));
    });
  });

  test('a back tab waits 250 ms and then writes at most 64 KiB at a time', () {
    fakeAsync((async) {
      final writes = <String>[];
      final feed = TerminalOutputFeed(writes.add)..isActive = false;
      feed.add('y' * 200000);
      expect(writes, isEmpty);
      async.elapse(const Duration(milliseconds: 249));
      expect(writes, isEmpty);
      async.elapse(const Duration(milliseconds: 1));
      expect(writes.map((w) => w.length), [64 * 1024]);
      async.elapse(const Duration(milliseconds: 250));
      async.elapse(const Duration(milliseconds: 250));
      async.elapse(const Duration(milliseconds: 250));
      expect(writes.join().length, 200000);
    });
  });

  test('coming to the front writes what waits at once', () {
    fakeAsync((async) {
      final writes = <String>[];
      final feed = TerminalOutputFeed(writes.add)..isActive = false;
      feed.add('z' * 1000);
      feed.isActive = true;
      expect(writes, ['z' * 1000]);
      async.elapse(const Duration(seconds: 1));
      expect(writes, hasLength(1));
    });
  });

  test('going to the back stops the fast ticks', () {
    fakeAsync((async) {
      final writes = <String>[];
      final feed = TerminalOutputFeed(writes.add);
      feed.add('a' * 300000); // one piece out, the rest waits for the tick
      feed.isActive = false;
      async.elapse(const Duration(milliseconds: 100));
      expect(writes, hasLength(1));
      async.elapse(const Duration(milliseconds: 200));
      expect(writes.length, greaterThan(1));
    });
  });

  test('the echo of what was just typed is written at once, even behind a burst elsewhere', () {
    fakeAsync((async) {
      var now = DateTime(2026, 10, 7, 9);
      final writes = <String>[];
      final feed = TerminalOutputFeed(writes.add, now: () => now);
      feed.noteInput();
      feed.add('a'); // the echo
      expect(writes, ['a']);
      now = now.add(const Duration(milliseconds: 300));
      feed.add('late'); // after the window it is ordinary output (written at once: nothing waits)
      expect(writes, ['a', 'late']);
    });
  });

  test('an echo does not jump the queue: pending output goes first', () {
    fakeAsync((async) {
      final writes = <String>[];
      final feed = TerminalOutputFeed(writes.add);
      feed.add('b' * 300000);
      feed.noteInput();
      feed.add('!'); // output is waiting: stays in order
      expect(writes.length, 1);
      async.elapse(const Duration(milliseconds: 40));
      expect(writes.join().endsWith('!'), isTrue);
      expect(writes.join().length, 300001);
    });

  test('a big echo (a paste coming back) is paced like any output', () {
    fakeAsync((async) {
      final writes = <String>[];
      final feed = TerminalOutputFeed(writes.add);
      feed.noteInput();
      feed.add('c' * 10000);
      expect(writes.single.length, 10000, reason: 'nothing waited, so the first piece goes at once');
      feed.add('d' * 300000);
      async.elapse(const Duration(seconds: 1));
      expect(writes.join().length, 310000);
    });
  });

  test('over 2 MiB waiting: only the newest 1 MiB is kept, from a line start', () {
    fakeAsync((async) {
      final writes = <String>[];
      final feed = TerminalOutputFeed(writes.add)..isActive = false;
      for (var i = 0; i < 40; i++) {
        feed.add('${'e' * 99}\n' * 1000); // 100 000 characters
      }
      expect(feed.dropped, greaterThan(0));
      expect(feed.pendingLength, lessThanOrEqualTo(2 * 1024 * 1024));
      feed.isActive = true;

      expect(all.length, lessThanOrEqualTo(2 * 1024 * 1024));
      expect(all.startsWith('e' * 99 + '\n'), isTrue, reason: 'never cut mid-line');
    });

  test('a surrogate pair is never split between two writes', () {
    fakeAsync((async) {
      final writes = <String>[];
      final feed = TerminalOutputFeed(writes.add, activeBudget: 10);
      feed.add('${'a' * 9}😀${'b' * 20}');
      async.elapse(const Duration(seconds: 1));
      for (final w in writes) {
        final u = w.codeUnitAt(w.length - 1);
        expect(u >= 0xD800 && u <= 0xDBFF, isFalse);
      }
      expect(writes.join(), '${'a' * 9}😀${'b' * 20}');
    });
  });

  test('drain writes everything now; clear forgets it; dispose stops', () {
    fakeAsync((async) {
      final writes = <String>[];
      final feed = TerminalOutputFeed(writes.add);
      feed.add('f' * 300000);
      feed.drain();
      expect(writes.join().length, 300000);
      feed.add('g' * 300000);
      feed.clear();
      async.elapse(const Duration(seconds: 1));
      expect(writes.join().length, 300000 + 128 * 1024);
      feed.dispose();
      feed.add('h');
      async.elapse(const Duration(seconds: 1));
      expect(writes.join().contains('h'), isFalse);
    });
  });

  test('a burst of 1 MiB in one tab leaves the frame budget: no write is bigger than the budget', () {
    fakeAsync((async) {
      final sizes = <int>[];
      final feed = TerminalOutputFeed((t) => sizes.add(t.length));
      feed.add('k' * (1024 * 1024));
      async.elapse(const Duration(seconds: 1));
      expect(sizes.reduce((a, b) => a > b ? a : b), lessThanOrEqualTo(128 * 1024));
      expect(sizes.fold<int>(0, (a, b) => a + b), 1024 * 1024);
    });
  });
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the feed**


File: `apps/mobile/lib/features/terminal/domain/terminal_output_feed.dart`

```dart
import 'dart:async';

/// Output of one tab on its way to xterm (A10 D29): the foreground tab is written at once and then
/// at most [activeBudget] characters per [activeTick]; a tab in the background is written every
/// [idleTick], at most [idleBudget] characters at a time, so a burst in one terminal costs the
/// others (and the keyboard) little. The echo of what the user just typed is never held back.
///
/// When more than [cap] characters wait, only the newest [keep] are kept (cut at a line start), as
/// the agent's ring buffer drops the oldest bytes. A copy of the desktop's `TerminalFeed` for text
/// that is already decoded, plus the two priorities; the apps share no package with xterm.
final class TerminalOutputFeed {
  TerminalOutputFeed(
    this._write, {
    this.activeBudget = 128 * 1024,
    this.activeTick = const Duration(milliseconds: 16),
    this.idleBudget = 64 * 1024,
    this.idleTick = const Duration(milliseconds: 250),
    this.cap = 2 * 1024 * 1024,
    this.keep = 1024 * 1024,
    this.echoWindow = const Duration(milliseconds: 250),
    this.echoMax = 4096,
    DateTime Function()? now,
  }) : _now = now ?? DateTime.now;

  final void Function(String text) _write;
  final int activeBudget;
  final Duration activeTick;
  final int idleBudget;
  final Duration idleTick;
  final int cap;
  final int keep;
  final Duration echoWindow;
  final int echoMax;
  final DateTime Function() _now;

  final _pending = StringBuffer();
  Timer? _timer;
  bool _active = true;
  bool _disposed = false;
  DateTime? _typedAt;

  /// How many times older pending output was dropped.
  int dropped = 0;

  /// Characters waiting.
  int get pendingLength => _pending.length;

  bool get isActive => _active;

  /// The tab came to the front (true) or went to the back (false).
  set isActive(bool value) {
    if (_active == value) return;
    _active = value;
    if (_disposed) return;
    _timer?.cancel();
    _timer = null;
    if (_pending.isEmpty) return;
    // To the front: what waits goes at once. To the back: the slow tick takes over.
    if (_active) {
      _flush();
    } else {
      _timer = Timer(idleTick, _flush);
    }
  }

  /// The user typed in this tab: output that follows at once is its echo.
  void noteInput() => _typedAt = _now();

  void add(String text) {
    if (_disposed || text.isEmpty) return;
    final typed = _typedAt;
    final echo =
        _active &&
        _pending.isEmpty &&
        text.length <= echoMax &&
        typed != null &&
        _now().difference(typed) <= echoWindow;
    if (echo) {
      _write(text);
      return;
    }
    _pending.write(text);
    _trim();
    if (_timer != null) return;
    // The foreground tab writes the first piece at once; one in the background waits a tick.
    if (_active) {
      _flush();
    } else {
      _timer = Timer(idleTick, _flush);
    }
  }

  /// Writes everything waiting now (before an exit banner).
  void drain() {
    _timer?.cancel();
    _timer = null;
    if (_pending.isEmpty) return;
    final text = _pending.toString();
    _pending.clear();
    _write(text);
  }

  /// A screen reset: what waits belongs to the old screen.
  void clear() {
    _timer?.cancel();
    _timer = null;
    _pending.clear();
  }

  void dispose() {
    _disposed = true;
    _timer?.cancel();
    _timer = null;
    _pending.clear();
  }

  void _flush() {
    _timer = null;
    if (_disposed || _pending.isEmpty) return;
    final budget = _active ? activeBudget : idleBudget;
    var text = _pending.toString();
    _pending.clear();
    if (text.length > budget) {
      var cut = budget;
      // Never split a surrogate pair between two writes.
      final unit = text.codeUnitAt(cut - 1);
      if (unit >= 0xD800 && unit <= 0xDBFF) cut--;
      _pending.write(text.substring(cut));
      text = text.substring(0, cut);
    }
    _write(text);
    if (_pending.isNotEmpty) _timer = Timer(_active ? activeTick : idleTick, _flush);
  }

  void _trim() {
    if (_pending.length <= cap) return;
    final text = _pending.toString();
    final start = _cutPoint(text, text.length - keep);
    _pending
      ..clear()
      ..write(text.substring(start));
    dropped++;
  }

  static int _cutPoint(String text, int from) {
    final newline = text.indexOf('\n', from);
    if (newline != -1 && newline < text.length - 1) return newline + 1;
    final escape = text.indexOf('\x1b', from);
    if (escape != -1) return escape;
    final carriageReturn = text.indexOf('\r', from);
    if (carriageReturn != -1) return carriageReturn;
    final unit = text.codeUnitAt(from);
    return unit >= 0xDC00 && unit <= 0xDFFF ? from + 1 : from;
  }
}
```

```dart
  /// A10 D29: output reaches xterm in budgeted pieces, the foreground tab first. Writes to whichever
  /// terminal is current (a `session.reset` swaps it).
  late final TerminalOutputFeed _feed = TerminalOutputFeed((text) {
    if (!_disposed) _terminal.peek().write(text);
  });

```

**Placeholder scan:** no "TBD"/"similar to Task N"; every code block is the code that was run. Two places say "`<the files of this task>`" inside a `dart format` command: it means the files in that task's commit list, which are spelled out in the commit step. Task 3 Step 6 and Task 3's `integration_test` were analysed but not run (no display in the authoring environment): stated as ⚠, not as done.

**Type consistency:** `PasteSink`/`PasteSender`/`PanePasteSink` (Task 3) are what `PaneInput` (Task 9) uses; `LinkHit`/`findLinkAt`/`LinkResolver` (Task 6) are what `LinkLayer` and the pane menu (Task 7) use; `terminalTabsProvider` notices `PasteTooLargeNotice` (Task 3), `LinkOpenFailedNotice` (Task 7), `InputNotice` (Task 9) each add one arm to the toast `switch` in the task that introduces them; `PaneTerminal.viewKey` (Task 5) and `onMenu(Offset, CellOffset)` (Task 7) are changed once each; `TerminalTabs.flushResizes` (Task 2) is only used by tests; `RecordingUrlOpener` gains both new methods in Task 7; `pumpConfiguredWorkspace(clipboard:)` (Task 9) keeps `paste_flow_test` (Task 3) green; mobile `TerminalTabController.setActive` (Task 16) is called only by `TerminalWorkspace`.

**Fixes applied during review:** the original design put the link click on `TerminalView.onTapUp` (dead in xterm 4.0.0) — replaced by a `Listener` in both apps; the first mobile link wiring used one shared `GlobalKey` (86 failures) — now `GlobalObjectKey(tab)`; the desktop view key map first missed the A8 reconnect move (2 failures) — fixed in Task 5; item 9 (notifications) was planned as a remap but A8 M5 drops the old pane's unread on purpose — reduced to a pinning test; `desktop_drop` cannot report a file count while hovering — the plural hint was dropped; a `late final` settler read in `_dispose` made Riverpod assert — created in `build()`.
